[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/6:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/6:3:1)


**הַנַּעֲשׂוֹת בִּכְלִי.** כְּגוֹן מִנְחַת מַרְחֶשֶׁת וּמַחֲבַת שֶׁהֵן נַעֲשׂוֹת בִּכְלִי:


הנעשות בכלי – as for example, the meal-offering prepared in a deep pan [in a container full of oil] and a meal-offering prepared by frying on a flat pan which are made in a utensil.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 6:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/6:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/6:3:2)


**טְעוּנוֹת שָׁלֹשׁ מַתְּנוֹת שֶׁמֶן.** לְמַעוֹטֵי מִנְחַת מַאֲפֵה תַּנּוּר שֶׁאֵינָהּ טְעוּנָה יְצִיקָה:


טעונות שלש מתנות שמן – to exclude that meal-offering which is baked in an oven that does not require pouring [of oil].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 6:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/6:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/6:3:3)


**וּמַתַּן שֶׁמֶן בַּכְּלִי קֹדֶם לַעֲשִׂיָּתָן.** תְּחִלָּה נוֹתֵן שֶׁמֶן בִּכְלִי שָׁרֵת וְנוֹתֵן סֹלֶת עָלָיו, דִּכְתִיב בְּמִנְחַת מַרְחֶשֶׁת (ויקרא ב) סֹלֶת בַּשֶּׁמֶן תֵּעָשֶׂה, כְּלוֹמַר תִּנָּתֵן, אַלְמָא דִּטְעוּנָה מַתַּן שֶׁמֶן קוֹדֵם לַעֲשִׂיָּתָהּ, וּבְמִנְחַת מַחֲבַת כְּתִיב בְּלִילָה וִיצִיקָה וְלֹא כְּתִיב בָּהּ מַתַּן שֶׁמֶן בַּכְּלִי תְּחִלָּה, וּבַמַּרְחֶשֶׁת כְּתִיב מַתַּן שֶׁמֶן וְלֹא כְּתִיב בָּהּ יְצִיקָה וּבְלִילָה. וְגָמְרִינַן הַךְ מֵהַךְ, נֶאֱמַר קָרְבָּנְךָ בַּמַרְחֶשֶׁת וְנֶאֱמַר קָרְבָּנְךָ בַּמַּחֲבַת. וְכֵיצַד הוּא עוֹשֶׂה, נוֹתֵן שֶׁמֶן תְּחִלָּה בִּכְלִי שָׁרֵת, וְנוֹתֵן עָלָיו אֶת הַסֹּלֶת, [וְחוֹזֵר] וְנוֹתֵן עָלֶיהָ שֶׁמֶן וּבוֹלְלָן, הֲרֵי מַתַּן שֶׁמֶן בַּכְּלִי וּבְלִילָה, וְלָשָׁהּ בְּמַיִם וְאוֹפָהּ בַּתַּנּוּר וּפוֹתְתָהּ וְיוֹצֵק עָלֶיהָ שֶׁמֶן אַחַר פְּתִיתָה, וְזוֹ הִיא יְצִיקָה, הֲרֵי שְׁלָשְׁתָּן. וְקוֹמֵץ וּמַקְטִיר אֶת הַקֹּמֶץ, וְהַשְּׁאָר נֶאֱכָל לַכֹּהֲנִים:


ומתן שמן בכלי קודם לעשייתן – first he places the oil in a service/sacred vessel and places fine flour on top of it, as it is written regarding the meal-offering prepared in a deep pan (Leviticus 2:7): “[If your offering is a grain offering in a pan,] it shall be made of choice flour in oil,” that is to say, that it shall be placed, so we see that it requires the placing of oil prior to its being made, and regarding the meal-offering prepared by frying on a flat pan, it is written (Leviticus 2:5-6): “[If your offering is a grain offering on a griddle,] it shall be of choice flour with oil mixed in, unleavened. Break it into bits and pour oil on it;” mixing and pouring is written, but putting oil in a utensil first is not written regarding it, but with a meal-offering prepared in a deep pan [in a container full of oil], that oil is placed in it but pouring and mixing/stirring is not written, and we learn this from that, it is stated, קרבנך/your offering (Leviticus 2:7) with the meal-offering prepared in a deep pan [in a container full of oil] and it is stated קרבנך/your offering (Leviticus 2:5) with the meal-offering prepared by frying on a flat pan/griddle , and how does he make it? He places oil first in the sacred vessel and places upon it the choice flour, [and he returns] and puts upon it oil and mixes/stirs it, behold there is the placing of oil in a utensil and mixing/stirring, and he kneads it with water and bakes it in an oven and crumbles it and pours oil upon it after crumbling/breaking it into pieces, and this is the pouring of the oil, behold three of them, and he grabs a handful and offers up the handful in smoke and the rest is consumed by the Kohanim.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 6:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/6:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/6:3:4)


**וְהַחַלּוֹת בּוֹלְלָן דִּבְרֵי רַבִּי.** הַשְׁתָּא מַיְרֵי בְּמִנְחַת מַאֲפֵה תַּנּוּר שֶׁהִיא בָּאָה חַלּוֹת אוֹ רְקִיקִים, וּכְתִיב בָּהּ (שם) סֹלֶת חַלּוֹת מַצֹּת בְּלוּלֹת בַּשֶּׁמֶן. רַבִּי סָבַר חַלּוֹת בְּלוּלוֹת כְּתִיב, שֶׁבּוֹלְלָן כְּשֶׁהֵן חַלּוֹת. וְרַבָּנָן סָבְרֵי סֹלֶת בְּלוּלָה כְּתִיב, מְלַמֵּד שֶׁנִּבְלֶלֶת כְּשֶׁהִיא סֹלֶת. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


והחלות בוללן דברי רבי – now we are speaking of the meal-offerings baked in an oven which produces loaves or wafers, and it is written concerning it (Leviticus 2:4): “[When you present an offering of grain baked in the oven] it shall be of choice flour: unleavened cakes with oil mixed in,[or unleavened wafers spread with oil].” Rabbi [Judah the Prince] holds that loaves mixed with oil is written, and he mixes them when they are loaves. But the Rabbis hold that mixed/stirred choice flour is written, teaching us that it is mixed/stirred when it is choice flour. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 6:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/6:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/6:3:5)


**הַחַלּוֹת טְעוּנוֹת בְּלִילָה וְהָרְקִיקִים מְשׁוּחִים.** דִּכְתִיב חַלּוֹת בְּלוּלוֹת, וְלֹא רְקִיקִים בְּלוּלִים. רְקִיקִים מְשׁוּחִים, וְלֹא חַלּוֹת מְשׁוּחוֹת:


חלות טעונות בלילה והרקיקים משוחים – as it is written (Leviticus 2:4): “unleavened cakes with oil mixed in,” but wafers do not have it (i.e., oil) mixed in. wafters are spread with oil, but unleavened cakes are not spread with oil.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 6:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/6:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/6:3:6)


**כֵּיצַד מוֹשְׁחָן כְּמִין כִי.** יְוָנִית, שֶׁהִיא כְּמִין ט'‏ שֶׁלָּנוּ, כְּהַפְרָשַׁת גּוּדָל [שֶׁל שְׂמֹאל] מִן הָאֶצְבַּע [כָּזֶה c ]:


כיצד מושחן כמין כי – Greek (in the form of an X) – our letter ט' – like the separation of the thumb [of the left hand] from the fingers [like this C].



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 6:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/6:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/6:4:1)


**כָּל הַמְּנָחוֹת הַנַּעֲשׂוֹת בִּכְלִי.** הָכָא לָאו לְמַעוֹטֵי מִנְחַת מַאֲפֵה תַּנּוּר אֲתָא, דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא ב) פָּתוֹת אֹתָהּ פִּתִּים וְגוֹ'‏ מִנְחָה, לְרַבּוֹת כָּל הַמְּנָחוֹת לִפְתִיתָה. אֶלָּא לְמַעוֹטֵי שְׁתֵּי הַלֶּחֶם וְלֶחֶם הַפָּנִים, שֶׁאֵין טְעוּנִים פְּתִיתָה:


כל המנחות הנעשות בכלי – here it does not come to include the meal-offering that is baked in an oven, as it is written (Leviticus 2:6): “Break it into bits and pour oil on it; it is a grain offering,” but to include all the meal offerings for breaking into bits, but rather to exclude the two loaves and the shewbread which do not require breaking into bits/crumbling.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 6:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/6:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/6:4:2)


**כּוֹפֵל אַחַת לִשְׁנַיִם.** דִּכְתִיב פָּתוֹת אֹתָהּ פִּתִּים. פָּתוֹת הַיְנוּ שְׁנַיִם, דְּכֵיוָן דְּנִשְׁבַּר הַיְנוּ שְׁתֵּי חֲתִיכוֹת, פִּתִּים מְרַבִּינַן שֶׁתְּהֵא הַחֲתִיכָה לִשְׁתֵּי חֲתִיכוֹת דְּהַיְנוּ אַרְבַּע. יָכוֹל יַעֲשֶׂנָּה פֵּרוּרִים, תַּלְמוּד לוֹמַר אֹתָהּ פִּתִּים, וְלֹא פְּתִיתֶיהָ לִפְתִיתִים. וְכֻלָּן פְּתִיתִין כַּזֵּיתִים, דְּסֵיפָא דְּמַתְנִיתִין לָאו רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן קָאָמַר לֵיהּ אֶלָּא דִּבְרֵי הַכֹּל הִיא, דִּלְאַחַר שֶׁעָשָׂה פְּתִיתִין כַּזֵּיתִים כּוֹפֵל כָּל זַיִת לִשְׁנַיִם וּשְׁנַיִם לְאַרְבָּעָה:


כופל אחת לשנים – as it is written (Leviticus 2:6): “Break it into bits,” breaking them (i.e., plural) which is two, for since it was broken, that is two pieces, “bits,” we extend the scope that each piece is broken into two, which is four. It is possible that he can make them crumbs, the inference teaches us, “into bits,” but not its bits into bits. And all of them are broken pieces like olives, that the concluding clause of the Mishnah is not Rabbi Shimon who states, but rather the words of all, for after he made the broken pieces like olives, he folds each olive into two and two into four.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 6:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/6:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/6:4:3)


**מְכַפְּלָהּ.** לֹא הָיָה מְכַפְּלָהּ לְאַרְבָּעָה, אֶלָּא לִשְׁנַיִם. דְּלֹא כְּתִיב בָּהּ אֶלָּא מִנְחַת פִּתִּים וְלֹא פָּתוֹת:


מכפלה – he does not fold them up into four, but rather only into two, for it is not written concerning it other than the meal-offering of broken pieces but not [whole] pieces.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 6:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/6:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/6:4:4)


**רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן אוֹמֵר כוּ'.** וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


רבי שמעון אומר כו' – But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 6:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/6:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/6:5:1)


**שִׁיפָה.** שֶׁמְּשַׁפְשֵׁף הַחִטָּה בֵּין יָדוֹ לַכְּלִי כְּדֵי שֶׁתְּהֵא נוֹחָה לְהָשִׁיר קְלִפָּתָהּ:


שיפה – he rubs the wheat between his hand and the utensil in order that it be pleasant to remove its husk.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 6:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/6:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/6:5:2)


**בְּעִיטָה.** שֶׁבּוֹעֵט בָּעֳבִי אֶגְרוֹפוֹ אוֹ בָּעֳבִי פַּס יָדוֹ. וְהָיָה שָׁף אַחַת וּבוֹעֵט שְׁתַּיִם, שָׁף שְׁתַּיִם וּבוֹעֵט שָׁלֹשׁ, וְחוֹזֵר וְעוֹשֶׂה כַּסֵּדֶר הַזֶּה עַד שֶׁגּוֹמֵר חֲמֵשׁ מֵאוֹת בְּעִיטוֹת לִשְׁלֹשׁ מֵאוֹת שִׁיפוֹת:


בעיטה – that he strikes with the thickness of his fist or the thickness of the palm of his hand. And he would rub once and beat twice, [or] rub twice and strike three times, and he goes back and does it according to this order until he completes five hundred beatings to three hundred rubbings.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 6:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/6:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/6:5:3)


**הָכִי גָּרְסִינַן רַבִּי יוֹסֵי אוֹמֵר בַּבָּצֵק.** כְּלוֹמַר, שִׁיפָה וּבְעִיטָה בַּבָּצֵק וְלֹא בַּחִטִּין. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


ה"ג ר' יוסי אומר בבצק – that is to say, rubbing and eating he dough but not the wheat. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yosi.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 6:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/6:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/6:5:4)


**עֶשֶׂר עֶשֶׂר חַלּוֹת.** וּמִנְחַת סֹלֶת נַמִּי אַף עַל גַּב דְּקֹדֶם אֲפִיָּה נִקְמֶצֶת, אֲפִלּוּ הָכִי עֶשֶׂר חַלּוֹת הִיא בָּאָה:


עשר עשר חלות – and the meal-offering of choice flour also even though that prior to baking a handful is taken, nevertheless, ten loaves are brought.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 6:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/6:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/6:5:5)


**לֶחֶם הַפָּנִים.** בְּהֶדְיָא כְּתִיב בֵּיהּ שְׁתֵּים עֶשְׂרֵה חַלּוֹת:


לחם הפנים – it is explicitly written regarding them twelve loaves (Leviticus 24:5: “You shall take choice flour and bake of it twelve loaves/שתים עשרה חלות, two-tenths of a measure for each loaf”).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 6:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/6:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/6:5:6)


**וַחֲבִתֵּי כֹהֵן גָּדוֹל.** נֶאֱמַר בְּלֶחֶם הַפָּנִים חָק עוֹלָם וְנֶאֱמַר בַּחֲבִתֵּי כֹּהֵן גָּדוֹל חָק עוֹלָם, מַה לְּהַלָּן שְׁתֵּים עֶשְׂרֵה חַלּוֹת, אַף כָּאן שְׁתֵּים עֶשְׂרֵה חַלּוֹת, וּמַקְרִיבִין מֵהֶן שֵׁשׁ חַלּוֹת בַּבֹּקֶר וְשֵׁשׁ חַלּוֹת בָּעֶרֶב:


וחביתי כהן גדול – it is stated regarding the shew bread, it is stated, “a due for all time/חק עולם” (Leviticus 24:9) and it is stated regarding the baked cakes of the High Priest (Leviticus 6:15): “חק עולם/a law for all time,” just as there (i.e., with the shew bread) there are twelve loaves, so here too there are twelve loaves, and we offer up six loaves in the morning and six loaves in the evening.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 6:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/6:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/6:5:7)


**חוּץ מֵחַלּוֹת תּוֹדָה שֶׁהֵן בָּאוֹת עֶשֶׂר.** כִּדְבָעִינַן לְמֵימַר טַעֲמָא לְקַמָּן בְּאִידָךְ פִּרְקִין. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי מֵאִיר:


חוץ מחלות תודה שהן באות עשר (see Tractate Menahot, Chapter 7, Mishnah 1) – that we require to state the reason further on tin that chapter. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Meir.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 6:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/6:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/6:6:1)


**הָעֹמֶר הָיָה בָא עִשָּׂרוֹן מִשָּׁלֹשׁ סְאִין.** מִנְחַת הָעֹמֶר שֶׁמְּבִיאִין מִמָּחֳרַת הַפֶּסַח הָיוּ קוֹצְרִין שָׁלֹשׁ סְאִין דְּהַיְנוּ אֵיפָה שְׂעֹרִים, וְטוֹחֲנִין אוֹתוֹ, וּמְנִיפִין אוֹתוֹ בְּנָפָה שְׁלֹשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה פְּעָמִים עַד שֶׁמַּעֲמִידִים סָלְתּוֹ עַל עִשָּׂרוֹן שֶׁהִיא עֲשִׂירִית הָאֵיפָה. וְטַעֲמָא דְּבָעֵי עִשָּׂרוֹן שֶׁל עֹמֶר שָׁלֹשׁ סְאִים שֶׁל שְׂעֹרִים, כֵּיוָן דְּמִתְּבוּאָה חֲדָשָׁה הוּא וְשֶׁל שְׂעֹרִים יֵשׁ בָּהֶן סֻבִּין הַרְבֵּה וְסֹלֶת מֻעָט וְלֹא אָתֵי עֶשְׂרוֹנוֹ מֻבְחָר אֶלָּא מִשָּׁלֹשׁ סְאִין:


העומר היה בא עשרון משלש סאין – the meal-offering of the Omer that we bring on the morrow of [the first day of] Passover, they would reap three Seah which is an Ephah of barley, and grind it and wave it in a sieve thirteen times until they produce its fine flour of an Issaron which is the tenth of an Ephah. And the reason that they require an Issaron of Omer of three Seah of barley, since that it comes from new grain and it is of barley, , they have in it much coarse bran and a small amount of choice flour, and one doesn’t bring his choice Issaron other than from three Seah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 6:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/6:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/6:6:2)


**שְׁתֵּי הַלֶּחֶם שְׁנֵי עֶשְׂרוֹנִים מִשָּׁלֹשׁ סְאִין.** כֵּיוָן דְּמֵחִטִּים קָאָתוּ, אַף עַל גַּב דְּמִתְּבוּאָה חֲדָשָׁה אָתְיָין, דְּבַעֲצֶרֶת תְּבוּאָה שֶׁל חִטִּין חֲדָשָׁה הִיא, אֲפִלּוּ הָכִי שְׁנֵי עֶשְׂרוֹנִים מֻבְחָרִים אָתוּ מִשָּׁלֹשׁ סְאִין:


שתי הלחם שני עשרונים משלש סאים – for since they come from wheat, even though that it comes from new gain, for on Atzeret/Shavuot it is the new grain of wheat, nevertheless two choice tenths of an Ephah come from three Seah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 6:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/6:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/6:6:3)


**לֶחֶם הַפָּנִים עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבָּעָה עֶשְׂרוֹנִים.** דְּהָכִי כְּתִיב (ויקרא כד) שְׁתֵּים עֶשְׂרֵה חַלּוֹת שְׁנֵי עֶשְׂרֹנִים יִהְיֶה הַחַלָּה הָאֶחָת:


לחם הפנים עשרים וארבעים עשרונים – and thus it is written (Leviticus 24:5): “[You shall take choice flour] and bake of it twelve loaves, two-tenths of a measure for each loaf.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 6:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/6:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/6:6:4)


**מֵעֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבַּע סְאִים.** כֵּיוָן דְּמֵחִטִּין אָתוּ וּמִתְּבוּאָה יְשָׁנָה, נָפֵיק עִשָּׂרוֹן מֻבְחָר מִסְּאָה:


מעשרים וארבע סאים – since they come from wheat and from old grain, we remove one choice Issaron from the Seah.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 6:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/6:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/6:7:1)


**הָיָה מְנֻפֶּה בִּשְׁלֹשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה נָפָה.** זוֹ לְמַעְלָה מִזּוֹ. וְכָל זֶה לְמִצְוָה אֲבָל לֹא לְעַכֵּב, שֶׁאִם הֵבִיא עֹמֶר עִשָּׂרוֹן מֵאַרְבַּע סְאִין אוֹ שֶׁהֵבִיאוֹ מִשְּׁנֵי סְאִין, לֹא פָּסַל:


היה מנופה בשלש עשרה נפה – one above the other. And all of this is for the commandment, but it does not indispensable, for if he brought Omer Issaron from four Seah or he brought from two Seah, he did not invalidate it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 6:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/6:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/6:7:2)


**רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן אוֹמֵר לֹא הָיָה לָהּ קִצְבָּה.** דַּאֲפִלּוּ לְכַתְּחִלָּה לֹא נָתְנוּ חֲכָמִים קִצְבָּה מִכַּמָּה סְאִין חִטִּין אוֹ שְׂעֹרִין מְבִיאִין עֹמֶר וּשְׁתֵּי הַלֶּחֶם וְלֶחֶם הַפָּנִים, אֶלָּא רוֹאִין בַּסֹּלֶת שֶׁתְּהֵא מְנֻפָּה כָּל צָרְכָּהּ וְדַיּוֹ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


רבי שמעון אומר לא היה לה קצבה – and even ab initio the Sages did not give a limit from how many Seah of wheat or barely they bring the Omer or the two loaves or the shew bread, but rather we see in the choice flour that it was sifted according to its needs and it was sufficient. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon.



## Chapter 7


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 7:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:1:1)


**הַתּוֹדָה.** חָמֵשׁ סְאִין יְרוּשַׁלְמִיּוֹת שֶׁהֵן שֵׁשׁ מִדְבָּרִיּוֹת. חָמֵשׁ סְאִין שֶׁל יְרוּשָׁלַיִם הֵן שֵׁשׁ מֵאוֹתָן שֶׁהָיוּ בַּמִּדְבָּר בִּימֵי מֹשֶׁה. שֶׁהוֹסִיפוּ עַל הַמִּדָּה שֶׁהָיְתָה בִּימֵי מֹשֶׁה שְׁתּוּת. דְּקַיְמָא לָן מוֹסִיפִין עַל הַמִּדּוֹת וְאֵין מוֹסִיפִין יוֹתֵר מִשְּׁתוּת. וְאוֹתוֹ שְׁתּוּת הוּא שְׁתּוּת מִלְּבַר:


התודה חמש סאין ירושלמיות שהן שש מדבריות – five Seah of Jerusalem [measure] which are six from those that were in the wilderness during the days of Moses. That they added a sixth on the measure that was in the days of Moses. For we hold that we add on the measurements but we don’t add more than one-sixth. But that one-sixth is one-sixth externally/from the outside.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 7:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:1:2)


**שְׁתֵּי אֵיפוֹת.** כְּלוֹמַר, הָנָךְ שֵׁשׁ סְאִין הֵם שְׁתֵּי אֵיפוֹת, שֶׁהֵם עֶשְׂרִים עִשָּׂרוֹן, שֶׁהֲרֵי בְּכָל אֵיפָה עֲשָׂרָה עֶשְׂרוֹנוֹת:


שתי איפות – that is to say, these six Seah which are two Ephahs, which are twenty Issaron/tenths of an Ephah, for in each Ephah are ten Issarons/tenths.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 7:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:1:3)


**עֲשָׂרָה לֶחָמֵץ.** עֲשָׂרָה עֶשְׂרוֹנִים לְעֶשֶׂר חַלּוֹת חָמֵץ שֶׁבַּתּוֹדָה:


עשרה לחמץ – ten Issarons for ten unleavened loaves that are in the thanksgiving offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 7:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:1:4)


**רְבוּכָה.** חֲלוּטָה בְּמַיִם רוֹתְחִין קְרוּיָה רְבוּכָה. וְרַמְבַּ"ם פֵּרֵשׁ רְבוּכָה, מְרֻבָּה בְּשֶׁמֶן וּקְלוּיָה בּוֹ, לְפִי שֶׁהָיָה בָּהּ שֶׁמֶן כְּנֶגֶד הַחַלּוֹת וְהָרְקִיקִין:


רבוכה – a paste made by stirring flour in hot water which is called רבוכה/pulp of flour mixed with hot water and oil (see Rashi) . But Maimonides (see the Laws of Maaseh HaKorbanot, Chapter 9, Halakha 19) explained that his pulp of flour mixed with hot water and oil has a lot of oil and it is roasted/parched in it, because there was oil in it corresponding to the loaves and the wafers.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 7:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:1:5)


**נִמְצְאוּ שְׁלֹשָׁה עֶשְׂרוֹנוֹת וּשְׁלִישׁ לְכָל מִין וָמִין.** שֶׁבַּמַּצָּה:


נמצאו שלשה עשרונות ושליש לכל מין ומין – that is in the Matzah/unleavened bread.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 7:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:1:6)


**שָׁלֹשׁ חַלּוֹת לְעִשָּׂרוֹן.** דַּהֲוָה לְהוּ עֶשֶׂר חַלּוֹת לִשְׁלֹשָׁה עֶשְׂרוֹנוֹת וּשְׁלִישׁ:


שלש חלות לעשרון – they would have ten loaves for three Esronot and a third.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 7:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:1:7)


**בְּמִדָּה יְרוּשַׁלְמִית.** שֶׁלֹּא הָיוּ בָּהּ עֶשְׂרוֹנוֹת אֶלָּא קַבִּים, הָוֵי הָנָךְ חָמֵשׁ סְאִין שְׁלֹשִׁים קַבִּין. שֶׁהַסְּאָה שִׁשָּׁה קַבִּים. וּבַגְּמָרָא בַּבְּרַיְתָא מַיְתֵי מִגְּזֵרָה שָׁוָה שֶׁכָּל מִין וָמִין מֵאַרְבָּעָה מִינִים שֶׁבַּתּוֹדָה הָיוּ בָּאִים עֶשֶׂר חַלּוֹת, וְאָמְרוּ, נֶאֱמַר כָּאן וְהִקְרִיב מִמֶּנּוּ אֶחָד מִכָּל קָרְבָּן תְּרוּמָה לַה', וְנֶאֱמַר בִּתְרוּמַת מַעֲשֵׂר [מִמֶּנּוּ] תְּרוּמָה לַה', מַה לְּהַלָּן אֶחָד מֵעֶשֶׂר אַף כָּאן אֶחָד מֵעֶשֶׂר. וְלָמַדְנוּ שֶׁמִּין אֶחָד שֶׁל חָמֵץ הָיָה עֲשָׂרָה עֶשְׂרוֹנִים כְּנֶגֶד שְׁלֹשָׁה מִינִים שֶׁל מַצָּה, מִדִּכְתִיב (ויקרא ז) עַל חַלֹּת לֶחֶם חָמֵץ, אָמְרָה תּוֹרָה כְּנֶגֶד חָמֵץ הָבֵא מַצָּה:


במדה ירושלמית – they did not have in them Esronot but rather Kabim, that these five Seah would be thirty Kabim. For the Seah is six Kabim. But in the Gemara (Tractate Menahot 77a) in a Baraita, it brings from an analogy that each and every species from the four kinds that are in the thanksgiving offering, and they stated, it is stated here (Leviticus 7:14): “Out of this he shall offer one of each kind as a gift to the LORD; [it shall go to the priest who dashes the blood of the offering of well-being],” and it is stated regarding the tenth of the tenth (Numbers 15:21): “You shall set aside a gift to the LORD from the first yield of your baking, [throughout the ages].” Just as there it is one out of ten, so even here it is one out of ten. And we learned that one kind is of leavened [flour] was ten Issarons corresponding to the three species of Matzah, from what is written (Leviticus 7:13): “This offering, with cakes of leavened bread added, [he shall offer along with his thanksgiving sacrifice of well-being],” the Torah stated corresponding to the leavened that comes the unleavened.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 7:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:2:1)


**הַמִּלּוּאִים.** שֶׁהָיוּ בִּימֵי מֹשֶׁה. כְּשֶׁנִּתְחַנֵּךְ אַהֲרֹן וּבָנָיו לַכְּהֻנָּה:


המלואים – that were in the days of Moses when Aaron and his sons were inaugurated/dedicated into the priesthood.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 7:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:2:2)


**הָיוּ בָּאִים כַּמַּצָּה שֶׁבַּתּוֹדָה חַלּוֹת וּרְקִיקִים וּרְבוּכָה.** דִּכְתִיב בַּמִּלּוּאִים (שם ח) וּמִסַּל הַמַּצוֹת אֲשֶׁר לִפְנֵי ה'‏ לָקַח חַלַּת מַצָּה אַחַת וְחַלַּת לֶחֶם שֶׁמֶן אַחַת וְרָקִיק אֶחָד, חַלַּת מַצָּה אֵלּוּ חַלּוֹת, וְחַלַּת לֶחֶם שֶׁמֶן זוֹ רְבוּכָה דְּמוֹסִיף בָּהּ שֶׁמֶן כְּנֶגֶד חַלּוֹת וּרְקִיקִים, וְרָקִיק זֶה רָקִיק:


היו באים כמצה שבתודה חלות ורקיקים ורבוכה – as it is written in the inauguration (Leviticus 8:26): “From the basket of unleavened bread that was before the LORD, he took one cake of unleavened bread, one cake of oil bread, and one wafer, [and placed them on the fat parts and on the right thigh],” one cake of unleavened bread – these are the loaves, and the one cake of oil bread – this is the pulp of flour mixed with hot water and oil that they add into it oil corresponding to the loaves and the wafers. And the wafer is the wafter.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 7:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:2:3)


**נְזִירוּת.** לַחְמֵי נָזִיר, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (במדבר ו) וְסַל מַצּוֹת סֹלֶת חַלֹּת בְּלוּלֹת בַּשֶּׁמֶן וּרְקִיקֵי מַצּוֹת מְשֻׁחִים בַּשָּׁמֶן. וּרְבוּכָה לֹא הֻזְכַּר שָׁם:


נזירות – the loaves of the Nazirite, as it is stated (Numbers 6:15): “a basked of unleavened cakes of choice flour with oil mixed in, and unleavened wafters spread with oil; [and the proper grain offerings and libations],” but the pulp of flour mixed with oil and water is not mentioned there.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 7:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:2:4)


**שְׁתֵּי יָדוֹת.** שְׁנֵי חֲלָקִים שֶׁל מִינֵי מַצָּה שֶׁבַּתּוֹדָה הָיוּ בַּנְּזִירוּת, וּכְדִמְפָרֵשׁ וְאָזֵיל:


שתי ידות – two parts of kinds of unleavened bread that are in the thanksgiving offering they were in the Nazirite offering, as it is explained further on.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 7:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:2:5)


**נִמְצְאוּ.** לַנְּזִירוּת:


נמצאו – for the Nazirite.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 7:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:2:6)


**עֲשָׂרָה קַבִּין.** שֶׁהֲרֵי בַּשְּׁלֹשָׁה מִינֵי מַצָּה שֶׁבַּתּוֹדָה הָיוּ חֲמִשָּׁה עָשָׂר קַבִּין, וְהָוֵי לְהוּ עֲשָׂרָה שְׁנֵי יָדוֹת שֶׁל חֲמִשָּׁה עָשָׂר:


עשר קבין – that behold with the three kinds of unleavened bread that were associated with the thanksgiving offering, they were fifteen Kab, and they were ten [Kab] which were two thirds of the fifteen [for the Nazirite’s offering].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 7:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:2:7)


**שֶׁהֵן שֵׁשׁ עֶשְׂרוֹנוֹת וַעֲדוּיָן.** כְּלוֹמַר, עוֹד שֶׁנּוֹסָף עֲלֵיהֶן. שֶׁהֲרֵי הָיוּ שְׁלֹשָׁה עֶשְׂרוֹנִים וּשְׁלִישׁ לְכָל מִין כְּדַאֲמַרַן לְעֵיל גַּבֵּי מַצָּה שֶׁבַּתּוֹדָה, נִמְצָא לִשְׁנֵי מִינִים שִׁשָּׁה עֶשְׂרוֹנוֹת וּשְׁנֵי שְׁלִישֵׁי עִשָּׂרוֹן, וְהַיְנוּ דְּקָאָמַר שִׁשָּׁה עֶשְׂרוֹנוֹת וַעֲדוּיָן:


שהן שש עשרונות ועדוין – that is to say, more that was added to them, for there were three Issarons and one-third of each kind, as it was stated above concerning the unleavened bread that is in the thanksgiving offering, it is found that two kinds are six Issarons and two-thirds of an Issaron, which are as we state six Issaron and something left over.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 7:2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:2:8)


**וּמִכֻּלָּן.** מִכָּל אַרְבָּעָה מִינִים שֶׁבַּתּוֹדָה הָיוּ הַכֹּהֲנִים נוֹטְלִים אֶחָד מֵעֲשָׂרָה תְּרוּמָה:


ומכולן – from all of the four kinds that are in the thanksgiving offering, the Kohanim would take one from ten as a heave-offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 7:2:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:2:9)


**שֶׁלֹּא יִטֹּל פָּרוּס.** שָׁבוּר וְחָתוּךְ:


שלא יטול פרוס – broken and cut into.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 7:2:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:2:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:2:10)


**שֶׁיִּהְיוּ כָל הַקָּרְבָּנוֹת שָׁוִין.** הַמִּינִים יְהוּ שָׁוִין, עֲשָׂרָה חַלּוֹת לְכָל מִין:


שיהיה כל הקרבנות שוין – the kinds will be equivalent, ten loaves for each kind [of animal].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 7:2:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:2:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:2:11)


**שֶׁלֹּא יִטֹּל תְּרוּמָה מִקָּרְבָּן עַל חֲבֵרוֹ.** שֶׁאִם הָיָה מִמִּין זֶה חֲמִשָּׁה וּמִמִּין זֶה חֲמִשָּׁה עָשָׂר, נִמְצָא מַפְרִישׁ מִזֶּה עַל זֶה:


שלא יטול תרומה מקרבן על חבירו – that if there was five [loaves] from this kind [of animal] and fifteen [loaves] from that kind of animal, it is found that he would separate from this one for that one.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 7:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:3:1)


**הַשּׁוֹחֵט אֶת הַתּוֹדָה בִּפְנִים.** לִפְנִים מִן הָעֲזָרָה:


השוחט את התודה בפנים – inside from the Temple courtyard.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 7:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:3:2)


**וְלַחְמָהּ חוּץ לַחוֹמָה.** בַּגְּמָרָא מוֹקְמִינַן לַהּ חוּץ לְחוֹמַת בֵּית פַּאגִי. רַבּוֹתַי פֵּרְשׁוּ חוּץ לַחוֹמָה הַחִיצוֹנָה שֶׁל יְרוּשָׁלַיִם. [אֲבָל חוּץ לָעֲזָרָה קָדוֹשׁ]. וְאַף עַל גַּב דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא ז) וְהִקְרִיב עַל זֶבַח הַתּוֹדָה חַלּוֹת, דְּמַשְׁמַע לִכְאוֹרָה שֶׁיְּהֵא הַלֶּחֶם אֶצְלָהּ בִּשְׁעַת זְבִיחָה, לֹא דָרְשִׁינַן עַל בְּסָמוּךְ. וְרַמְבַּ"ם גּוֹרֵס בֵּית בַּגִּי. וּמְפָרֵשׁ שֶׁהוּא מָקוֹם קָרוֹב לְהַר הַבַּיִת אֶלָּא שֶׁהוּא חוּץ לְחוֹמַת הַר הַבַּיִת וְשָׁם אוֹפִים הַמְּנָחוֹת. וְעַל שֵׁם כָּךְ הָיוּ קוֹרִים לוֹ בֵּית בַּגִּי. לְשׁוֹן פַּת בַּג הַמֶּלֶךְ:


ולחמה חוץ לחומה – In the Gemara (Tractate Menahot 78b) we establish that it is outside the wall of the House of Pagi. My teachers/Rabbis explained outside the outer wall of Jerusalem [but outside of the Temple courtyard is holy]. But even though it is written (Leviticus 7:12): “[If he offers it (i.e., the sacrifice of well-being/peace offering) for thanksgiving,] he shall offer it together with the sacrifice of thanksgiving unleavened cakes/והקריב על זבח התודה חלות [with oil mixed in, unleavened wafers spread with oil, and cakes of choice flour with oil mixed in, well soaked],” that implies, apparently that the bread is near it at the time of the sacrifice, we don’t expound [the word] "על"/nearby. But Maimonides has the reading of “the house of Bagi.” And he explains that it is a place close to the Temple Mount which is outside the wall of the Temple Mount and there they bake the meal/grain offerings. And on account of this, they would call it Bet Bagi, the language of bread/Bag of the king.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 7:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:3:3)


**עַד שֶׁלֹּא קָרְמוּ בַתַּנּוּר.** לָאו לֶחֶם נִינְהוּ. אֶלָּא עִסָּה בְּעָלְמָא:


עד שלא קרמו בתנור – these are not bread, but rather mere dough [prior to their forming a crust].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 7:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:3:4)


**שְׁחָטָהּ.** עַל מְנָת לְאָכְלָה חוּץ לִזְמַנָּהּ, קָדַשׁ הַלֶּחֶם וְנִפְגַּל. עַל מְנָת לְאָכְלָהּ חוּץ לִמְקוֹמָהּ, קָדַשׁ הַלֶּחֶם וְנִפְסַל. וְטַעֲמָא דְּקָדַשׁ הַלֶּחֶם, מִשּׁוּם דִּפְסוּלוֹ בַּקֹּדֶשׁ, וְקַיְמָא לָן כָּל שֶׁפְּסוּלוֹ בַּקֹּדֶשׁ הַקֹּדֶשׁ מְקַבְּלוֹ:


שחטה – in order to eat it outside of its appropriate time, the bread is sanctified, but it (i.e., the offering) was done with the wrong intention and is disqualified. If on condition to eat it outside of its appropriate place, the bread is sanctified but it (i.e., the sacrifice) is disqualified. But the reason that the bread is sanctified, is because it is disqualified within the Temple courtyard. And we hold that any offering that was disqualified within the Temple courtyard, the Temple courtyard, the Temple courtyard receives it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 7:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:3:5)


**שְׁחָטָהּ וְנִמְצֵאת טְרֵפָה לֹא קָדַשׁ הַלָּחֶם.** דִּפְסוּלוֹ קֹדֶם שְׁחִיטָה הוּא:


שחטה ונמצאת טריפה לא קדש הלחם – for its disqualification occurred prior to ritual slaughtering (see also Tractate Zevakhim, Chapter 9, Mishnayot 2-3).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 7:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:3:6)


**שְׁחָטָהּ וְנִמְצֵאת בַּעֲלַת מוּם רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר אוֹמֵר קָדָשׁ.** בַּגְּמָרָא מוֹקֵי לַהּ בְּדֻקִּין שֶׁבָּעַיִן, דִּבְכִי הַאי מוּמָא סָבַר רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר דְּאִם עָלוּ לֹא יֵרְדוּ הוֹאִיל וְאֵין מוּמָן נִכָּר, הִלְכָּךְ קָדַשׁ הַלֶּחֶם. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר:


שחטה ונמצאת בעלת מום ר' אליעזר אומר קדש – In the Gemara (Tractate Menahot 79a), it establishes it as a defect in the eye [which is not recognized], that with this defect Rabbi Eliezer held that if they (i.e., the sacrifices) went up [to the altar], they should not go down, since their defect is not recognized, therefore, the bread is sanctified. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Eliezer.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 7:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:3:7)


**שֶׁלֹּא לִשְׁמָהּ.** לֹא קָדַשׁ הַלֶּחֶם, דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא ז) עַל חַלֹּת לֶחֶם חָמֵץ יַקְרִיב קָרְבָּנוֹ עַל זֶבַח תּוֹדַת שְׁלָמָיו, עַל זֶבַח שֶׁנִּזְבַּח לְשֵׁם תּוֹדַת שְׁלָמָיו:


שלא לשמה – the bread is not sanctified, as it is written (Leviticus 7:13): “This offering, with cakes of leavened bread added, he shall offer along with his thanksgiving sacrifice of well-being,” on the sacrifice that was made for the sake of the peace-offerings of thanksgiving.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 7:3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:3:8)


**וְכֵן אֵיל הַמִּלּוּאִים.** לְפִי שֶׁהֵן הָיוּ תְּחִלָּה לְכָל הַקָּרְבָּנוֹת נָקַט אֵיל הַמִּלּוּאִים. וְהוּא הַדִּין לְאֵיל נָזִיר, דְּמִלּוּאִים כְּקָרְבַּן יָחִיד הֵן חֲשׁוּבִים:


וכן איל המילואים – because they were the first of all the sacrifices, it (i.e., the Mishnah) took the ram of ordination. And the same law applies to the ram of the Nazirite, for the rams of ordination are considered like the sacrifice of an individual.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 7:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:4:1)


**נְסָכִים שֶׁקָּדְשׁוּ בִכְלִי.** לָאו דַּוְקָא בִּכְלִי. שֶׁאֵין נְסָכִים מִתְקַדְּשִׁים לִפָּסֵל בְּיוֹצֵא וּבְלִינָה וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁקָּדְשׁוּ בִּכְלִי, אֶלָּא בִּשְׁחִיטַת הַזֶּבַח, דִּכְתִיב זֶבַח וּנְסָכִים, הַנְּסָכִים תְּלוּיִים בַּזֶּבַח:


נסכים שקדשו בכלי – not exactly in a utensil. For the libations are not sanctified to be defiled by going out [of the Temple] or by being kept overnight, even though they were sanctified in a vessel, other than through the ritual slaughtering of the sacrifice, as it is written (Leviticus 23:37): “[Those are the set times of the LORD that you shall celebrate as sacred occasions, bringing gifts to the LORD – burnt offerings, grain offerings,] sacrifices and libations, [on each day what is proper to it],” the libations are dependent upon the sacrifices.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 7:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:4:2)


**וְנִמְצָא הַזֶּבַח פָּסוּל.** שֶׁנִּפְסַל בַּזְּרִיקָה. דְּאִלּוּ בַּשְּׁחִיטָה נִפְסַל, לֹא קָדְשׁוּ נְסָכִים:


ונמצא הזבח פסול – that it was disqualified through the sprinkling [of the blood], for had it become disqualified through ritual slaughter, the libations would not be sanctified.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 7:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:4:3)


**אִם יֶשׁ שָׁם זֶבַח אַחֵר יִקְרְבוּ עִמּוֹ.** בִּנְסָכִים שֶׁל צִבּוּר מוֹקְמִינַן לַהּ לְמַתְנִיתִין, מִשּׁוּם דְּלֵב בֵּית דִּין מַתְנֶה עֲלֵיהֶם, אִם הֻצְרְכוּ לְזֶבַח זֶה הֻצְרְכוּ וְאִם לָאו יִהְיוּ לְזֶבַח אַחֵר. אֲבָל בִּנְסָכִים שֶׁל יָחִיד אֵין כְּשֵׁרִים לְזֶבַח אַחֵר. וַאֲפִלּוּ נְסָכִים שֶׁל צִבּוּר אֵין קְרֵבִים עִם זֶבַח אַחֵר אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן הָיָה אוֹתוֹ זֶבַח זָבוּחַ כְּשֶׁנִּפְסַל הַזֶּבַח הָרִאשׁוֹן, אֲבָל אִם לֹא הָיָה זָבוּחַ בְּשָׁעָה שֶׁנִּפְסַל הַזֶּבַח הָרִאשׁוֹן, אֵין קְרֵבִים עִם הַזֶּבַח הָאַחֵר. וּמַתְנִיתִין חַסּוֹרֵי מִחַסְּרָא וְהָכִי קָתָנֵי, יִקְרְבוּ עִמּוֹ, בַּמֶּה דְּבָרִים אֲמוּרִים שֶׁהָיָה זֶבַח זָבוּחַ בְּאוֹתָהּ שָׁעָה, אֲבָל אִם לֹא הָיָה זֶבַח זָבוּחַ בְּאוֹתָהּ שָׁעָה נַעֲשֶׂה כְּמוֹ שֶׁנִּפְסְלוּ בְּלִינָה וּפְסוּלִים. וְהָכִי מִפָּרְשָׁא מַתְנִיתִין בַּגְּמָרָא. וְכָל זְמַן שֶׁלֹּא נִתְקַדְּשׁוּ נְסָכִים בִּכְלִי אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁנִּשְׁחַט הַזֶּבַח יְכוֹלִים לְהַקְרִיב נְסָכִים אֲפִלּוּ אַחַר כַּמָּה יָמִים, דְּקַיְמָא לָן מֵבִיא אָדָם קָרְבָּנוֹ הַיּוֹם וְנִסְכּוֹ אֲפִלּוּ אַחַר כַּמָּה יָמִים, בֵּין נְסָכִים שֶׁל יָחִיד בֵּין נְסָכִים שֶׁל צִבּוּר. וְכֵן מְצֹרָע מֵבִיא אֲשָׁמוֹ הַיּוֹם וְלֹג שֶׁמֶן שֶׁלּוֹ לְאַחַר כַּמָּה יָמִים:


אם יש שם זבח אחר יקרבו עמו – we establish it in our Mishnah with the libations of the community, because the heart of the Jewish court makes a condition upon them, if they were needed for this sacrifice, they were needed, but if not, they would be for another sacrifice. But regarding the libation of an individual [offering], they are not appropriate/fit for another offering. But even the libations of the communal [offering] are not offered with another sacrifice unless it was the same sacrifice that was offered when the first sacrifice was disqualified, but if it was not offered at the time when the first sacrifice was disqualified, it is not offered with another sacrifice. But our Mishnah is deficient and should be read as follows: it should be sacrificed with it. When is this said? That the sacrifice was offered at the same time, but if the sacrifice was not offered at the same time, it becomes like it was disqualified by staying overnight and they are disqualified. And this is how our Mishnah is explained in the Gemara (see Tractate Menahot 79b). And all the time that the libations were not sanctified in a utensil, even though the sacrifice was ritually slaughtered, we are able to offer the libations even after several days, for we hold that a person brings his sacrifice today and his libation even after sevral days, whether it is the libations of an individual or whether it is the libations of the community. And similarly, the leper brings his guilt offering today and his Log of oil after several days.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 7:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:4:4)


**וְלַד תּוֹדָה.** שֶׁהִפְרִישׁ תּוֹדָה מְעֻבֶּרֶת וְיָלְדָה:


ולד תודה – he separated out a pregnant thanksgiving offering and its young.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 7:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:4:5)


**וְכֵן הַמַּפְרִישׁ תּוֹדָתוֹ וְאָבְדָה וְהִפְרִישׁ אַחֶרֶת תַּחְתֶּיהָ אֵינָהּ טְעוּנָה לֶחֶם.** הָרִאשׁוֹנָה, אִם נִמְצֵאת לְאַחַר הַקְרָבַת שְׁנִיָּה. וְכֵן שְׁנִיָּה אִם נִמְצֵאת הָרִאשׁוֹנָה קֹדֶם הַקְרָבָתָהּ וְהִקְרִיב רִאשׁוֹנָה, שׁוּב אֵין הַשְּׁנִיָּה טְעוּנָה לֶחֶם:


וכן המפריש תודתו ואבדה והפריש אחרת תחתיה אינה טעונה לחם – the first [bread] if it (i.e., the first sacrifice) was found after the offering up of the second. And similarly, the second, if the first was found prior to its being offered up and he offered up the first, the second no longer requires bread.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 7:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:4:6)


**חֲלִיפָתָהּ.** הַיְנוּ הַמַּפְרִישׁ תּוֹדָה וְאָבְדָה וְהִפְרִישׁ אַחֶרֶת תַּחְתֶּיהָ:


חליפתה – that is that he separates a thanksgiving offering and it was lost and he separated out another one in its place.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 7:4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:4:7)


**תְּמוּרָתָהּ.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁעוֹמֶדֶת בְּעֵין וְאוֹמֵר זוֹ תְּמוּרַת זוֹ, וּכְתִיב (ויקרא כז) וְהָיָה הוּא וּתְמוּרָתוֹ יִהְיֶה קֹּדֶשׁ:


תמורתה – as for example, that it stands clearly visible and he states, “this is in place of that”, and it is written (Leviticus 27:33): “then it and its substitute shall be holy: [it cannot be redeemed].”



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 7:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:5:1)


**יָבִיא הִיא וְלַחְמָהּ מִן הַחֻלִּין.** וְלֹא מִן הַמַּעֲשֵׂר. דְּכֵיוָן דְּאָמַר הֲרֵי עָלַי הָוֵי לֵיהּ דָּבָר שֶׁבְּחוֹבָה, וְכָל דָּבָר שֶׁבְּחוֹבָה אֵינוֹ בָּא אֶלָּא מִן הַחֻלִּין:


יביא הוא ולחמה מן החולין – but not from [Second] Tithe. For since he said, “I pledge myself/הרי עלי,” this is for him a matter that is of liability, and every matter that is of a liability does not come other than from that that which is unconsecrated.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 7:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:5:2)


**וְאִם אָמַר הֲרֵי עָלַי תּוֹדָה מִן הַחֻלִּין וְלַחְמָהּ מִן הַמַּעֲשֵׂר יָבִיא הִיא וְלַחְמָהּ מִן הַחֻלִּין.** דְּלֶחֶם נִגְרָר אַחַר תּוֹדָה, וְכֵיוָן דְּאָמַר הֲרֵי עָלַי תּוֹדָה מִן הַחֻלִּין בְּעַל כָּרְחֵיהּ אִיחַיַּב לֵיהּ בַּלֶּחֶם, הִלְכָּךְ הַאי דְּמַהֲדַר וְאָמַר לַחְמָהּ מִן הַמַּעֲשֵׂר לָאו כְּלוּם הוּא:


ואם אמר הרי עלי תודה מן החולין ולחמה מן המעשר יביא היא ולחמה מן החולם – for the bread is dragged (i.e., attached) to the thanksgiving offering, for since he said, “I pledge myself [to bring] a thank-offering from that which is unconsecrated, he is forced to become liable for the bread, therefore, that which he repeats and states that its bread is from the [Second] Tithe is nothing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 7:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:5:3)


**תּוֹדָה מִן הַמַּעֲשֵׂר וְלַחְמָהּ מִן הַחֻלִּין יָבִיא.** כְּלוֹמַר יָבִיא כְּמוֹ שֶׁנָּדַר. וְלָאו חוֹבָה, דְּכָל שֶׁכֵּן אִם יָבִיא שְׁנֵיהֶם מִן הַחֻלִּין דְּשַׁפִּיר עֲבַד. אֶלָּא אִם רָצָה לְהָבִיא כְּמוֹ שֶׁנָּדַר יָבִיא:


תודה מן המעשר ולחמה מן החולין יביא – that is to say, he shall bring according to what he vowed. But it is not a requirement, for all the more so, if he would bring both from that which is unconsecrated, he acted well. But if he wants to bring like he vowed, he can bring.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 7:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:5:4)


**וְלֹא יָבִיא.** הַלֶּחֶם: **מֵחִטֵּי מַעֲשֵׂר שֵׁנִי.** עַצְמוֹ, דְּלֶחֶם דּוּמְיָא דִּשְׁלָמִים בָּעִינַן, מַה שְּׁלָמִים מִמְּעוֹת מַעֲשֵׂר שֵׁנִי וְלֹא מִמַּעֲשֵׂר שֵׁנִי עַצְמוֹ, אַף לֶחֶם כֵּן:


ולא יביא הלחם מחטי מעשר שני עצמו – for we require bead that is similar to peace-offerings. Just as peace-offerings come from Second Tithe monies and not from Second Tithe itself, so too, the bread.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 7:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:6:1)


**מַה פֶּסַח שֶׁהוּא בָא בְחוֹבָה אֵינוֹ בָא אֶלָּא מִן הַחֻלִּין.** דְּפֶסַח מִצְרַיִם לֹא בָּא אֶלָּא מִן הַחֻלִּין, שֶׁעֲדַיִן לֹא הָיְתָה לָהֶם שׁוּם תְּבוּאַת מַעֲשֵׂר שֵׁנִי, שֶׁאֵין מַעֲשֵׂר אֶלָּא מִשֶּׁנִּכְנְסוּ לָאָרֶץ, וּמַה פֶּסַח מִצְרַיִם לֹא בָּא אֶלָּא מִן הַחֻלִּין, אַף פֶּסַח דּוֹרוֹת אֵינוֹ בָּא אֶלָּא מִן הַחֻלִּין, שֶׁהֲרֵי הוּא אוֹמֵר (שמות יג) וְעָבַדְתָּ אֶת הָעֲבֹדָה הַזֹּאת בַּחֹדֶשׁ הַזֶּה, שֶׁיְּהוּ כָּל עֲבוֹדַת הַחֹדֶשׁ הַזֶּה כְּזֶה שֶׁל מִצְרַיִם:


מה פסח שהוא בא בחובה אינו בא אלא מן החולין – that the Passover offering of Egypt did not come other than from unconsecrated [animals], for they did not yet have any Second Tithe grain for there was no tithe until they entered into the Land [of Israel], and just as the Passover offering of Egypt did not come other than from unconsecrated [animals] even the Passover offering for the generations did not come other than from unconsecrated [animals], for it states (Exodus 13:5): “You shall observe in this month the following practice,” that all the Divine service of this month would be like that of Egypt.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 7:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:6:2)


**אַף כָּל דָּבָר שֶׁבְּחוֹבָה.** נַמִּי כוּ'. לְפִיכָךְ, הָאוֹמֵר הֲרֵי עָלַי תּוֹדָה אוֹ שְׁלָמִים, הוֹאִיל וְהֵן בָּאִים חוֹבָה, דְּקָאָמַר הֲרֵי עָלַי, לֹא יָבִיא אֶלָּא מִן הַחֻלִּין:


אף כל דבר שבחובה נמי כו' - therefore, the person who states, “I pledge myself [to bring] a thank offering or a peace offering” for since they come as an obligation, as it states, “I pledge myself [to bring}, he should not bring anything other than from that which is unconsecrated.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 7:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/7:6:3)


**וְהַנְּסָכִים.** אֲפִלּוּ אָמַר הֲרֵי עָלַי לְהָבִיא מִמַּעֲשֵׂר, לֹא יָבִיא אֶלָּא מִן הַחֻלִּין. דְּכִי שָׁרָא רַחֲמָנָא לַאֲתוֹיֵי שְׁלָמִים מִמַּעֲשֵׂר הָנֵי מִלֵּי שְׁלָמִים גּוּפַיְהוּ דִּבְנֵי אֲכִילָה נִינְהוּ, אֲבָל נְסָכִים דְּכָלִיל הֵם לֹא יָבִיאוּ מִן הַמַּעֲשֵׂר:


והנסכים – even if he said, “I pledge myself to bring them from [Second] Tithe, he should not bring them other than from unconsecrated [animals]. For when the All-Merciful permits to include peace-offerings from [Second] Tithe, these words of the peace-offerings themselves, are those things that are consumed, but the libations are burned entirely [on the altar] , he should not bring them from the [Second] Tithe.



## Chapter 8


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:1:1)


**כָּל קָרְבָּנוֹת.** מִן הֶחָדָשׁ וּמִן הַיָּשָׁן. בִּמְנָחוֹת קָאָמַר:


כל קרבנות. מן החדש ומן הישן – it is speaking of meal-offerings.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:1:2)


**שֶׁאֵינָן בָּאִים אֶלָּא מִן הֶחָדָשׁ.** בָּעֹמֶר כְּתִיב (ויקרא כג) מִנְחָה חֲדָשָׁה, וּבִשְׁתֵּי הַלֶּחֶם כְּתִיב (שמות לד) בִּכּוּרֵי קְצִיר חִטִּים:


שאינן באים אלא מן החדש – regarding the Omer, it is written (Leviticus 23:16): “an offering of new grain,” and in regard to the two loaves of bread, it is written (Exodus 34:22): “of the first fruits of the wheat harvest.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:1:3)


**וּמִן הָאָרֶץ.** בָּעֹמֶר כְּתִיב (ויקרא כג) כִּי תָבֹאוּ אֶל הָאָרֶץ וְגוֹ'‏ וּקְצַרְתֶּם אֶת קְצִירָהּ, וּבִשְׁתֵּי הַלֶּחֶם כְּתִיב (שם) מִמּוֹשְׁבֹתֵיכֶם תָּבִיאוּ לֶחֶם:


ומן הארץ – in regard to the Omer, it is written (Leviticus 23:10): “When you enter the Land [that I am giving you and you reap its harvest,” and with the two loaves [of bread] it is written (Leviticus 23:17): “You shall bring from your settlements two loaves of bread [as an elevation offering].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:1:4)


**מִכְמָס וְזוֹנִיחָה.** שֵׁמוֹת שֶׁל מְקוֹמוֹת הֵן:


מכמס וזוניחה – these are names of places (see Ezra 2:27 and Joshua 15:35, 56).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:1:5)


**אַלְפָא לַסֹּלֶת.** סֹלֶת שֶׁלָּהֶן רִאשׁוֹן וּמֻבְחָר לְכָל הַסְּלָתוֹת. כְּאָלֶ"ף זוֹ שֶׁהִיא רִאשׁוֹנָה לְכָל הָאוֹתִיּוֹת:


אלפא לסלת – their choice flour is first and chosen to all the choice/fine flour. Like this Aleph, it is the first of all the letters.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:1:6)


**אַלְפָא.** זוֹ אָלֶ"ף בְּלָשׁוֹן יְוָנִי:


אלפא – this is Aleph in the Greek language.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:1:7)


**שְׁנִיָּה לָהֶן.** קְרוֹבָה סָלְתָּהּ לִהְיוֹת מְשֻׁבַּחַת כְּסֹלֶת מִכְמָס וְזוֹנִיחָה:


שנייה להן – its choice flour is close to being praiseworthy like the choice flour of Michmas and Zonicha.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:1:8)


**חֲפָרַיִם בַּבִּקְעָה.** שְׁתֵּי חֲפָרַיִם הֵן, אַחַת בָּהָר וְאַחַת בַּבִּקְעָה, וְאוֹתָהּ שֶׁבַּבִּקְעָה הִיא שֶׁסֹּלֶת שֶׁלָּהּ מְשֻׁבַּחַת:


חפרים בבקעה – these are two Chafarayim -one in the mountains and one in the valley, and the one that is in the valley that its choice flour is praiseworthy.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:1:9)


**כָּל הָאֲרָצוֹת.** שֶׁל אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל הָיוּ כְּשֵׁרוֹת, אֶלָּא שֶׁמִּכָּאן הָיוּ מְבִיאִים:


כל הארצות – of the Land of Israel they were kosher/fit, but from here (i.e., the places mentioned in the Mishnah) they would bring them.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:2:1)


**אֵין מְבִיאִין.** עֹמֶר וּשְׁתֵּי הַלֶּחֶם:


אין מביאין – the Omer nor the two loaves [of bread].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:2:2)


**לֹא מִבֵּית הַזְּבָלִים.** מִשָּׂדֶה שֶׁצְּרִיכָה לְזֶבֶל, דְּשֶׁמָּא לֹא נִזְדַּבְּלָה כָּל צָרְכָּהּ וְנִמְצְאוּ פֵּרוֹתֶיהָ כְּחוּשִׁים. אִי נַמִּי, לְפִי שֶׁהַזֶּבֶל מַבְאִישׁ וּמַפְסִיד טַעַם הַפְּרִי:


לא מבית הזבלים – from a field that needs to be manured, for perhaps it was not manured all of its need and it was found that its fruits were lean/weak. Alternatively, since the manure deteriorates and causes the loss of taste of the fruit.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:2:3)


**וְלֹא מִבֵּית הַשְּׁלָחִים.** אֶרֶץ צְמֵאָה לְמַיִם שֶׁפֵּרוֹתֶיהָ כְּחוּשִׁין:


ולא מבית השלחים – a land that is thirsty for water that its fruits are lean/week.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:2:4)


**לֹא מִבֵּית הָאִילָן.** מִתְּבוּאָה שֶׁבֵּין הָאִילָנוֹת. שֶׁהָאִילָנוֹת הַגְּדֵלִים שָׁם יוֹנְקִים הַקַּרְקַע וּמַכְחִישִׁים הַזְּרָעִים:


ולא מבית האילן – from grain that is among the trees, that the trees that grow there suck the ground and weaken the seeds.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:2:5)


**נָרָהּ.** חוֹרֵשׁ. לְשׁוֹן נִירוּ לָכֶם נִיר (ירמיה ד):


נרה – ploughs. It is the language of (Jeremiah 4:3): “Break up the untilled ground,[and do not sow among thorns].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:2:6)


**וּבַשְּׁנִיָּה זוֹרְעָהּ.** בַּגְּמָרָא מַסִּיק, דְּשָׁנָה רִאשׁוֹנָה הוּא נָר כֻּלָּהּ וְזוֹרֵעַ חֶצְיָהּ וּמַנִּיחַ חֶצְיָהּ נִיר. וְכֵן בַּשְּׁנִיָּה חוֹרְשָׁהּ כֻּלָּהּ וְזוֹרֵעַ הַחֵצִי שֶׁלֹּא זָרַע אֶשְׁתָּקַד, וְהַחֵצִי שֶׁזָּרַע אֶשְׁתָּקַד מַנִּיחוֹ נִיר. וְכֵן בְּכָל שָׁנָה זוֹרֵעַ נִיר שֶׁל אֶשְׁתָּקַד:


ובשניה זורעה – In the Gemara (Tractate Menahot 84b) it reaches the definite conclusion that in the first year, he breaks up all of it and seeds half of it and leaves half of it as newly broken land, and similarly in the second year he ploughs all of it and seeds the half hat he didn’t seed in the previous year, and the half that he seeded in the previous year, he leaves it as broken land, and similarly in every year he seeds that which was broken in the previous year.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:2:7)


**כֵּיצַד בּוֹדֵק.** הַסֹּלֶת אִם מְנֻפָּה כָּל צָרְכָּהּ אִם לָאו:


כיצד בודק – the choice flour if it is winnowed/sifted all that it needs to be or not.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:2:8)


**עָלָה בְיָדוֹ אָבָק.** קֶמַח דַּק, וְגָרוּעַ הוּא:


עלה בידו אבק – thin flour and it is inferior.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:2:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:2:9)


**עַד שֶׁיְּנִיפֶנָּה.** בְּנָפָה לַעֲבֹר הָאָבָק דַּק שֶׁנִּשְׁאַר בָּהּ:


עד שיניפנה – with a winnow to remove the thin dust that remains in it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:2:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:2:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:2:10)


**וְאִם הִתְלִיעָה.** הַסֹּלֶת אוֹ הַחִטָּה:


ואם התליעה – the choice flour or the wheat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:2:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:2:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:2:11)


**פְּסוּלָה.** וְהוּא שֶׁהִתְלִיעָה בְּרֻבָּהּ. וְיָלְפִינַן לַהּ לְקַמָּן סוֹף פִּרְקִין מִדִּכְתִיב (במדבר כח) תְּמִימִם יִהְיוּ לָכֶם וּמִנְחָתָם. תְּמִימִים יִהְיוּ לָכֶם וְנִסְכֵּיהֶם. שֶׁיִּהְיוּ גַּם כֵּן הַמְּנָחוֹת וְהַנְּסָכִים תְּמִימִים:


פסולה – and its majority had become maggoty/wormy. And we derive it further on at the end of the chapter (see Mishnah 7) from what is written (Numbers 28:19-20): “see that are without blemish. The grain offering with them,” (Numbers 28:31): “You shall present them – see that they are without blemish with their libations,” that the meal-offerings and the libations will be pure.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:3:1)


**תְּקוֹעָה.** עִיר שֶׁשְּׁמָהּ תְּקוֹעָה, כְּדִכְתִיב (שמואל ב יד) וַיִּשְׁלַח יוֹאָב תְּקוֹעָה:


תקועה – a city whose name is Tekoa, as it is written (II Samuel 14:2): “So Joab sent to Tekoa [and bought a clever woman from there].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:3:2)


**אַלְפָא לַשָּׁמֶן.** הַשֶּׁמֶן שֶׁלָּהּ רִאשׁוֹן וּמֻבְחָר לַשְּׁמָנִים. כְּאָלֶ"ף זוֹ שֶׁהִיא רִאשׁוֹנָה לָאוֹתִיּוֹת:


אלפא לשמן – its oil is first-rate and choice of the oils. As this Aleph is the first of the letters.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:3:3)


**אַנְפִּיקְנוֹן.** שֶׁמֶן הֶעָשׂוּי מִזֵּיתִים שֶׁלֹּא הֵבִיאוּ שְׁלִישׁ בִּשּׁוּלָן, וְהוּא מַר מְאֹד:


אנפיקנון – oil that is made from olives that did not bring one-third of their ripening and it is very bitter.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:3:4)


**שֶׁנִּשְׁרוּ בַמַּיִם.** שֶׁהַמַּיִם מְקַלְקְלִים אֶת הַשֶּׁמֶן:


שנשרו במים – that the water ruins the oil.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:4:1)


**שְׁלֹשָׁה זֵיתִים.** שָׁלֹשׁ פְּעָמִים בַּשָּׁנָה מְלַקְּטִים אֶת הַזֵּיתִים. וּבָהֶן שָׁלֹשׁ שְׁמָנִים, בְּכָל פַּעַם יֵשׁ שְׁלֹשָׁה מִינִים שֶׁמֶן:


שלשה זיתים – three times during the year they harvest/prepare the olives. And in them there are three oils, and each time there are three kinds of oil.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:4:2)


**הַזַּיִת הָרִאשׁוֹן.** פַּעַם רִאשׁוֹנָה שֶׁמְּלַקֵּט:


הזית הראשון – the first time that they harvest/prepare [the olives].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:4:3)


**מְגַרְגְּרוֹ בְרֹאשׁ הַזַּיִת.** מְלַקֵּט גַּרְגְּרִים שֶׁהֵן בְּרֹאשׁ הַזַּיִת, שֶׁהֵן מִתְבַּשְּׁלִים תְּחִלָּה לְפִי שֶׁהַחַמָּה זוֹרַחַת עֲלֵיהֶן וּמְבַשַּׁלְתָּן:


מגרגרו בראש הזית – he harvests the berries that are at the top of the olive/tree, for they ripen first because the sun shines upon them and ripens them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:4:4)


**לְתוֹךְ הַסַּל.** וּמִסְתַּנֵּן וְיוֹצֵא, וְהַכְּלִי תַּחַת הַסַּל לְקַבֵּל הַשֶּׁמֶן:


לתוך הסל – he refines them and [the oil] comes up and there is a utensil underneath the basked to receive the oil.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:4:5)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר סְבִיבוֹת דָּפְנֵי הַסַּל.** נוֹתֵן אֶת הַזֵּיתִים וְהַשֶּׁמֶן זָב דֶּרֶךְ הַדְּפָנוֹת וְנוֹפֵל לְשׁוּלֵי הַסַּל וּמִשָּׁם מִסְתַּנֵּן וְיוֹצֵא, וְנִמְצָא מְזֻקָּק, שֶׁהַפְּסֹלֶת נִשְׁאָר מְדֻבָּק בְּדָפְנֵי הַסַּל. אֲבָל לֹא יִתְּנֵם בְּשׁוּלֵי הַסַּל, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁמִתְעָרֵב בּוֹ שֶׁמֶן וּשְׁמָרִים וְיוֹצְאִים וְנִמְצָא שֶׁמֶן עָכוּר. וְהַשֶּׁמֶן הַזָּב מֵאֵלָיו בְּלֹא שׁוּם טְעִינָה נִקְרָא שֶׁמֶן רִאשׁוֹן שֶׁל הַזַּיִת הָרִאשׁוֹן:


רבי יהודה אומר סביבות דופני הסל – he places the olives and the oil flows through the walls and falls into the rim of the basket and from there he refines it and goes up and it is found to be refined . For the refuse that remains is attached on to the walls of the basket. But he soul dot place them on the rim at the bottom of the basket, because they get combined with the oil and the sediment/lees and it is found that the oil is turbid. But the oil that flows from itself without any assistance rendered in loading up is called the first oil of the first olive.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:4:6)


**טָעַן בְּקוֹרָה.** זֵיתִים שֶׁבַּסַּל:


טען בקורה – the olives that are in the basket.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:4:7)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר בַּאֲבָנִים.** וְלֹא בְּקוֹרָה. שֶׁהַקּוֹרָה כְּבֵדָה וּמוֹצִיאָה אֶת הַשְּׁמָרִים:


רבי יהודה אומר באבנים – but not with a beam, for the beam is heaven and it removes the sediment/lees.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:4:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:4:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:4:8)


**חָזַר וְטָחַן.** בָּרֵחַיִם, אֶת הַזֵּיתִים שֶׁתַּחַת הַקּוֹרָה, וְטוֹעֵן אַחַר כָּךְ הַקּוֹרָה:


חזר וטחן – with a millstone, those olives that are under the beam and the beam presses them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:4:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:4:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:4:9)


**הָרִאשׁוֹן לַמְּנוֹרָה.** דְּבָעִינַן שֶׁמֶן זַיִת זָךְ:


הראשון למנורה – that we require pure olive oil (see Leviticus 24:2).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:4:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:4:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:4:10)


**וְהַשְּׁאָר כָּשֵׁר לַמְּנָחוֹת.** דְּלֹא כְּתִיב בְּהוּ זָךְ:


והשאר כשר למנחות – for it is not written with them that it is pure [olive oil].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:4:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:4:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:4:11)


**הַזַּיִת הַשֵּׁנִי.** פַּעַם שְׁנִיָּה כְּשֶׁמְּלַקֵּט אֶת שֶׁנִּמְצָאִים מְבֻשָּׁלִים עַתָּה:


הזית השני – the second time when he harvests that which is found that are ripened now.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:4:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:4:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:4:12)


**מְגַרְגְּרוֹ בְרֹאשׁ הַגַּג.** מְלַקֵּט הַגַּרְגְּרִין הַסְּמוּכִים לַגַּג. שֶׁזֵּיתֵיהֶן הָיוּ סְמוּכִים לְגַגּוֹתֵיהֶן, וְאוֹתָן מִתְבַּשְּׁלִים בַּשְּׁנִיָּה:


מגרגרו בראש הגג – he harvests the berries that are near the roof, for their olives were near their rooves, and those ripen second.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:4:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:4:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:4:13)


**הָרִאשׁוֹן.** שֶׁיָּצָא קֹדֶם טְעִינָה, כָּשֵׁר לַמְּנוֹרָה:


הראשון – that went out prior to the pressing [of the beam] are fit/appropriate for the Menorah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:4:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:4:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:4:14)


**הַזַּיִת הַשְּׁלִישִׁי.** שֶׁמִּתְלַקֵּט פַּעַם שְׁלִישִׁית, אֵין מִתְבַּשְּׁלִים לְעוֹלָם כָּל צָרְכָּן, שֶׁהֵן עֲנָפִים שֶׁתַּחַת הַגַּג שֶׁאֵין חַמָּה מַגַּעַת בָּהֶם:


הזית השלישי – when he harvests the third time, they don’t ripen sufficiently all the way, for they are branches that are under the roof where the sun does not reach them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:4:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:4:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:4:15)


**עוֹטְנוֹ.** לְשׁוֹן מַעֲטָן שֶׁל זֵיתִים, שֶׁהִיא הַגֻּמָּא שֶׁמַּנִּיחִים בָּהּ הַזֵּיתִים כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּתְעַפְּשׁוּ שָׁם. וּלְשׁוֹן מִקְרָא הוּא, עֲטִינָיו מָלְאוּ חָלָב (איוב כא):


עוטנו (packs in an a vat preparatory to crushing) – the language of vat or pit where olives ar e packed until they form a viscid mass, and it is the indentation/hole where they place the olives in order that they can grow moldy/decay there, and the language is Biblical (Job 21:24): “His pails are full of milk; [the marrow of his bones is juicy].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:4:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:4:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:4:16)


**שֶׁיִּלְקֶה.** שֶׁיִּתְעַפֵּשׁ:


שילקה – that it will grow moldy/decay.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:4:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:4:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:4:17)


**וּמְנַגְּבוֹ בְרֹאשׁ הַגַּג.** דְּמִתּוֹךְ שֶׁהוּא צָבוּר וּמְכֻנָּס בְּמָקוֹם אֶחָד אַרְבָּעָה וַחֲמִשָּׁה יָמִים זָב מֵאֵלָיו מֹהַל שֶׁאֵינוֹ יָפֶה, לְפִיכָךְ צָרִיךְ לְנַגְּבוֹ:


ומנגבו בראש הגג – because it is collected/piled up and gathered in one place for four or five days, it (i.e., the oil) flows on its own, there is a thin secretion and it is not pleasant, therefore it is necessary to dry them off.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:5:1)


**שָׁוִין.** לָאו לְכָל מִלֵּי שָׁוִין, דְּהָא בְּכֻלְּהוּ תְּנַן הָרִאשׁוֹן לַמְּנוֹרָה וְהַשְּׁאָר לַמְּנָחוֹת, אֶלָּא שָׁוִין לַמְּנָחוֹת קָאָמַר. וּמִשּׁוּם דְּקַיְמָא לָן בְּכָל מָקוֹם מִבְחַר נְדָרֶיךָ, שֶׁיָּבִיא מִן הַמֻּבְחָר, קָא מַשְׁמַע לָן הַשְׁתָּא שֶׁהָרִאשׁוֹן שֶׁבַּשֵּׁנִי וְהַשֵּׁנִי שֶׁבָּרִאשׁוֹן שָׁוִין. שֶׁאִם יֵשׁ לוֹ מִנְחָה לְהָבִיא וְיֵשׁ לוֹ מִשְּׁנֵיהֶם, מֵאֵיזֶה שֶׁיִּרְצֶה יָבִיא. אֲבָל אִם יֵשׁ לוֹ מִשְּׁלִישִׁי שֶׁבָּרִאשׁוֹן וּמֵרִאשׁוֹן שֶׁבַּשֵּׁנִי, יָבִיא מִנְחָתוֹ מֵרִאשׁוֹן שֶׁבַּשֵּׁנִי שֶׁהוּא מֻבְחָר:



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:6:1)


**קְרוּתִים וְהַטּוּלִים.** שֵׁם מְקוֹמוֹת הֵם:


קרותים והטולים – they are the names of places (in Judea; in the actual Talmud text of Talmud Menahot 86b, they are called קרוחים ועטולין and some have a reading of חטולין for the second location).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:6:2)


**אִלְיוּסְטָן.** יַיִן מָתוֹק מֵחֲמַת הַשֶּׁמֶשׁ, שֶׁתָּלוּ הָעֲנָבִים בַּשֶּׁמֶשׁ לְמַתְּקָן. שֶׁמֶשׁ בִּלְשׁוֹן יָוָן יוּסְטָן:


אליוסטן – sweet wine on account of the sun, for the grapes were suspended in the sun to sweeten them. Sun in the Greek language is יוסטן/Yostan [but in the Mishnah itself, it is called אליסטון ; in the Talmud (ibid.), it is called הליסטיון].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:6:3)


**יַיִן יָשָׁן.** שֶׁעָבְרוּ עָלָיו שְׁנֵים עָשָׂר חֹדֶשׁ, עוֹבֵר אַדְמִימוּתוֹ, וְהַכָּתוּב אוֹמֵר (משלי כג) אַל תֵּרֶא יַיִן כִּי יִתְאַדָּם, אַלְמָא בִּשְׁעַת אַדְמִימוּתוֹ מֻבְחָר הוּא:


יין ישן – that twelve months have passed upon it, its redness/reddish color passes, and in Sceripture it states (Proverbs 23:31): “Do not ogle that red wine [as it lends its color to the cup],” so we see, at the time that it is reddish, it is choice [wine].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:6:4)


**לֹא מָתוֹק.** כְּשֶׁהוּא מָתוֹק מֵחֲמַת עַצְמוֹ. דְּאִלּוּ מֵחֲמַת שֶׁמֶשׁ, הָא תָּנָא רֵישָׁא אִלְיוּסְטָן אִם הֵבִיא כָּשֵׁר. פֵּרוּשׁ אַחֵר, תִּירוֹשׁ שֶׁלֹּא עָבְרוּ עָלָיו אַרְבָּעִים יוֹם. וְרִאשׁוֹן עִקָּר:


לא מתוק – when it is sweet on account of itself, for if it were on account of the sun, the first clause [of the Mishnah] would teach that if he brought אליוסטן /Elyostan, it would be kosher/fit (whereas it is only the de facto position where it is kosher according to the earlier passage in the Mishnah). Another explanation: wine that did not pass over it forty days, but the first [explanation] is essential.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:6:5)


**מִן הַדָּלִיּוֹת.** מִן הַגְּפָנִים הַמֻּדְּלוֹת עַל גַּבֵּי כְּלוֹנְסוֹת וְקָנִים גְּבוֹהִים מִן הָאָרֶץ:


מן הדליות (from branches of the vine trained to an espalier) – from the grape-vines that are suspended on top of beams of the loom and reeds that are high off the ground.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:6:6)


**אֶלָּא מִן הָרוֹגְלִיּוֹת.** מִגְּפָנִים שֶׁשּׁוֹכְבוֹת עַל גַּבֵּי קַרְקַע בֵּין רַגְלֵי בְּנֵי הָאָדָם, שֶׁאֵינָן מֻדְּלוֹת:


אלא מן הרגליות (grapes growing in a row on isolated vines) – from grape-vines that lie on the ground between the feet of people that are not trained to an espalier.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:6:7)


**הָעֲבוּדִים.** שֶׁנֶּעֶבְדוּ שְׁתֵּי פְּעָמִים בַּשָּׁנָה. שֶׁחוֹפְרִים סְבִיבוֹת הַגְּפָנִים לְהַפֵּךְ בַּקַּרְקַע שֶׁבְּשָׁרְשֵׁיהֶן וְעוֹשִׂין בָּהֶן גֻּמּוֹת לְהַשְׁקוֹתָן, וְזוֹ הִיא עֲבוֹדָתָן:


העבודים – that are tended to twice a year. That they did around the grape-vines to turn over the ground soil that are in their roots and make indentations/holes there to water them, and this is their being tended to.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:7:1)


**חֲצָבִים גְּדוֹלִים.** שֶׁהַכֵּלִים הַגְּדוֹלִים פּוֹגְמִים טַעַם הַיַּיִן:


בחצבים גדולים (large stone pitchers/earthenware jugs) – that these large utensils damage the flavor of the wine.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:7:2)


**כְּדֵי שֶׁיְּהֵא רֵיחוֹ נוֹדֵף.** כְּשֶׁהֶחָבִית מְלֵאָה, יוֹצֵא הָרֵיחַ לַחוּץ וְאֵינוֹ נוֹדֵף:


כדי שיהא ריחו נודף – when the wine jug/vessel is full, the smell/odor goes outside and does not spread.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:7:3)


**קְמָחִין.** כְּמִין גַּרְגְּרִים דַּקִּים לְבָנִים שֶׁעוֹלִים עַל פְּנֵי הַיַּיִן דּוֹמִין לְקֶמַח:


קמחין – like small, thin, white berries that ascend over the face of the wine that are similar to flour.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:7:4)


**וּמֵאֶמְצָעָהּ.** מֵשִׂים בַּרְזָא בְּאֶמְצַע הֶחָבִית:


ומאמצעה – he places a bung in the middle of the (earthen) wine jug .


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:7:5)


**קָנֶה.** אַמַּת הַמִּדָּה שֶׁהָיְתָה רְגִילָה לִהְיוֹת בְּיַד הַגִּזְבָּר:


קנה – the cubit of measurement was regularly in the hand of the treasurer.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:7:6)


**זָרַק אֶת הַגִּיד וְהִקִּישׁ בַּקָּנֶה.** כְּלוֹמַר כְּשֶׁזָּרַק הַיַּיִן הַגִּיד שֶׁל שְׁמָרִים, שֶׁמַּתְחִילִים הַשְּׁמָרִים לָצֵאת, הִקִּישׁ הַגִּזְבָּר בַּקָּנֶה שֶׁבְּיָדוֹ וְדָחָהוּ שֶׁלֹּא יִכָּנֵס בַּכְּלִי שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ הַיַּיִן. וְרַבּוֹתַי פֵּרְשׁוּ הִקִּישׁ בַּקָּנֶה שֶׁבְּיָדוֹ לִרְמֹז אֶל מוֹשֵׁךְ הַיַּיִן מִן הֶחָבִית שֶׁיִּסְתֹּם הֶחָבִית בַּבַּרְזָא, וְלֹא הָיָה אוֹמֵר לוֹ סְתֹם, לְפִי שֶׁהַדִּבּוּר קָשֶׁה לְיַיִן. וְהָכִי מְפָרֵשׁ לֵיהּ בַּגְּמָרָא:


זרק את הגיד והקיש בקנה – that is to say, when he tossed the wine, the froth of the sediment when the sediment begins to leave, the treasurer struck with a reed that was in his hand and pushed up away that he should not enter with a utensil that has wine in it. But my Rabbis/teachers explained that he struck with the reed that was in his hand to hint to the one pulling the wine from the (earthen) wine jug, that he should close the jug with a bung, and he would not say to him, ‘close it,” because the speech was difficult (i.e., injurious/bad) for the wine. And this is what it explains in the Gemara (Tractate Menahot 87a).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 8:7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/8:7:7)


**רַבִּי יוֹסֵי בְרַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר יַיִן שֶׁעָלוּ בוֹ קְמָחִין פָּסוּל.** וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי בְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה. וְכָל הָנֵי דְּאָמְרִינַן בְּמַתְנִיתִין שֶׁהֵם פְּסוּלִים, בֵּין בַּסֹּלֶת בֵּין בַּשֶּׁמֶן בֵּין בַּיַּיִן, אִם עָבַר וְהִקְדִּישָׁן מַכִּין אוֹתוֹ מַכּוֹת מַרְדּוּת מִדִּבְרֵיהֶם, כְּדִין הַמַּקְדִּישׁ בַּעַל מוּם לַמִּזְבֵּחַ שֶׁהוּא לוֹקֶה מִן הַתּוֹרָה, מִדִּכְתִיב בְּבַעַל מוּם לֹא תַקְרִיבוּ, וְאָמְרוּ בְּסִפְרָא, אֵין לֹא תַקְרִיבוּ אֶלָּא לֹא תַקְדִּישׁוּ. וּכְשֵׁם שֶׁהָיוּ מְבִיאִים הַסֹּלֶת וְהַיַּיִן וְהַשֶּׁמֶן מִמְּקוֹמוֹת מֻבְחָרִים יְדוּעִים כְּדִתְנַן בְּמַתְנִיתִין, כָּךְ הָיוּ מְבִיאִים הַקָּרְבָּנוֹת מִמְּקוֹמוֹת יְדוּעִים, אֵילִים מִמּוֹאָב, כְּבָשִׂים מֵחֶבְרוֹן, עֲגָלִים מִשָּׁרוֹן, גּוֹזָלוֹת דְּהַיְנוּ תּוֹרִים וּבְנֵי יוֹנָה מֵהַר הַמֶּלֶךְ:


רבי יוסי בר' יהודה אומר יין שעלו בו קמחין פסול – But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yosi b’Rabbi Yehuda. And all of those that we have stated in our Mishnah that are invalid, whether with choice flour, whether with oil or whether with wine, if he transgressed and sanctified them, we flog him with the floggings of rebelliousness according to the Rabbis, for it is like the law of someone who sanctifies [an animal] with a blemish to the altar, for he is flogged according to the Torah, as it is written regarding something with a blemish (Leviticus 22:20): “You shall not offer any that has a defect, [for it will not be accepted in your favor],” and they (i.e., the Rabbis) stated in the Sifra, that the words, לא תקריבו/you shall not offer, means nothing other than do not sanctify. And just as they would bring the choice flour and the wine and the oil from known choice places, as it is taught in our Mishnah, so they would bring the sacrifices from known places, rams from Moab, lambs from Hebron, calves from Sharon, and pigeons, that is turtle-doves and young pigeons from the Mount of the King.



## Chapter 9


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:1:1)


**שְׁתֵּי מִדּוֹת.** עִשָּׂרוֹן עִשָּׂרוֹן וַחֲצִי עִשָּׂרוֹן. שְׁתֵּי מִדּוֹת שֶׁל עִשָּׂרוֹן הָיוּ, אַחַת מוֹדְדִים אוֹתָהּ גְּדוּשָׁה, שֶׁהָיְתָה קְטַנָּה וְלֹא הָיְתָה מַחְזֶקֶת כְּשֶׁהִיא גְּדוּשָׁה אֶלָּא עִשָּׂרוֹן, כְּשִׁעוּר חֲבֶרְתָּהּ כְּשֶׁהִיא מְחוּקָה. דְּרַבִּי מֵאִיר גָּמַר מִקְּרָא דִּכְתִיב (במדבר כח) עִשָּׂרוֹן עִשָּׂרוֹן לַכֶּבֶשׂ הָאֶחָד, דִּשְׁתֵּי עֶשְׂרוֹנוֹת הָיוּ שָׁם, וְאִי שְׁתֵּיהֶן שָׁווֹת הַוְיָא לְהוּ מִדָּה אַחַת. אֶלָּא אַחַת מְחוּקָה וְאַחַת גְּדוּשָׁה. גְּדוּשָׁה שֶׁבָּהּ הָיָה מוֹדֵד לְכָל הַמְּנָחוֹת, מְחוּקָה שֶׁבָּהּ הָיָה מוֹדֵד לַחֲבִתֵּי כֹּהֵן גָּדוֹל, וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים, לֹא הָיָה שָׁם אֶלָּא עִשָּׂרוֹן אֶחָד, דִּכְתִיב (שם כט) וְעִשָּׂרוֹן אֶחָד לַכֶּבֶשׂ הָאֶחָד, וְאוֹתוֹ עִשָּׂרוֹן מָחוּק הָיָה, וּבוֹ הָיוּ מוֹדְדִים לְכָל הַמְּנָחוֹת. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


שתי מידות. עשרון עשרון וחצי עשרון (see Tractate Menahot, Chapter 9, Mishnah 5) – there were two measures of an Issaron/Omer (1/10th of an Ephah), one we measure it heaped/overflowing, that it was small and would not support anything other than an Issaron when it was heaped/overflowing, like the size/limit of its neighbor when it smooth/level. For Rabbi Meir learned it from Scripture as it is written (Numbers 28:29): “and one-tenth for each [of the seven lambs]/עשרון עשרון לכבש האחד [לשבעת הכבשים],” for there were two Issarons there, and if both of them were equivalent, it would be for them one measure. But one is smooth/level and the other is heaped/overflowing. That which is heaped/overflowing in it, he would measure for all of the meal-offerings, that which is smoothed/leveled in it, he would measure for the cakes of the High Priest, but the Sages state that there wasn’t there other than one Issaron, as it is written (Numbers 29:4): “and one-tenth for each of the seven lambsועשרון אחד לכבש האחד [לשבעת הכבשים] ,” and that this particular Issaron was smooth/level, and through it they would measure all of the meal-offerings. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:1:2)


**לֹא בְשֶׁל שְׁלֹשָׁה לְפַר.** לְמִנְחַת נְסָכִים שֶׁל פַּר דִּכְתִיב בֵּיהּ (שם כח) וּשְׁלֹשָׁה עֶשְׂרֹנִים לַפָּר הָאֶחָד, לֹא הָיוּ מוֹדְדִין בְּמִדָּה אַחַת שֶׁתְּהֵא מַחְזֶקֶת שְׁלֹשָׁה עֶשְׂרוֹנִים, שֶׁלֹּא הָיְתָה שָׁם מִדָּה גְּדוֹלָה מֵעִשָּׂרוֹן:
