לא בשל שלשה לפר – for the meal-offerings of libations of the bull as it is written concerning it (Numbers 28:12): “[As grain offering] for each bull: three-tenths of a measure [of choice flour with oil mixed in],” (and also Numbers 28:28 – though the text does not have ושלשה עשרנים but rather (שלשה עשרנים, they would not measure them with one measure for it holds three Esronim, for there was not a measure there that was larger than an Issaron.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:1:3)


**אֶלָּא מוֹדְדָן עֶשְׂרוֹנוֹת.** כָּל עִשָּׂרוֹן עִשָּׂרוֹן בִּפְנֵי עַצְמוֹ:


אלא מודדן עשרונות each and every Issaron on its own.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:1:4)


הָכִי גָּרְסִינַן **חֲצִי עִשָּׂרוֹן מֶה הָיָה מְשַׁמֵּשׁ, שֶׁבּוֹ הָיָה מוֹדֵד לַחֲבִתֵּי כֹהֵן גָּדוֹל.** וְהָכִי פֵּרוּשָׁא, לַחֲבִתֵּי כֹּהֵן גָּדוֹל, מֵבִיא מִבֵּיתוֹ עִשָּׂרוֹן שָׁלֵם, וְחוֹצֵהוּ בַּחֲצִי עִשָּׂרוֹן שֶׁבַּמִּקְדָּשׁ, וְלָשׁ כָּל חֲצִי עִשָּׂרוֹן בִּפְנֵי עַצְמוֹ, וְעוֹשֶׂה מִכָּל חֲצִי עִשָּׂרוֹן שֵׁשׁ חַלּוֹת, שֶׁהֵן לִשְׁנֵי חֲצָאֵי עִשָּׂרוֹן שְׁתֵּים עֶשְׂרֵה חַלּוֹת, וְאוֹפֶה כֻּלָּן בְּיַחַד, וְאַחַר כָּךְ מְחַלֵּק כָּל חַלָּה לִשְׁנַיִם וּמַקְרִיב שְׁנֵים עָשָׂר חֲצָאִין בַּבֹּקֶר, וּשְׁנֵים עָשָׂר חֲצָאִין בָּעֶרֶב. וְקֹדֶם שֶׁיַּקְרִיב פּוֹתֵת אוֹתָן לְפִתִּין כַּזַּיִת, וְכוֹפֵל כָּל פְּתִיתָה לִשְׁנַיִם, וְאֵינוֹ מַבְדִּיל. אֲבָל פְּתִיתַת כָּל שְׁאָר מְנָחוֹת אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁפִּתִּין שֶׁלָּהֶן כַּזַּיִת, כּוֹפֵל אוֹתָן לִשְׁנַיִם וּשְׁנַיִם לְאַרְבָּעָה וּמַבְדִּיל, כְּדִתְנַן לְעֵיל בְּפֶרֶק ו':


הכי גרסינן, חצי עשרון מה היה משמש, שבו היה מודד לחביתי כהן גדול – and this is the explanation, for the baked cakes of the High Priest, he brings from his home a complete Issaron and they split it with half on half of an Issaron that is in the Temple, and he kneads each half-Issaron on its own, and makes from each half-Issaron six Hallot/loaves, which are for the two halves of the Issaron, twelve Hallot/loaves, and he bakes all of them together and afterwards he divides each Hallah/loaf in half and sacrifices twelve halves in the morning and twelve halves in the evening, but prior to his offering them, he crumbles them to olive-size piece, and doubles each crumble to two, and doesn’t separate them. But the opening of all of the rest of the meal-offerings even though their small pieces are like an olive, he doubles them into two and two into four and separates them as it is taught in the Mishnah (Tractate Menahot) chapter Six (Mishnah 4 – see also Chapter 4, Mishnah 5).



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:2:1)


**שְׁנָתוֹת הָיוּ בַהִין.** לֹא הָיָה שָׁם אֶלָּא הִין, וּבוֹ הָיוּ סִימָנִים מַסְמְרוֹת אוֹ פְּגִימוֹת:


שנתות היו בהין (notches/teeth-like marks – the Hin (12 logs or 72 egg-bulks) measure in the Temple had marks – see Tractate Menahot 87b) – there wasn’t there anything but a Hin, and in it there were signs of nails or notches.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:2:2)


**עַד כָּאן לְפַר.** חֲצִי הַהִין:


עד כאן לפר – one-half of a Hin.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:2:3)


**עַד כָּאן לְאַיִל.** שְׁלִישִׁית הַהִין:


עד כאן לאיל – one-third of a Hin.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:2:4)


**וְעַד כָּאן לְכֶבֶשׂ.** רְבִיעִית הַהִין. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר:


ועד כאן לכבש – one-quarter of a Hin. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Eliezer.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:2:5)


**וְכִי מֶה הָיָה הַהִין מְשַׁמֵּשׁ.** שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה בַּמִּקְדָּשׁ דָּבָר שֶׁיִּהְיֶה צָרִיךְ לְהִין שָׁלֵם, שֶׁלְּעוֹלָם לֹא נִצְטָרְכוּ לַהִין אֶלָּא בְּשֶׁמֶן הַמִּשְׁחָה בִּימֵי מֹשֶׁה וְאוֹתוֹ שֶׁמֶן עֲדַיִן הוּא קַיָּם וְעוֹמֵד, וְאִם כֵּן לֹא הָיוּ צְרִיכִים לַהִין:


וכי מה היה ההין משמש – that there wasn’t in the Temple a thing that required a complete Hin, for after all, they had no need for the Hin other than for the anointing oil (see Exodus 30:24) in the days of Moses and that kind of oil still exists, and if so, they had no need for a Hin [measurement].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:2:6)


**אֶלָּא מִדָּה יְתֵרָה.** הָיְתָה שָׁם לְהַשְׁלִים הַשֶּׁבַע מִדּוֹת:


אלא מדה יתירה (but there was a further measure) – it was there to complete the seven measurements.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:2:7)


**לְמִנְחַת כֹּהֵן גָּדוֹל.** הָיוּ לָהּ שְׁלֹשָׁה לֻגִּין שֶׁמֶן, לֹג וּמֶחֱצָה שַׁחֲרִית וְלֹג וּמֶחֱצָה בֵּין הָעַרְבַּיִם:


למנחת כהן גדול – there were for it three LOG of oil, a LOG and a half in the morning and a LOG and a half in the evening.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:3:1)


**רְבִיעִית מַיִם לִמְצֹרָע.** דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא יד) וְשָׁחַט אֶת הַצִּפּוֹר הָאֶחָת אֶל כְּלִי חֶרֶשׂ עַל מַיִם חַיִּים, לְמַיִם שֶׁדַּם הַצִּפּוֹר נִרְאֶה בָּהֶם, וְשִׁעֲרוּ חֲכָמִים רְבִיעִית הַלֹּג:


רביעית מים למצורע – as it is written (Leviticus 14:5): “[The priest shall order] one of the birds slaughtered over fresh water in an earthen vessel,” into water that the blood of the bird shall be seen in them, and the Sages estimated one-quarter of a LOG.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:3:2)


**רְבִיעִית שֶׁמֶן לְנָזִיר.** לְלֶחֶם נְזִירוּת. וּרְבִיעִית לֹא קַדִּישׁ לִהְיוֹת כְּלִי שָׁרֵת מִשּׁוּם רְבִיעִית מַיִם שֶׁל מְצֹרָע, דְּהָא חוּץ הוּא. וְלֹא מִשּׁוּם לֶחֶם שֶׁל נָזִיר, דְּאֵין לֶחֶם שֶׁל נָזִיר קָדוֹשׁ אֶלָּא בִּשְׁחִיטַת הַזֶּבַח. אֶלָּא מִפְּנֵי שֶׁבָּהּ הָיָה מוֹדֵד לַחֲבִתֵּי כֹּהֵן גָּדוֹל רְבִיעִית שֶׁמֶן לְכָל חַלָּה וְחַלָּה, שֶׁהֵן שְׁתֵּים עֶשְׂרֵה חַלּוֹת וְנִסְכֵּיהֶן שְׁלֹשָׁה לֻגִּין שֶׁמֶן:


רביעית שמן לנזיר – for the bread of the Nazirite [when he completes his period of Naziriteship]. But the one-quarter [of a LOG] he diodes not sanctify to be in a service vessel because of the one-quarter [of a LOG] of water of the leper, for this is outside [the Temple courtyard]. But not because of the bread of the Nazirite, for there bread of a Nazirite is not holy other than with the slaughter of the sacrifice. But, because in he would measure for the baked cakes of the High Priest a quarter [of a LOG] of oil for each and every Hallah [loaf], which are twelve Hallah loaves and their libations are three LOG of oil (see also Tractate Menahot, Chapter 7, Mishnah 2).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:3:3)


**חֲצִי לֹג מַיִם לְסוֹטָה.** דִּכְתִיב (במדבר ה) וְלָקַח הַכֹּהֵן מַיִם קְדֹשִׁים בִּכְלִי חָרֶשׂ, חֲצִי לֹג מַיִם הָיָה מְמַלֵּא מִן הַכִּיּוֹר:


חצי לוג מים לסוטה – as it is written (Numbers 5:17): “The priest shall take sacral water in an earthen vessel,” half a LOT of mater he would fill from the basin (i.e., the laver for the priests in the Temple court).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:3:4)


**חֲצִי לֹג שֶׁמֶן לְתוֹדָה.** הֲלָכָה לְמֹשֶׁה מִסִּינַי, וְכֵן רְבִיעִית שֶׁל נָזִיר. וַחֲצִי לֹג נַמִּי לֹא מִשּׁוּם חֲצִי לֹג מַיִם שֶׁל סוֹטָה וַחֲצִי לֹג שֶׁמֶן שֶׁל תּוֹדָה הוּא דְּקָדוֹשׁ לִהְיוֹת כְּלִי שָׁרֵת. אֶלָּא מִפְּנֵי שֶׁבּוֹ מְחַלֵּק חֲצִי לֹג שֶׁמֶן לְכָל נֵר וְנֵר שֶׁל מְנוֹרָה:


חצי לוג שמן לתודה – It is a traditional interpretation of a written law, and similarly, a quarter [of a LOG] of the Nazirite. And the one-half LOG is also not because of the one-half LOG of water of the Sotah (i.e., suspected adulterous woman) and one-half of a LOG of oil of the thanksgiving offering is holy to be in the service utensils, but rather because that in it he separates one-half LOG of oil for each and every candle of the Menorah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:3:5)


**אֵין לָהּ אֶלָּא לֻגָּהּ.** לֹג אֶחָד לְכָל שִׁשִּׁים עִשָּׂרוֹן. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר בֶּן יַעֲקֹב:


אין לה אלא לוגה – one LOG for every sixty Issarons. But the Halakah is not according to Rabbi Eliezer ben Yaakov (see also Tractate Menahot, Chapter 12, Mishnah 4).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:3:6)


**שִׁשָּׁה לֻגִּים לְפַר.** דְּהַיְנוּ חֲצִי הַהִין, כְּדִכְתִיב (שם טו) בָּלוּל בַּשֶּׁמֶן חֲצִי הַהִין. וְאַרְבָּעָה לְאַיִל, דְּהַיְנוּ שְׁלִישִׁית הַהִין. וּשְׁלֹשָׁה לְכֶבֶשׂ, דְּהַיְנוּ רְבִיעִית הַהִין, שֶׁהַהִין שְׁנֵים עָשָׂר לֻגִּין:


ששה לוגים לפר – that is one-half of a Hin, as it is written (Numbers 15:9): “[three-tenths of a measure of choice flour] with half of a hin of oil mixed in.” And four Issaron for the ram, that is one-third of a Hin. And three Issaron for the lamb, that is one-quarter of a Hin, for the Hin is twelve LOG.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:3:7)


**מֵחֲצִי לֹג לְכָל נֵר.** שֶׁצָּרִיךְ שֶׁיִּתֵּן בָּהּ מִדָּתָהּ שֶׁתְּהֵא דּוֹלֶקֶת וְהוֹלֶכֶת מֵעֶרֶב עַד בֹּקֶר. וְשִׁעֲרוּ חֲכָמִים חֲצִי לֹג שֶׁמֶן לְלֵילֵי תְּקוּפַת טֵבֵת הָאֲרֻכִּים. וְכֵן הָיָה נוֹתֵן בְּכָל נֵר בְּכָל לַיְלָה. וְאִם כָּבְתָה, נִפְסְלָה אוֹתָהּ פְּתִילָה וְהַשֶּׁמֶן מִלְּהַדְלִיק בָּהֶן עוֹד, אֶלָּא מְסִירָן וְנוֹתֵן חֲצִי לֹג שֶׁמֶן אַחֵר וּפְתִילָה חֲדָשָׁה וּמַדְלִיק:


מחצי לוג לכל נר – that he must put in it its measure that it would continue burning from evening until morning, and the Sages estimated one and one-half LOG oil for the lengthy nights of the season of Tevet. And similarly, he would place in each candle on each night. But if it went out, that wick and the oil became disqualified from lighting with them further, but rather, he removes them and puts in another LOG of oil and a new wick and he kindles it.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:4:1)


**מְעָרְבִין נִסְכֵּי פָרִים.** מִנְחַת נִסְכֵּי פַּר בְּמִנְחַת נִסְכֵּי אַיִל. לְפִי שֶׁבְּלִילַת שְׁתֵּיהֶן שָׁוָה, שְׁנֵי לֻגִּים לְעִשָּׂרוֹן. דְּהָא תְּנַן לְעֵיל שִׁשָּׁה לֻגִּים לַפָּר, וְסֹלֶת הָוֵי לְהוּ שְׁלֹשָׁה עֶשְׂרוֹנִים כְּדִכְתִיב (שם) וְהִקְרִיב עַל בֶּן הַבָּקָר מִנְחָה סֹלֶת שְׁלֹשָׁה עֶשְׂרֹנִים בָּלוּל בַּשֶּׁמֶן חֲצִי הַהִין, דְּהַיְנוּ שִׁשָּׁה לֻגִּין. וְלָאַיִל אַרְבָּעָה לֻגִּין, וְסֹלֶת שְׁנֵי עֶשְׂרוֹנִים לָאַיִל:


מערבין נסכי פרים – the meal-offering of libations of the bull with the meal-offering of the libations of the ram. Because the mixing of both of them is equivalent, two LOGS for an Issaron, for it is taught in the Mishnah (Chapter 9, Mishnah 3 of Tractate Menahot): “six LOG for the bullock,” and choice flour of three Esronim that would be for them as it is written (Numbers 15:9): “there shall be offered as a grain offering along with the animal: three-tenths of a measure of choice flour with half a hin of oil mixed in,” which is six LOG. And for the ram, four LOGIM and choice flour of two Esronim.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:4:2)


**נִסְכֵּי כְבָשִׂים.** שְׁלֹשָׁה לֻגִּין לְעִשָּׂרוֹן, דִּכְתִיב עִשָּׂרוֹן אֶחָד לַכֶּבֶשׂ הָאֶחָד, וּכְתִיב בָּלוּל בְּשֶׁמֶן כָּתִית רְבִיעִית הַהִין, דְּהַיְנוּ שְׁלֹשָׁה לֻגִּין:


נכסי כבשים – three LOGS for the Issaron, as it is written (Numbers 29:4): “and one-tenth for each [of the seven lambs],” and it is written (Exodus 29:40): “[there shall be a tenth of a measure of choice flour] with a quarter of a hin of beaten oil mixed in,” which are three LOGS.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:4:3)


**וְשֶׁל הַיּוֹם בְּשֶׁל אָמֶשׁ.** אִם הֵבִיא אֶמֶשׁ זִבְחוֹ בְּלֹא נְסָכִים. דְּקַיְמָא לָן אָדָם מֵבִיא זִבְחוֹ הַיּוֹם וּנְסָכָיו מִכָּאן וְעַד עֲשָׂרָה יָמִים. וְאִם הַיּוֹם הֵבִיא קָרְבָּן אֶחָד וּשְׁנֵי נְסָכִים עִמּוֹ, אֶחָד בִּשְׁבִילוֹ וְאֶחָד בִּשְׁבִיל שֶׁל אֶמֶשׁ, מְעָרְבִין יַחַד אִם הַקָּרְבָּנוֹת שָׁוִין, שֶׁהָיוּ שְׁנֵיהֶם כְּבָשִׂים אוֹ אֵילִים [אוֹ פָּרִים] אוֹ פַּר וְאַיִל:


ושל היום בשל אמש – if he brought yesterday’s sacrifice without libations. For we hold that a person who brings his sacrifice today and his libations from now until ten days hence. But if he brought today one sacrifice and two libations with him, one for himself and one for yesterday, they mixed them together if the sacrifices are equivalent, that both were lambs or rams [or bulls] or a bull and a ram.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:4:4)


**אֲבָל אֵין מְעָרְבִים נִסְכֵּי כְבָשִׂים בְּנִסְכֵּי פָרִים וְאֵילִים.** לְפִי שֶׁמִּנְחַת פַּר וְאַיִל חֲרֵבָה הִיא לְגַבֵּי כְּבָשִׂים, וּבוֹלַעַת הֵימֶנָּה, וְנִמְצֵאת שֶׁל כֶּבֶשׂ חֲסֵרָה וְזוֹ יְתֵרָה:


אבל אין מערבים נסכי כבשים בנסכי פרים ואילים – because the meal-offering of the bull and the ram is dried up/destroyed in connection with the lambs and it absorbs from it, and it is found that the lamb is less and that one is greater.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:4:5)


**וְאִם בְּלָלָן.** שֶׁכְּבָר הָלְכָה מִצְוַת שַׁמְנָן:


ואם בללן – that already the commandment of their oil has gone.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:4:6)


**כְּשֵׁרוֹת.** כְּרַבָּנָן דִּפְלִיגֵי עֲלֵיהּ דְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה בְּהַקּוֹמֵץ רַבָּה וְאָמְרֵי חָרֵב שֶׁנִּתְעָרֵב בְּבָלוּל יִקְרַב:


כשרות – according to the Rabbis who dispute with Rabbi Yehuda in the chapter, the Great Handful in Tractate Menahot (Chapter 3, Mishnah 2) and they state that what is burned that became mixed with that which is became combined with something mixed can be sacrificed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:4:7)


**וְאִם עַד שֶׁלֹּא בְלָלָן.** נִתְעָרְבוּ:


ואם עד שלא בללן – when they were combined.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:4:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:4:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:4:8)


**פְּסוּלוֹת.** דְּבָעִינַן רָאוּי לְבִילָה, וְלֵיכָּא, דְּחָסְרָא לַהּ שֶׁל כֶּבֶשׂ, וְשֶׁל אַיִל יְתֵרָה:


פסולות – for we require that wherever [from the proportions and properties of the mixture a perfect fusion is possible/appropriate, but it is lacking, for it is missing that of the extra lamb and ram.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:4:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:4:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:4:9)


**אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁמִּנְחָתוֹ כְפוּלָה.** כְּדִכְתִיב בֶּאֱמֹר אֶל הַכֹּהֲנִים, וּמִנְחָתוֹ שְׁנֵי עֶשְׂרוֹנִים:


אע"פ שמנחתו כפולה – as it is written in [the Torah portion of] “Speak to the Kohanim”/Emor (Leviticus 23:13): “The grain offering with it shall be two-tenths of a measure of choice flour with oil mixed in.”



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:5:1)


**שֶׁהָיָה גוֹדְשָׁהּ בְּתוֹכָהּ.** כְּשֶׁהָיָה מָחוּק הָיָה מַחֲזִיק כִּשְׁאָר עִשָּׂרוֹן גָּדוּשׁ. וּמַתְנִיתִין רַבִּי מֵאִיר הִיא דְּאָמַר בְּרֵישׁ פִּרְקִין עִשָּׂרוֹן עִשָּׂרוֹן הָיָה בַּמִּקְדָּשׁ, אֶחָד מָחוּק וְאֶחָד גָּדוּשׁ. וְלֵית הִלְכָתָא כְּוָתֵיהּ:


שהיה גודשה בתוכה – when there was an instrument for levelling a measure of grain/ a strike, it would hold like the rest a heaped up Issaron. But our Mishnah is according to Rabbi Meir who stated at the beginning of the chapter (Mishnah 1) that was an Issaron and an Issaron (i.e., two different forms of the Issaron) in the Temple, one that was leveled and one that was heaped. But the Halakha is not according to him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:5:2)


**בֵּרוּצֵיהֶן.** גָּדְשָׁן. דְּלַח נַמִּי אִיכָּא גֹּדֶשׁ פּוּרְתָא:


בירוציהן – their heaping, for the moist also has a bit of a heaping.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:5:3)


**מִדּוֹת הַלַּח בֵּרוּצֵיהֶן קֹדֶשׁ וּמִדּוֹת הַיָּבֵשׁ בֵּרוּצֵיהֶן חֹל.** בַּגְּמָרָא מְפָרֵשׁ דְּהַאי תַּנָּא סָבַר מִדּוֹת הַלַּח נִמְשְׁחוּ בֵּין מִבִּפְנִים בֵּין מִבַּחוּץ, הִלְכָּךְ שְׂפַת הַכְּלִי מְקַדְּשָׁן לַבֵּרוּצִין. מִדּוֹת הַיָּבֵשׁ נִמְשְׁחוּ מִבִּפְנִים וְלֹא נִמְשְׁחוּ מִבַּחוּץ, הִלְכָּךְ בֵּרוּצִין שֶׁאֵינָן נוֹגְעִים מִבִּפְנִים בִּמְקוֹם מְשִׁיחָתָן לֹא קַדְּשֵׁי:


מדות הלח בירוציהן קדש ומדות היבש בירוציהן חול – in the Gemara (Tractate Menahot 90a) it explains that this Tanna held that the measurements of that which is liquid were anointed whether from the inside or from the outside, therefore the rim of the utensil sanctifies them to be heaped up. The measurements of that which is dry were anointed from inside, but were not anointed from the outside, therefore their heaping did not touch from the inside in the place of their measurement it was not sanctified.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:5:4)


**רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא אוֹמֵר מִדַּת הַלַּח קֹדֶשׁ.** דְּסָבַר מִדּוֹת הַלַּח נִמְשְׁחוּ בֵּין מִבִּפְנִים בֵּין מִבַּחוּץ:


ר"ע אומר מדת הלח קודש – since he holds that the liquid measurements are anointed whether from the inside or whether from the outside.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:5:5)


**מִדּוֹת הַיָּבֵשׁ חֹל.** דְּלֹא נִמְשְׁחוּ כָּל עִקָּר. וּמִיהוּ מַה שֶּׁמּוֹדְדִים בָּהֶן קָדוֹשׁ קְדֻשַּׁת הַפֶּה. וְגַבְרָא לְמַאי דְּצָרִיךְ מְקַדֵּשׁ בַּפֶּה, בֵּרוּצִין לֹא מְקַדֵּשׁ לְהוּ דְּלֹא צְרִיכֵי לֵיהּ:


מדות היבש חול – they were not anointed at all. However, what they measured of them was sanctified by the sanctify of the mouth (i.e., through the speech of mankind), but what did a person need anointing by mouth? When heaped up, he did not sanctify them for they did not require it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:5:6)


**רַבִּי יוֹסֵי אוֹמֵר לֹא מִשּׁוּם זֶה.** רַבִּי יוֹסֵי סָבַר אִידִי וְאִידִי נִמְשְׁחוּ מִבִּפְנִים וְלֹא נִמְשְׁחוּ מִבַּחוּץ. וְהָכָא הַיְנוּ טַעֲמָא מִשּׁוּם דְּלַח נֶעְכָּר, מַה שֶּׁבְּשׁוּלֵי הַכְּלִי כְּשֶׁמּוֹסִיפִין עָלָיו נֶעְכָּר וּמִתְעָרֵב וְנִבְלָל וְעוֹלֶה מִלְּמַעְלָה, וְנִמְצָא שֶׁכְּבָר קָדְשׁוּ הַבֵּרוּצִין בְּתוֹךְ הַכְּלִי:


רבי יוסי אומר לא משום זה – Rabbi Yosi held that both these and those (i.e., dry and liquid) were anointed from the inside but they were not anointed from the outside. But here is the reason, because the liquid is stirred up, that which is on the rim at the bottom of the vessel when they add to it is stirred and combined and mixed and goes up from above, and it is found that that which is heaped has already been sanctified within the utensil.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:5:7)


**וְהַיָּבֵשׁ אֵינוֹ נֶעְכָּר.** אֶלָּא בִּמְקוֹמוֹ עוֹמֵד, הִלְכָּךְ מַה שֶּׁבִּפְנִים קָדוֹשׁ וּמַה שֶּׁבַּחוּץ אֵינוֹ קָדוֹשׁ:


והיבש אינו נעכר – but rather it stands in its place, therefore, what is inside is holy and that which is outside is not holy.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:6:1)


**חוּץ מִן הַבְּכוֹר וְהַמַּעֲשֵׂר וְהַפֶּסַח וְהַחַטָּאת וְהָאָשָׁם.** מִשּׁוּם דִּכְתִיב בְּפָרָשַׁת נְסָכִים (במדבר טו) לְפַלֵּא נֶדֶר אוֹ בִנְדָבָה. בָּא בְּנֶדֶר וּבִנְדָבָה, טָעוּן נְסָכִים, יָצְאוּ בְּכוֹר וּמַעֲשֵׂר וּפֶסַח וְחַטָּאת וְאָשָׁם שֶׁהֵן בָּאִים חוֹבָה לֹא לִנְדָבָה, שֶׁאֵין טְעוּנִים נְסָכִים. יָכוֹל אַף חוֹבוֹת הַבָּאוֹת מֵחֲמַת הָרֶגֶל בָּרֶגֶל כְּגוֹן עוֹלוֹת רְאִיָּה וְשַׁלְמֵי חֲגִיגָה לֹא יִהְיוּ טְעוּנוֹת נְסָכִים, תַּלְמוּד לוֹמַר אוֹ בְּמֹעֲדֵיכֶם, כָּל הַבָּא בְּמוֹעֲדֵיכֶם טָעוּן נְסָכִים. וּשְׂעִירֵי חַטָּאת שֶׁבָּאִים חוֹבָה לָרֶגֶל אֵין טְעוּנִים נְסָכִים, דִּכְתִיב בְּפָרָשַׁת נְסָכִים וְכִי תַעֲשֶׂה בֶּן בָּקָר, בֶּן בָּקָר בַּכְּלָל הָיָה, בִּכְלַל וַעֲשִׂיתֶם אִשֶּׁה, דְּמַשְׁמַע כָּל אִשֶּׁה טָעוּן נְסָכִים חוּץ [מֵאִשֶּׁה] שֶׁמִּעֵט, וְלָמָּה יָצָא, לְהָקִישׁ אֵלָיו, מַה בֶּן בָּקָר מְיֻחָד שֶׁבָּא בְּנֶדֶר וּנְדָבָה. אַף כָּל בָּא בְּנֶדֶר וּנְדָבָה, יָצְאוּ שְׂעִירֵי הָרְגָלִים שֶׁהֵן בָּאוֹת חַטָּאוֹת, שֶׁאֵין חַטָּאת בָּא בְּנֶדֶר וּנְדָבָה, שֶׁאֵין טְעוּנוֹת נְסָכִים:


חוץ מן הבכור והמעשר והפסח והחטאת והאשם – because it is written in the [Torah] portion of the libations (Numbers 15:3): “[and would present an offering by fire to the LORD from the herd or from the flock, be it a burnt-offering or sacrifice,] in fulfillment of a vow explicitly uttered, or as a freewill offering,” when it comes in the form of a vow or a freewill offering it requires libations, excluding the firstling and the tithe and the Passover offering and the sin-offering and guilt-offering that come as an obligation, not for a freewill offering, for they don’t require libations. One is even able [to bring] obligatory offerings that come on account of the Festival - on the Festival, as for example, the burnt offerings of appearance in the Temple/pilgrimage and the peace-offerings of the Festival do they would not require libations, the inference teaches us “or at your fixed occasions” (Numbers 15:3), anyone who comes on your fixed occasions is required to bring libations. But the goats of sin-offerings that come as a requirement for the Festival do not require libations, as it is written in the [Torah] portion of libations (Numbers 15:8): “And if it is an animal from the herd that you offer to the LORD as a burnt offering or as a sacrifice,” an animal from the herd was included, within the rule of (Numbers 15:3): “and would present an offering by fire to the LORD from the herd or from the flock,” that implies that all offerings by fire require libations except for [fire-offerings] that he (i.e., God) excluded, and why was it excluded? To make an analogy to it, that just as an animal from the herd is unique that comes as a vow or as a freewill offering, so also everything that comes as a vow or as a freewill offering, excluding the goats of the Festivals that come as sin-offerings, for the sin-offering does not come as a vow or as a freewill offering, for they don’t require libations (see Tractate Menahot 90b).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:6:2)


**חַטָּאתוֹ וַאֲשָׁמוֹ שֶׁל מְצֹרָע טְעוּנִים נְסָכִים.** לְפִי שֶׁאֵינָן בָּאִים עַל חֵטְא כִּשְׁאָר חַטָּאוֹת וַאֲשָׁמוֹת. וְחַטַּאת נָזִיר אֵינָהּ טְעוּנָה נְסָכִים, מִשּׁוּם דְּנָזִיר חוֹטֵא הוּא כְּדִכְתִיב (במדבר ו) מֵאֲשֶׁר חָטָא עַל הַנָּפֶשׁ, שֶׁצִּעֵר עַצְמוֹ מִן הַיַּיִן:


חטאתו ואשמו של מצורע טעונים נסכים – because they do not come for a sin-offering like other sin-offerings and guilt-offerings. For the sin-offering of a Nazirite does not require libations because the Nazirite [himself] is a sinner, as it is written (Numbers 6:11): “and make expiation for the guilt on his behalf for the guilt he incurred/מאשר חטא על –הנפש” – because he denied/restrained himself from the enjoyment of wine.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:7:1)


**פַּר הַבָּא עַל כָּל הַמִּצְוֹת.** עַל אַחַת מִכָּל הַמִּצְוֹת. כְּגוֹן הוֹרוּ בֵּית דִּין שֶׁחֵלֶב מֻתָּר. וְזֶהוּ פַּר הֶעְלֵם דָּבָר שֶׁל צִבּוּר שֶׁבַּמִּקְרָא, וּסְמִיכָה כְּתִיבָא בֵּיהּ (ויקרא ד) וְסָמְכוּ זִקְנֵי הָעֵדָה אֶת יְדֵיהֶם עַל רֹאשׁ הַפָּר, וּשְׁלֹשָׁה מִזִּקְנֵי בֵּית דִּין הָיוּ סוֹמְכִין עָלָיו:


פר הבא על כל המצות – on one of all of the commandments, as for example, the Jewish court/Bet Din errantly ruled that forbidden fat is permitted, and this is bull brought for an unwitting communal sin [because of an active unwitting transgression committed by the Jewish people, as a result of an erroneous halakhic decision handed down by the Great Sanhedrin], that is in Scripture, and placing of the hands [on the head of the animal] is written concerning it (Leviticus 4:15): “The elders of the community shall lay their hands upon the head of the bull” and three from the elders of the Jewish court would lay their hands [upon the head of the bull].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:7:2)


**וְשָׂעִיר הַמִּשְׁתַּלֵּחַ.** לַעֲזָאזֵל, כְּתִיב בֵּיהּ (שם טז) וְסָמַךְ אַהֲרֹן אֶת שְׁתֵּי יָדָיו עַל רֹאשׁ הַשָּׂעִיר הַחַי:


ושעיר המשתלח – to Azazel, as it is written concerning it (Leviticus 16:21): “Aaron shall lay both his hands upon the head of the live goat [and confess over it all the iniquities and transgressions of the Israelites].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:7:3)


**שְׂעִירֵי עֲבוֹדָה זָרָה.** דִּכְתִיב בְּפָרָשַׁת שְׁלַח לְךָ וְכִי תִשְׁגּוּ כוּ'. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


שעירי עבודה זרה – as it is written in the [Torah] portion of Shelah Lekha (Numbers 15:22): “If you unwittingly fail [to observe any one of the commandments that the LORD has declared to Moses.” But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:7:4)


**כָּל קָרְבְּנוֹת הַיָּחִיד טְעוּנִים סְמִיכָה.** דְּעִקַּר סְמִיכָה בְּקָרְבַּן יָחִיד הוּא דִּכְתִיבָא (שם ג) וְסָמַךְ יָדוֹ עַל רֹאשׁ קָרְבָּנוֹ:


כל קרבנות היחיד טעונים סמיכה – for the essence of the laying of the hands [on the head] of an individual sacrifice is written (Leviticus 3:2): “He shall lay his hand upon the head of his offering [and slaughter it at the entrance of the Tent of Meeting].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:7:5)


**הַיּוֹרֵשׁ סוֹמֵךְ.** אִם הִתְנַדֵּב אָבִיו בְּקָרְבַּן עוֹלָה וּשְׁלָמִים וּמֵת, בְּנוֹ סוֹמֵךְ עָלָיו:


היורש סומך – if his father had offered a freewill offering with a sacrifice of a burnt offering or a peace-offering and died, his son places his hands upon it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:7:6)


**וּמֵבִיא נְסָכִים.** שֶׁל קָרְבָּן:


ומביא נסכים – of he sacrifice.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:7:7)


**וּמֵמִיר.** אִם הֱמִירָהּ בִּבְהֵמָה אַחֶרֶת, תְּמוּרָתוֹ חָלָה עָלֶיהָ וּשְׁתֵּיהֶן קְדוֹשׁוֹת כְּאִלּוּ הֱמִירָהּ אָבִיו:


וממיר – if he made a substitution of another animal, his exchange takes effect upon it and both of them are holy, it is as if his father substituted it.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:8:1)


**חוּץ מֵחֵרֵשׁ שׁוֹטֶה וְקָטָן.** לְפִי שֶׁאֵין לָהֶם דַּעַת:


חוץ מחרש שוטה וקטן – because they lack knowledge.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:8:2)


**סוּמָא.** דִּכְתִיב בְּפַר הֶעְלֵם דָּבָר שֶׁל צִבּוּר וְסָמְכוּ זִקְנֵי הָעֵדָה, וְהֵם סַנְהֶדְרִי גְּדוֹלָה, וּבַסַּנְהֶדְרִין לֹא הָיָה בָּהֶן סוּמָא, כִּדְמוּכָח בְּמַסֶּכֶת סַנְהֶדְרִין, וְהוּא הַדִּין לְכָל שְׁאָר סְמִיכוֹת שֶׁאֵין סוּמָא יָכוֹל לִסְמֹךְ:


סומא – as it is written regarding the bull for an unwitting communal sin (Leviticus 4:15): “The elders of the community shall lay their hands [upon the head of the bull],” and they are the Great Sanhedrin, but in the Sanhedrin there was not among them anyone who was blind, as it is proved in Tractate Sanhedrin [36b], and the same law applies for the rest of the laying of the hands that a blind person is not able is not able to lay his hands [upon the head of the bull].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:8:3)


**וְנָכְרִי.** דִּכְתִיב דַּבֵּר אֶל בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְגוֹ', בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל סוֹמְכִין וְאֵין הַנָּכְרִים סוֹמְכִין:


ונכרי – as it is written (Leviticus 1:2): “Speak to the Israelite people,” the Israelite people lay their hands, but the heathens do not lay their hands [upon the head of a sacrifice].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:8:4)


**וְהָעֶבֶד וְהַשָּׁלִיחַ.** דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא א) וְסָמַךְ יָדוֹ, וְלֹא יַד עַבְדּוֹ וְלֹא יַד שְׁלוּחוֹ:


והעבד והשליח – as it is written (Leviticus 1:4): “He shall lay his hand [upon the head of the burnt offering, that it may be acceptable in his behalf], but not the hand of his servant and not the hand of his agent.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:8:5)


**וְהָאִשָּׁה.** בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל סוֹמְכִין, וְלֹא בְּנוֹת יִשְׂרָאֵל סוֹמְכוֹת:


והאשה – the children (i.e., male) of Israel lay their hands [upon the head of the sacrifice], but the daughters of Israel do not lay their hands [upon the head of the sacrifice].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:8:6)


**וּסְמִיכָה שְׁיָרֵי מִצְוָה.** דְּאֵינָהּ מְעַכֶּבֶת כַּפָּרָה. וּמִיהוּ מַעֲלֶה עָלָיו הַכָּתוּב כְּאִלּוּ לֹא כִּפֵּר:


וסמיכה שיירי מצוה – that it is not indispensable for atonement, but however, Scripture considers it for him as if he did not attain expiation.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:8:7)


**בִּשְׁתֵּי יָדָיִם.** דִּכְתִיב בַּשָּׂעִיר הַמִּשְׁתַּלֵּחַ (שם טז) וְסָמַךְ אַהֲרֹן אֶת שְׁתֵּי יָדָיו, זֶה בָּנָה אָב לְכָל הַסְּמִיכוֹת שֶׁיִּהְיוּ בִּשְׁתֵּי יָדַיִם:


בשתי ידים – as it is written concerning the scapegoat (Leviticus 16:21): “Aaron shall lay both his hands [upon the head of the live goat],” and this is an analogy for all the laying of the hands that it shall be with both (i.e., the two) hands.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:8:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:8:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:8:8)


**וּבִמְקוֹם שֶׁסּוֹמְכִים שׁוֹחֲטִים.** שֶׁאִם סָמַךְ חוּץ לָעֲזָרָה חוֹזֵר וְסוֹמֵךְ בָּעֲזָרָה בִּמְקוֹם שְׁחִיטָה:


ובמקום שסומכים שוחטים – that if he lay his hands [upon the head of the sacrifice] outside of the Temple courtyard and he returns and lays his hands in the Temple courtyard in the place of the ritual slaughter.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:8:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:8:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:8:9)


**וְתֵכֶף לַסְּמִיכָה שְׁחִיטָה.** דִּכְתִיב וְסָמַךְ וְשָׁחַט:


ותיכף לסמיכה שחיטה – as it is written (Leviticus 1:4-5): “He shall lay his hand upon the head of the burnt offering….the bull shall be slaughtered before the LORD.”



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:9:1)


**שֶׁאֶחָד מֵנִיף לְכָל הַחֲבֵרִים.** שֶׁנִּתְחַבְּרוּ לְהִתְנַדֵּב קָרְבָּן אֶחָד. שֶׁכָּל קָרְבְּנוֹת נְדָבָה יְכוֹלִים לְהָבִיא בְּשֻׁתָּפוּת, וְאֶחָד מֵנִיף עַל יְדֵי כֻּלָּן, אֲבָל כֻּלָּן יַחַד אֵין יְכוֹלִין לְהָנִיף, דְּהַוְיָא חֲצִיצָה בֵּין יָדוֹ שֶׁל זֶה וְלַקָּרְבָּן. וְכֹהֵן שֶׁמַּנִּיחַ יָדוֹ תַּחַת יַד הַבְּעָלִים וּמֵנִיף וְהַוְיָא יַד בְּעָלִים חֲצִיצָה, לֹא אִכְפַּת לָן, דְּהָא עִקַּר תְּנוּפָה בַּבְּעָלִים:


שאחד מניף לכל החברים – that associated together to make one freewill sacrifice. For all of he the freewill sacrifices one can bring in partnerships, and one waves on behalf of everyone, but all of them together are not able to wave for that would be an interposition/obstruction between the hand of this one and the sacrifice. But the Kohen places his hand underneath the owners and waves and hand of the owners would be an obstruction/interposition, it does not matter to us, for the essence of the waving is by the owners.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:9:2)


**בְּקָרְבְּנוֹת הַיָּחִיד.** כְּגוֹן חָזֶה וְשׁוֹק שֶׁל שְׁלָמִים:


בקרבנות היחיד – as for example, the breast and the thigh of peace-offerings.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:9:3)


**וּבְקָרְבְּנוֹת צִבּוּר.** כִּבְשֵׂי עֲצֶרֶת שֶׁטְּעוּנִים תְּנוּפָה חַיִּים וּשְׁחוּטִים:


ובקרבנות צבור – the lambs of Atzeret/Shavuot which require waving while alive and when slaughtered.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:9:4)


**בְּדָבָר שֶׁיֶּשׁ בּוֹ רוּחַ חַיִּים.** קָרְבַּן בְּהֵמָה:


בדבר שיש בו רוח חיים – the sacrifice of an animal.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 9:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/9:9:5)


**וּבְדָבָר שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ רוּחַ חַיִּים.** כְּגוֹן לַחְמֵי תּוֹדָה וְנָזִיר:


ובדבר שאין בו רוח חיים – as for example, the bread of thanksgiving offerings and the Nazirite offering.



## Chapter 10


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:1:1)


**רַבִּי יִשְׁמָעֵאל אוֹמֵר הָעֹמֶר הָיָה בָא בְשַׁבָּת.** כְּשֶׁחָל שִׁשָּׁה עָשָׂר בְּנִיסָן לִהְיוֹת בְּשַׁבָּת, הָעֹמֶר דּוֹחֶה אֶת הַשַּׁבָּת, שֶׁכָּל קָרְבָּן שֶׁזְּמַנּוֹ קָבוּעַ דּוֹחֶה אֶת הַשַּׁבָּת וְאֶת הַטֻּמְאָה. וְהָיָה הָעֹמֶר בָּא מִשָּׁלֹשׁ סְאִין, שֶׁהָיוּ קוֹצְרִין שָׁלֹשׁ סְאִין שְׂעֹרִין וּמְנִיפִין אוֹתָן בְּנָפָה עַד שֶׁמַּעֲמִידִין אוֹתָן עַל עִשָּׂרוֹן אֶחָד מֻבְחָר:


רבי ישמעאל אומר העומר היה בא בשבת – when the sixteenth of Nisan occurs on the Sabbath, the Omer supersedes the Sabbath, for any sacrifice whose time is fixed supersedes the Sabbath and ritual impurity. The Omer comes from three Seah, that they would reap three Seah of barley and wave it in a sieve until he places them upon on choice Issaron/tenth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:1:2)


**וּבְחֹל.** אִם חָל שִׁשָּׁה עָשָׂר בְּנִיסָן לִהְיוֹת בְּחֹל, הָיוּ קוֹצְרִין אוֹתוֹ חָמֵשׁ סְאִין, דְּסָבַר רַבִּי יִשְׁמָעֵאל עִשָּׂרוֹן מֻבְחָר בְּלֹא טִרְחָא שֶׁאֵינוֹ מְנַפֶּה אוֹתוֹ כָּל כָּךְ אָתֵי מֵחָמֵשׁ, בְּטִרְחָא כְּשֶׁמְּנַפֶּה אוֹתוֹ הַרְבֵּה אָתֵי מִשָּׁלֹשׁ. בְּחֹל מַיְתִינַן מֵחָמֵשׁ, שֶׁכָּךְ מְשֻׁבָּח הַדָּבָר יוֹתֵר שֶׁמְּנִיפִין מִכָּל סְאָה מְעַט אוֹתוֹ סֹלֶת דַּק הַיּוֹצֵא רִאשׁוֹן שֶׁהוּא מֻבְחָר, וּמוֹצִיאִין מֵחָמֵשׁ סְאִין עִשָּׂרוֹן. בְּשַׁבָּת מֵבִיא שָׁלֹשׁ, מוּטָב שֶׁיַּרְבֶּה בִּמְלָאכָה אַחַת שֶׁרוֹקֵד שָׁלֹשׁ סְאִים פְּעָמִים הַרְבֵּה, וְאַל יַרְבֶּה בִּמְלָאכוֹת הַרְבֵּה שֶׁיִּקְצֹר וְיִבְרֹר וְיִטְחֹן וִירַקֵּד שְׁנֵי סְאִין יוֹתֵר. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יִשְׁמָעֵאל:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:1:3)


**בִּשְׁלֹשָׁה בְּנֵי אָדָם וּבְשָׁלֹשׁ קֻפּוֹת וּבְשָׁלֹשׁ מַגָּלוֹת.** לְפַרְסֵם הַדָּבָר שֶׁקְּצִירַת הָעֹמֶר בְּמוֹצָאֵי חֲמִשָּׁה עָשָׂר בְּנִיסָן, מִפְּנֵי הַצְּדוֹקִים שֶׁהָיוּ אוֹמְרִים שֶׁאֵין הָעֹמֶר בָּא אֶלָּא בְּאֶחָד בְּשַׁבָּת:


בשלשה בני אדם ובשלש קופות ובשלש מגלות – to publicize the matter that the reaping of the Omer occurs on the aftermath of the fifteenth day of Nisan. Because the Sadducees would say that the Omer does not come other than on Sunday (i.e., the first day of the week).



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:2:1)


**מִצְוַת הָעֹמֶר לָבֹא מִן הַקָּרוֹב.** מִמָּקוֹם שֶׁקָּרוֹב לִירוּשָׁלַיִם, לְפִי שֶׁאֵין מַעֲבִירִין עַל הַמִּצְוֹת, הִלְכָּךְ כְּשֶׁיּוֹצֵא מִירוּשָׁלַיִם לְבַקֵּשׁ עֹמֶר, אוֹתָהּ תְּבוּאָה שֶׁמּוֹצֵא רִאשׁוֹנָה נוֹטְלָהּ:


מצות העומר לבוא מן הקרוב – from a place that is close to Jerusalem because they do forego the occasion for performing a religious commandment, therefore, when they go out from Jerusalem to search for the Omer, that grain that he finds first he takes it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:2:2)


**לֹא בִכֵּר.** לֹא בָּשַׁל כָּל צָרְכּוֹ:


לא ביכר – it didn’t ripen all of the way.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:2:3)


**מַעֲשֶׂה שֶׁבָּא מִגַּגּוֹת צְרִיפִין וְעֵין סוֹכֵר.** רְחוֹקִים מִירוּשָׁלַיִם הַרְבֵּה. מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהֶחֱרִיבוּ הַגְּיָסוֹת כָּל תְּבוּאוֹת שֶׁסְּבִיבוֹת יְרוּשָׁלַיִם:


מעשה שבא מגגות צריפין ועין סוכר – greatly distant from Jerusalem, because the ravaging [Roman] troos destroyed all the grain that was surrounding Jerusalem.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:3:1)


**כְּרִיכוֹת.** כּוֹרְכִים וְקוֹשְׁרִים רָאשֵׁי שִׁבֳּלִים מְלֹא אֶגְרוֹף:


כריכות – they wrap up and tie the tops of the spikes/ears of corn as large as a fist.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:3:2)


**מִתְכַּנְּסוֹת לְשָׁם.** לְמוֹצָאֵי יוֹם טוֹב כְּשֶׁקּוֹצְרִים אוֹתוֹ:


מתכנסות לשם – at the conclusion of the Holy Day when they cut it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:3:3)


**כְּדֵי שֶׁיְּהֵא נִקְצָר בְּעֵסֶק גָּדוֹל.** בְּקוֹל הֲמֻלָּה גְּדוֹלָה. שֶׁיַּכִּירוּ הַצְּדוֹקִים שֶׁבְּמוֹצָאֵי יוֹם טוֹב קוֹצְרִים אוֹתוֹ, מִשּׁוּם דְּאִינְהוּ לֹא מוֹדוּ בְּהַאי, כְּדִלְקַמָּן:


כדי נקצר בעסק גדול – with the great sound. That the Sadducees will know that the conclusion of the first Holy Day [of Passover] they reap it, because they did not admit to this, as will be seen further on.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:3:4)


**אָמַר לָהֶן.** הַקּוֹצֵר, לִבְנֵי הָעֲיָרוֹת הָעוֹמְדִים עָלָיו, בָּא הַשֶּׁמֶשׁ, וְאָמְרוּ לוֹ הֵין:


אמר להן – the reaper , to the people of the villages that are standing upon him: Did the sun set? And they respond to him: “Yes.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:3:5)


**מַגָּל זֶה.** כְּלוֹמַר, אֶקְצֹר הַתְּבוּאָה בְּמַגָּל זֶה:


מגל זה – meaning to say, shall I reap the grain with this scythe?


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:3:6)


**קֻפָּה זוֹ.** אַכְנִיס הַתְּבוּאָה לְתוֹךְ קֻפָּה זוֹ:


קופה זו – shall I put the grain into this basket?


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:3:7)


**שַׁבָּת זוֹ.** אֶקְצֹר בְּשַׁבָּת זוֹ, וְאָמְרוּ לוֹ הֵין:


שבת זו – shall I reap on this Sabbath? And they said to him: “Yes.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:3:8)


**שָׁלֹשׁ פְּעָמִים.** שׁוֹאֵל לָהֶן כָּל דָּבָר וְדָבָר:


שלש פעמים – he asks them about each and every matter.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:3:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:3:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:3:9)


**וְכָל כָּךְ.** שֶׁהָיָה שׁוֹאֵל, לָמָּה:


וכל כך – that he would ask. Why?


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:3:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:3:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:3:10)


**מִפְּנֵי הַצְּדוֹקִים וּבַיְתוֹסִים שֶׁהָיוּ אוֹמְרִים אֵין קְצִירַת הָעֹמֶר בְּמוֹצָאֵי יוֹם טוֹב.** אֶלָּא בְּמוֹצָאֵי שַׁבָּת, דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא כג) וּסְפַרְתֶּם לָכֶם מִמָּחֳרַת הַשַּׁבָּת, מִמָּחֳרַת שַׁבָּת בְּרֵאשִׁית מַשְׁמַע, וּמָסֹרֶת בְּיָדֵינוּ מֵאֲבוֹתֵינוּ דְּהַאי מִמָּחֳרַת הַשַּׁבָּת, הַיְנוּ מִמָּחֳרַת יוֹם טוֹב רִאשׁוֹן שֶׁל פֶּסַח, בֵּין שֶׁחָל בְּחֹל בֵּין בְּשַׁבָּת. וְכֵן מָצִינוּ בְּסֵפֶר יְהוֹשֻׁעַ (פרק ה) וַיֹּאכְלוּ מֵעֲבוּר הָאָרֶץ מִמָּחֳרַת הַפֶּסַח מַצּוֹת וְקָלִי, וַהֲרֵי נֶאֱמַר בַּתּוֹרָה (שם) וְלֶחֶם וְקָלִי וְכַרְמֶל לֹא תֹאכְלוּ עַד עֶצֶם הַיּוֹם הַזֶּה, וּמֵאַחַר שֶׁתָּלָה הֶתֵּר הֶחָדָשׁ בְּמָחֳרַת הַפֶּסַח, הַדָּבָר בָּרוּר שֶׁמָּחֳרַת הַפֶּסַח הוּא הַמַּתִּיר אֶת הֶחָדָשׁ, וּבוֹ קוֹצְרִים הָעֹמֶר, בֵּין שֶׁחָל יוֹם טוֹב רִאשׁוֹן שֶׁל פֶּסַח בְּחֹל בֵּין שֶׁחָל לִהְיוֹת בְּשַׁבָּת. וּלְפִיכָךְ הַקּוֹצְרִים מַגְבִּיהִין קוֹלָם, כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּשְׁמְעוּ הַבַּיְתוֹסִים וּלְהוֹצִיא מִלִּבָּן. וְקוֹצְרִים הָעֹמֶר בַּלַּיְלָה וְלֹא בַּיּוֹם, דִּכְתִיב וּסְפַרְתֶּם לָכֶם מִמָּחֳרַת הַשַּׁבָּת מִיּוֹם הֲבִיאֲכֶם וְגוֹ'‏ שֶׁבַע שַׁבָּתוֹת תְּמִימוֹת תִּהְיֶינָה, וְאִי אֶפְשָׁר לִהְיוֹת תְּמִימוֹת אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן מַתְחִיל לִמְנוֹת מִתְּחִלַּת הַלַּיְלָה, שֶׁהֲרֵי הַלַּיְלָה תְּחִלַּת הַיּוֹם הוּא, וַהֲרֵי הוּא אוֹמֵר (דברים טז) מֵהָחֵל חֶרְמֵשׁ בַּקָּמָה תָּחֵל לִסְפֹּר, אַלְמָא הַקְּצִירָה בַּלַּיְלָה הוּא בְּשָׁעָה שֶׁמַּתְחִיל לִמְנוֹת:


מפני הצדוקים והביתוסים שהיו אומרים אין קצירת העומר במוצאי יום טוב – other than on Saturday night (i.e., after the Sabbath day), as it is written (Leviticus 23:15): “[And from the day on which you bring the sheaf of elevation offering -] the day after the sabbath – you shall count off [seven weeks].” From the first day after the Sabbath of creation is implied, and it is a tradition in our hands that from the day after the sabbath which is the conclusion of the first Holy Day of Passover, whether it occurs on a weekday or whether it occurs on a Sabbath. And similarly, we found in the Book of Joshua (5:11): “On the day after the Passover offering, on that very day, they ate of the produce of the country, unleavened bread and parched grain,” for behold it states in the Torah (Leviticus 23:14): “Until that very day, [until you have brought the offering of your God], you shall eat no bread or parched grain or fresh ears,” but from after that it held in suspense the permission of the new grain on the aftermath [of the first day] of Passover, the matter was clear that the aftermath of [the first day of] Passover is what permits the new grain and on it, they reap the Omer whether the first Holy Day of Passover occurs on a weekday or whether it occurs on the Sabbath. And therefore, the reapers raise their voices in order that the Boethusians will hear it and to remove it (i.e., the practice of waiting until the Saturday night after the holiday) from their hearts. And they reap the Omer at night and not during the day, as it is written (Leviticus 23:15): “And from the day on which you bring the sheaf of elevation offering – the day after the sabbath – you shall count off seven weeks. They must be complete,” and it is impossible to be “complete” unless one begins to count from the beginning of the nighttime, for the nighttime is the beginning of the day, and behold it states (Deuteronomy 16:9): “start to count the seven weeks when the sickle is first put to the standing grain,” so we see that the reaping is at night at the time when they begin to count.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:4:1)


**מְהַבְהְבִין אוֹתוֹ בָאוּר.** בְּעוֹדוֹ בַּשִּׁבֳּלִין, כְּדֵי לְקַיֵּם בּוֹ מִצְוַת קָלִי, כְּדִכְתִיב (ויקרא ב) אָבִיב קָלוּי בָּאֵשׁ, וּבְמִנְחַת הָעֹמֶר מַיְרֵי קְרָא:


מהבהבין אותו באור (they parch it in fire) – while it is still in the ears, in order to fulfill through it the Mitzvah of parched ears , as it is written (Leviticus 2:14): “[If you bring a grain offering of first fruits to the LORD,] you shall bring near ears parched with fire, and Scripture is speaking of the meal-offering of the Omer.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:4:2)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים.** תְּחִלָּה חוֹבְטִים. וְלֹא כְּדֶרֶךְ תְּבוּאָה יְבֵשָׁה שֶׁחוֹבְטִים אוֹתוֹ בְּמַקֵּל, אֶלָּא בְּקָנִים לַחִים:


וחכמים אומרים – first they knock upon the ears [to thresh the grain out], and not in the manner of dry grain that they beat it with a staff, but rather with moist reeds.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:4:3)


**וּבִקְלִיחוֹת.** בְּקֶלַח שֶׁל כְּרוּב, כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יִתְמָעֵךְ. וְאַחַר כָּךְ מְקַיְּמִין בּוֹ מִצְוַת קָלִי:


ובקליחות – with stalks of cabbage, in order that it would not be crushed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:4:4)


**נְתָנוּהוּ לָאַבּוּב.** דִּסְבִירָא לְהוּ לְרַבָּנָן שֶׁאִם מְהַבְהְבִים אוֹתוֹ בָּאוּר מַמָּשׁ אֵין נִקְרָא קָלִי, שֶׁאֵין נִקְרָא קָלִי אֶלָּא עַל יְדֵי דָּבָר אַחֵר דְּהַיְנוּ עַל יְדֵי כְּלִי, שֶׁנּוֹתְנִים אוֹתוֹ בָּאַבּוּב, וְהוּא כְּלִי שֶׁל נְחֹשֶׁת מְנֻקָּב שֶׁמּוֹכְרֵי קְלָיוֹת קוֹלִין בּוֹ. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


נתנוהו לאבוב – for the Rabbis hold that if they parch it with actual fire, it is not called “parched ears,” but rather through another thing, which is through a utensil, that they place it in the iron tube for roasting grain, and this is a perforated utensil of copper/bronze that sellers of parched ears parch it. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:4:5)


**בְּרֵחַיִם שֶׁל גָּרוֹסוֹת.** שֶׁאֵין טוֹחֲנוֹת דַּק אֶלָּא עָבֶה. שֶׁאִם יִטְחֲנוּ יָפֶה יַעַבְרוּ הַסֻּבִּין שֶׁל קְלִפָּה בַּנָּפָה עִם הַסֹּלֶת. וְגָרוֹסוֹת לְשׁוֹן גְּרִיסִין שֶׁל פּוֹל, וְעַל שֵׁם כֵּן נִקְרֵאת גֶּרֶשׂ כַּרְמֶל:


ברחיים של גרוסות (grist grinders’ mills) – that they don’t mill/grind it thin, but rather thick, for if they grind it well, the coarse bran flour of the husks will pass through in the winnow with the choice flour. And [the word] גרוסות/grist is the language of a dish of grounded beans and as such it is called grist of early ripened and tender barley (see Tractate Menahot 68b).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:4:6)


**וְחַיָּב בַּחַלָּה.** דְּחִיּוּב חַלָּה הַיְנוּ גִּלְגּוּל הָעִסָּה, וְגִלְגּוּל עִסָּה זוֹ בְּיַד הֶדְיוֹט הִיא לְאַחַר שֶׁנִּפְדָּה:


וחייב בחלה – that the obligation [to separate] Hallah which is the rolling of this started dough that is in the hand of the lay person/commoner after it is redeemed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:4:7)


**וּפָטוּר מִן הַמַּעַשְׂרוֹת.** דְּמֵרוּחוֹ בְּיַד הֶקְדֵּשׁ הוּא, וּמֵרוּחַ הֶקְדֵּשׁ פּוֹטֵר מִן הַמַּעַשְׂרוֹת:


ופטור מן המעשרות – that its being evenly shaped is in the hands of that which is dedicated to Temple property, and the giving of a pile of grain an even shape that is dedicated to Temple property is exempt from the tithes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:4:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:4:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:4:8)


**רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא מְחַיֵּב.** הַאי קֶמַח הַנּוֹתָר מֵעִשָּׂרוֹן שֶׁל עֹמֶר:


ר"ע מחייב – this flour that remains from the Issaron of the Omer.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:4:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:4:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:4:9)


**בַּחַלָּה וּבַמַּעַשְׂרוֹת.** לְפִי שֶׁלֹּא נִתְּנוּ מְעוֹת הֶקְדֵּשׁ אֶלָּא בַּצָּרִיךְ לָהֶם לְעִשָּׂרוֹן לְבַד, אֲבָל הָאַחֵר לֹא קָדַשׁ, הִלְכָּךְ לָאו מֵרוּחַ הֶקְדֵּשׁ הוּא, דְּהָא לֹא קַדּוּשׁ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא:


בחלה ובמעשרות – because they did not give money to consecrated property, therefore, the giving of a pile of grain an even shape is not consecrated property , for it is not sacred. But the Halakah is not according to Rabbi Akiva.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:4:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:4:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:4:10)


**בָּא לוֹ לָעִשָּׂרוֹן.** נוֹתֵן תְּחִלָּה שַׁמְנוֹ וּלְבוֹנָתוֹ קֹדֶם נְתִינַת הַסֹּלֶת:


בא לו לעשרון – he puts in its oil first and its frankincense prior to giving the choice flour.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:4:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:4:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:4:11)


**יָצַק וּבָלַל.** לְאַחַר נְתִינַת הַסֹּלֶת, כְּדֶרֶךְ כָּל הַמְּנָחוֹת שֶׁנּוֹתֵן שֶׁמֶן בִּכְלִי תְּחִלָּה וְאַחַר כָּךְ נוֹתֵן סֹלֶת וְחוֹזֵר וְיוֹצֵק עָלֶיהָ שֶׁמֶן וּבוֹלֵל:


יצק ובלל – after putting in the choice flour in the manner of all the meal-offerings that they put oil into a utensil firs and afterwards put in the choice flour and he goes back and pours upon it oil and mingles it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:4:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:4:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:4:12)


**הֵנִיף וְהִגִּישׁ.** דְּמִנְחַת הָעֹמֶר טְעוּנָה הֲנָפָה וְהַגָּשָׁה, כִּדְאָמְרִינַן בְּפֶרֶק כָּל הַמְּנָחוֹת:


הניף והגיש – the meal-offering of the Omer requires waving and bringing it near, as we stated in [Tractate Menahot] Chapter [Five], “All the Meal-Offerings” [Mishnah 6].



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:5:1)


**שֶׁלֹּא בִרְצוֹן חֲכָמִים.** דְּגָזְרֵי שֶׁמָּא יֹאכַל מִן הֶחָדָשׁ כְּשֶׁקּוֹצֵר קֹדֶם שֶׁיַּקְרִיבוּ הָעֹמֶר, וְזֶה שֶׁכְּבָר הוּא קֶמַח וַדַּאי נִקְצַר קֹדֶם הָעֹמֶר:


שלא ברצון חכמים – that he decreed that lest one eats from the new crop (i.e., grain that ripened before Passover. The Torah prohibits harvesting and eating this produce until after the Omer offering on the 16th of Nisan) when they reap prior to offering up the Omer sacrifice, and this is already certain flour that has been harvested prior to the Omer.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:5:2)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר בִּרְצוֹן חֲכָמִים.** דְּלֹא גָּזְרוּ שֶׁמָּא יֹאכַל כְּשֶׁקּוֹצֵר. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


ר' יהודה אומר ברצון חכמים – that they did not decree lest he eats when he harvests/reaps. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehuda.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:5:3)


**וְהָרְחוֹקִים.** שֶׁאֵינָן יוֹדְעִים אִם עֲדַיִן קָרַב הָעֹמֶר:


והרחוקים – that do not know if the Omer was yet offered.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:5:4)


**מֻתָּרִים מֵחֲצוֹת הַיּוֹם וּלְהַלָּן.** כִּדְקָתָנֵי טַעֲמָא לְקַמָּן:


מותרים מחצו היום ולהלן – as its reason will be taught further on.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:5:5)


**שֶׁיְּהֵא יוֹם הָנֵף.** יוֹם שִׁשָּׁה עָשָׂר בְּנִיסָן שֶׁבּוֹ מְנִיפִין אֶת הָעֹמֶר:


שיהא יום הנף – the sixteenth of Nisan that on it they would wave the Omer (see also Tractate Rosh Hashanah, Chapter Four, Mishnah Three and also Tractate Sukkah, Chapter 3, Mishnah 12).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:5:6)


**כֻּלּוֹ אָסוּר.** לֶאֱכֹל בּוֹ חָדָשׁ:


כולו אסור – to eat on it the new crop.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:5:7)


**אָמַר רַבִּי יְהוּדָה.** לְתַנָּא דְּמַתְנִיתִין, וְכִי רַבָּן יוֹחָנָן הִתְקִין:


אמר ר' יהודה – to the Tanna of our Mishnah, for Rabban Yohanan [ben Zakkai] ordained/established the practice.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:5:8)


**וַהֲלֹא מִן הַתּוֹרָה אָסוּר.** בִּזְמַן שֶׁאֵין בֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ קַיָּם. דִּתְרֵי קְרָאֵי כְּתִיבֵי, כָּתוּב אֶחָד אוֹמֵר עַד עֶצֶם הַיּוֹם הַזֶּה, דְּמַשְׁמַע דְּעַצְמוֹ שֶׁל יוֹם כֻּלּוֹ אָסוּר, דְּעַד וְעַד בִּכְלָל, וְכָתוּב אֶחָד אוֹמֵר (ויקרא כג) עַד הֲבִיאֲכֶם אֶת קָרְבַּן אֱלֹהֵיכֶם, דְּמַשְׁמַע דִּלְאַחַר קָרְבַּן הָעֹמֶר מֻתָּר. הָא כֵּיצַד, כָּאן בִּזְמַן שֶׁבֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ קַיָּם, כָּאן בִּזְמַן שֶׁאֵין בֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ קַיָּם. אַלְמָא בִּזְמַן שֶׁאֵין בֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ קַיָּם שֶׁאֵין הָעֹמֶר קָרֵב הֲוֵי יוֹם הָנֵף כֻּלּוֹ אָסוּר מִן הַתּוֹרָה. וּמְשָׁנֵי בַּגְּמָרָא דְּהָא דִּתְנַן הִתְקִין רַבָּן יוֹחָנָן בֶּן זַכַּאי שֶׁיְּהֵא יוֹם הָנֵף כֻּלּוֹ אָסוּר, לֹא תֵימָא הִתְקִין אֶלָּא דָּרַשׁ וְהִתְקִין, כְּלוֹמַר שֶׁדָּרַשׁ מִקְרָאוֹת הַלָּלוּ בָּרַבִּים וְהוֹדִיעָם שֶׁכָּךְ הַדִּין שֶׁיּוֹם הָנֵף כֻּלּוֹ אָסוּר מִן הַתּוֹרָה מִשֶּׁחָרַב בֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ. וְאִית דִּמְפָרְשֵׁי בַּגְּמָרָא, שֶׁאֵין הֶחָדָשׁ אָסוּר מִן הַתּוֹרָה בְּיוֹם שִׁשָּׁה עָשָׂר בְּנִיסָן בִּזְמַן שֶׁאֵין מִקְדָּשׁ אֶלָּא עַד שֶׁיָּאִיר פְּנֵי הַמִּזְרָח שַׁחֲרִית בְּיוֹם שִׁשָּׁה עָשָׂר, דִּכְתִיב עַד עֶצֶם הַיּוֹם הַזֶּה, עַד וְלֹא עַד בִּכְלָל, וְהִתְקִין רַבָּן יוֹחָנָן בֶּן זַכַּאי שֶׁיְּהֵא יוֹם הָנֵף כֻּלּוֹ אָסוּר, מִשּׁוּם מְהֵרָה יִבָּנֶה בֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ וְיֹאמְרוּ אֶשְׁתָּקַד מִי לֹא הֲוָה אָכְלִינַן מִשֶּׁהֵאִיר הַמִּזְרָח, הַשְׁתָּא נַמִּי נֵיכוּל, וְהֵן אֵינָן יוֹדְעִים דְּבִזְמַן הַמִּקְדָּשׁ אָסוּר לֶאֱכֹל חָדָשׁ עַד שֶׁיַּקְרִיב הָעֹמֶר, דִּכְתִיב עַד הֲבִיאֲכֶם אֶת קָרְבַּן אֱלֹהֵיכֶם. וְהָכִי מְפָרְשִׁינַן לַהּ בְּפֶרֶק לוּלָב הַגָּזוּל:


והלא מן התורה אסור – at the time [of the Torah], when the Temple didn’t exist. For two Biblical verses are written, one verse states (Leviticus 23:14): “Until that very day/עד עצם היום הזה, [until you have brought the offering of your God, you shall eat no bread or parched grain or fresh ears,” that it implies that the essence of [that] entire day it is prohibited, up to and including, and the other verse states (Leviticus 23:14): “until you have brought the offering of your God,” that implies that after [bringing] the sacrifice of the Omer it is permitted. How so? Here (i.e., in the second part of the verse just quoted), at the time when the Temple exists; there (i.e., the first part of the verse), at the time when the Temple does not exist. Hence, we see that at the time when the Temple does not exist, the Omer is not offered, it is that the day of waving is completely forbidden (and see the parallel Mishnah mentioned above from Tractate Rosh Hashanah, Chapter 4, Mishnah 3). But the Gemara (Tractate Menahot 68b) responds that what is taught in the Mishnah: “Rabban Yohanan ben Zakkai ordained that the day of waving [of the Omer, the Second Day of Passover] should be wholly prohibited [in respect to new produce],” you should not say, that he ordained, but rather that he expounded and ordained, that is to say, he expounded on these Biblical verses in public and informed them that this is the law, that the day of waving [of the Omer] should be wholly prohibited from the Torah from when the Temple was destroyed. But there are those who expound upon the Gemara (see also Tractate Menahot 68a), that the new crop is not prohibited from the Torah on the sixteenth day of Nisan at the time when there is no Temple but rather only until the eastern horizon is broken through/filled with light in the morning of the sixteenth day [of Nisan], as it is written (Leviticus 23:14): “Until that very day,” up to, but not including, and Rabban Yohanan ben Zakkai ordained that the day of waving would be wholly prohibited, because speedily the Temple should be rebuilt and people would say last year, did we not consume it (i.e., the new crop) from when the eastern horizon is broken through/filled with light, that now also we will eat, but they did not known that at the time of the Temple (i.e., when it existed), it was forbidden to eat new crops until the Omer was offered, as it is written (Leviticus 23:14): “until you have brought the offering of your God. And this we explained in the chapter [of Tractate Sukkah], “The Stolen Lulav” [Chapter 3, Mishnah 12].



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:6:1)


**הָעֹמֶר הָיָה מַתִּיר בַּמְּדִינָה.** לֶאֱכֹל הֶחָדָשׁ בְּכָל הַמְּקוֹמוֹת:


העומר היה מתיר במדינה – to eat the new crop in all places.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:6:2)


**וּשְׁתֵּי הַלֶּחֶם בַּמִּקְדָּשׁ.** שֶׁקֹּדֶם שְׁתֵּי הַלֶּחֶם אֵין מְבִיאִין מִנְחָה מִתְּבוּאָה חֲדָשָׁה, דִּכְתִיב בִּשְׁתֵּי הַלֶּחֶם (במדבר כח) מִנְחָה חֲדָשָׁה, שֶׁתְּהֵא חֲדָשָׁה לְכָל הַמְּנָחוֹת:


ושתי הלחם במקדש – that prior to [bringing of] the two loaves, they don’t bring a meal-offering from the new grain, as it is written in regard to the two loaves (Numbers 28:26): “[On the day of first fruits, your Feast of Weeks,] when you bring an offering of new grain [to the LORD,” that it will be new for all of he meal-offerings.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:6:3)


**וּמִנְחַת בְּהֵמָה.** מִנְחַת נְסָכִים שֶׁל בְּהֵמָה:


ומנחת בהמה – the meal-offering of libations of the cattle.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:6:4)


**וְאִם הֵבִיא.** קֹדֶם לָעֹמֶר:


ואם הביא – prior to the [offering of the] Omer sacrifice.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:6:5)


**פָּסוּל.** שֶׁלֹּא הֻתַּר הֶחָדָשׁ מִכְּלָלוֹ אֲפִלּוּ אֵצֶל הַהֶדְיוֹט:


פסול – for the new crop was not permitted by implication even with regard to the lay person/commoner. But prior to the [sacrifice of] the two loaves, he should not bring.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:6:6)


**אֲבָל קֹדֶם שְׁתֵּי הַלֶּחֶם לֹא יָבִיא, וְאִם הֵבִיא כָּשֵׁר.** שֶׁכְּבָר הֻתַּר מִכְּלָלוֹ אֵצֶל הַהֶדְיוֹט:


ואם ההביא כשר – for it was already permitted by implication with regard to the lay person/commoner.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:7:1)


**וּמִצְטָרְפִין זֶה עִם זֶה.** לְהַשְׁלִים שִׁעוּר הָעִסָּה הַחַיֶּבֶת בַּחַלָּה. וְלֹא שֶׁיִּצְטָרְפוּ כֻּלָּן יַחַד, דְּמִין בְּשֶׁאֵינוֹ מִינוֹ אֵין מִצְטָרֵף, אֶלָּא הַחִטִּין מִצְטָרְפִין עִם הַכֻּסְּמִין בִּלְבַד מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהֵן מִינָן, וְהַשְּׂעֹרִים מִצְטָרְפִין עִם הַכֹּל חוּץ מִן הַחִטִּים. וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּכֻסְּמִין מִין חִטִּין הֵן, לָאו מִין חִטִּין דַּוְקָא, אֶלָּא מִין שְׂעֹרִים וְאַף מִין חִטִּים, וּמִצְטָרְפִין עִם הַחִטִּים וְהַשְּׂעֹרִים. וּמִיהוּ בַּיְרוּשַׁלְמִי מַשְׁמַע דְּאִם נִלּוֹשׁוּ יַחַד מִצְטָרְפִין אֲפִלּוּ מִין בְּשֶׁאֵינוֹ מִינוֹ. אֲבָל אִם לֹא נִלּוֹשׁוּ יַחַד אֶלָּא שֶׁאַחַר כָּךְ הָיוּ נוֹשְׁכוֹת הָעִסּוֹת זוֹ בָּזוֹ, מִין בְּמִינוֹ מִצְטָרְפִין, שֶׁלֹּא בְּמִינוֹ אֵין מִצְטָרְפִין:


ומצטרפין זה עם זה – to complete the measure of the started dough that is liable for [separating out] Hallah [for the Kohen] (see Tractate Hallah, Chapter 1, Mishnah 1 and Chapter 2, Mishnah 6). But they don’t require all of them together for one type mixed with another do not combine, but rather the wheat combines with he spelt alone because they are a species of wheat (see Tractate Pesahim 35a), but the barley combines with all except for the wheat. And even though that spelt is a kind of wheat, it is not exactly the special of wheat, but rather a type of barley and even a type of wheat, and they combine with the wheat and the barley. But however, in the Jerusalem Talmud it implies that if they were kneaded together, they combine, even one type mixed with another type. But if they were not kneaded together, but rather afterwards the started dough would become affixed to each other, one type with a similar type combine, but [one type] mixed with another type do not combine.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:7:2)


**וַאֲסוּרִים בֶּחָדָשׁ.** כְּדִכְתִיב (ויקרא כג) וְלֶחֶם וְקָלִי וְכַרְמֶל לֹא תֹאכְלוּ עַד עֶצֶם הַיּוֹם הַזֶּה, וְגָמְרִינַן לֶחֶם לֶחֶם מִפֶּסַח, מַה לְּהַלָּן מֵחֲמֵשֶׁת הַמִּינִים אַף כָּאן מֵחֲמֵשֶׁת הַמִּינִים:


ואסורים בחדש – as it is written (Leviticus 23:14): “Until that very day, until you have brought the offering of your God, you shall eat no bread or parched grain or fresh ears,” and we learn/derive [through an analogy of] "לחם"\"לחם" – from Passover. Just as there, from the five species, also here from the five species.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:7:3)


**וּמִלִּקְצֹר מִלִּפְנֵי הַפֶּסַח.** שֶׁאָסוּר לִקְצֹר מֵאֶחָד מֵחֲמֵשֶׁת הַמִּינִים קֹדֶם קְצִירַת הָעֹמֶר. דִּכְתִיב בָּעֹמֶר (שם) רֵאשִׁית קְצִירְכֶם, שֶׁתְּהֵא תְּחִלָּה לְכָל הַנִּקְצָרִים, וְאָתְיָא רֵאשִׁית רֵאשִׁית מֵחַלָּה, כְּתִיב הָתָם (במדבר טו) רֵאשִׁית עֲרִסֹתֵכֶם, וּכְתִיב הָכָא רֵאשִׁית קְצִירְכֶם, מַה לְּהַלָּן מֵחֲמֵשֶׁת הַמִּינִים, אַף כָּאן מֵחֲמֵשֶׁת הַמִּינִים:


ומלקצור מלפני הפסח – that it is forbidden to harvest from the five species prior to the harvesting of the Omer. It is written with regard to the Omer (Leviticus 23:10): “[you shall bring] the first sheaf of your harvest to the priest,” that the beginning of all that is harvest, it comes (i.e., it is derived) [as an a fortiori/Kal VaHomer] "ראשית"\"ראשית" from Hallah, it is written there (Numbers 15:20): “as the first yield of our baking,” and it is written here (Leviticus 23:10): “[you shall bring] the first sheaf of your harvest to the priest,” just as there it is from the five species, so here too it is from the five species.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:7:4)


**וְאִם הִשְׁרִישׁוּ.** אֶחָד מֵחֲמֵשֶׁת הַמִּינִים הַלָּלוּ קֹדֶם קְצִירַת הָעֹמֶר:


ואם השרישו – one of these five species prior to the reaping of the Omer.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:7:5)


**הָעֹמֶר מַתִּירָן.** וּמֻתָּר לְקָצְרָן אַחַר קְצִירַת הָעֹמֶר. דִּכְתִיב (שמות כג) אֲשֶׁר תִּזְרַע בַּשָּׂדֶה, מִשָּׁעָה שֶׁנִּזְרַע וְנִשְׁרַשׁ בַּשָּׂדֶה:


העומר מתירן – and it is permitted to harvest them after the harvesting of the Omer. As it is written (Exodus 23:16): “[and the Feast of the Harvest, of the first fruits of your work,] of what you sow in the field,” from the time that it is sown and takes root in the field.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:7:6)


**וְאִם לָאו.** שֶׁלֹּא הִשְׁרִישׁוּ אֶלָּא לְאַחַר קְצִירַת הָעֹמֶר:


ואם לאו – they did not take root other than after the reaping of the Omer.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:7:7)


**אֲסוּרִים עַד שֶׁיָּבֹא הָעֹמֶר הַבָּא.** שֶׁל שָׁנָה הַבָּאָה:


אסורים שיבוא העומר הבא – of the coming year.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:8:1)


**קוֹצְרִים בֵּית הַשְּׁלָחִים שֶׁבָּעֲמָקִים.** שֶׁתְּבוּאָתָן רָעָה וְאֵין מְבִיאִין עֹמֶר מִשָּׁם. וְתַנְיָא, כָּתוּב אֶחָד אוֹמֵר (ויקרא כג) וּקְצַרְתֶּם אֶת קְצִירָהּ וַהֲבֵאתֶם אֶת עֹמֶר, דְּמַשְׁמַע דְּיָכוֹל לִקְצֹר קֹדֶם הֲבָאַת הָעֹמֶר, וְכָתוּב אַחֵר אוֹמֵר רֵאשִׁית קְצִירְכֶם, דְּמַשְׁמַע שֶׁתְּהֵא רֵאשִׁית לְכָל הַקְּצִירוֹת, הָא כֵּיצַד, מִמָּקוֹם שֶׁאַתָּה יָכוֹל לְהָבִיא הָעֹמֶר אִי אַתָּה קוֹצֵר קֹדֶם לָעֹמֶר, מִמָּקוֹם שֶׁאִי אַתָּה מֵבִיא, כְּגוֹן בֵּית הַשְּׁלָחִים וּבֵית הָעֲמָקִים שֶׁאֵין מְבִיאִין עֹמֶר מֵהֶן לְפִי שֶׁהֵן רָעוֹת, אַתָּה קוֹצֵר מֵהֶן קֹדֶם לָעֹמֶר:


קוצרים בית השלחים שבעמקים – that their grain is bad and we don’t bring the Omer from there. And it is taught in a Baraitha (Tractate Menahot 71a), one verse states (Leviticus 23:10): “and you repeat its harvest and you shall bring the… sheaf,” that implies that you are able to reap prior to bring the Omer, and another verse (ibid.,) states: “the first sheaf of your harvest,” which implies that it will be the first of all the reaping, how is this possible? From a place that you are able to bring the Omer you do not reap prior to the Omer, [and] from a place that you cannot bring, as for example, the dry arid land in need of irrigation and the valleys where they don’t bring Omer from them because they are poor [quality], one reaps prior to [bringing] the Omer.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:8:2)


**אֲבָל לֹא גוֹדְשִׁין.** לַעֲשׂוֹתָן גָּדִישׁ. דְּכַמָּה דְּאֶפְשָׁר לְשַׁנּוֹיֵי מְשַׁנִּינַן:


אבל לא גודשין – to make a heap. For whatever It is possible to change, we change.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:8:3)


**אַנְשֵׁי יְרִיחוֹ.** בֵּית הַשְּׁלָחִין הֲווּ לְהוּ:


אנשי יריחו – they had dry, arid lands requiring irrigation.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:8:4)


**קוֹצֵר לַשַּׁחַת.** מֻתָּר לִקְצֹר לַשַּׁחַת קֹדֶם לָעֹמֶר וּמַאֲכִיל לִבְהֶמְתּוֹ:


קוצר שחת – it is permissible to reap corn in its earliest stage [used instead of grass] prior to [the reaping of] the Omer and feed it to his cattle.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:8:5)


**אֵימָתַי בִּזְמַן שֶׁהִתְחִיל.** לְקָצְרָהּ לְצֹרֶךְ בְּהֶמְתּוֹ עַד שֶׁלֹּא הֵבִיאָה שְׁלִישׁ אַחֲרוֹן שֶׁל גְּמַר בִּשּׁוּלָהּ, קוֹצֵר אַף לְאַחַר שֶׁהֵבִיאָה שְׁלִישׁ:


אימתי בזמן שהתחיל – to reap it for the needs of his cattle until it did not bring up the final third of the completion of its ripening, he reaps even after it brought up the third.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:8:6)


**רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן אוֹמֵר אַף יִקְצֹר וְיַאֲכִיל.** יַתְחִיל לִקְצֹר וּמַאֲכִיל לִבְהֶמְתּוֹ מִשֶּׁהֵבִיאָה שְׁלִישׁ. דְּכָל לַשַּׁחַת לָאו קָצִיר הוּא. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה, שֶׁבָּא לְפָרֵשׁ דְּבָרָיו שֶׁל תַּנָּא קַמָּא:


ר' שמעון אומר אף יקצור ויאכיל – he will begin to reap and feed it to his cattle when it brings forth a third [of its full growth], and all of the corn in its earliest stage [used instead of grass] is not reaping. But the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehuda who comes to explain the words of the first Tanna/teacher.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:9:1)


**קוֹצְרִים.** קֹדֶם לָעֹמֶר:


קוצרים – prior to the Omer.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:9:2)


**מִפְּנֵי הַנְּטִיעוֹת.** שֶׁלֹּא יַפְסִידוּ. לְפִי שֶׁאוֹתָהּ תְּבוּאָה אֵינָהּ רְאוּיָה לָעֹמֶר, כִּדְאָמְרִינַן בְּפֶרֶק כָּל הַקָּרְבָּנוֹת, אֵין מְבִיאִין לֹא מִשְּׂדֵה בֵּית הַשְּׁלָחִים וְלֹא מִשְּׂדֵה אִילָן, וּלְעֵיל אָמְרִינַן מִמְּקוֹם שֶׁאִי אַתָּה מֵבִיא אַתָּה קוֹצֵר. פֵּרוּשׁ אַחֵר, מִפְּנֵי הַנְּטִיעוֹת, מִשּׁוּם כִּלְאַיִם. לְפִי שֶׁפְּעָמִים אָדָם זוֹרֵעַ תְּבוּאָה וְאֵין שָׁם נְטִיעוֹת, וּלְאַחַר זְמַן עוֹלוֹת שָׁם נְטִיעוֹת בֵּינֵיהֶן מֵאֲלֵיהֶן, וְצָרִיךְ לִקְצֹר הַזְּרָעִים מִשּׁוּם כִּלְאַיִם:


מפני בנטיעות – that they will lose them. Because that grain is not appropriate for the Omer, as we stated in the chapter “All of the Sacrifices/כל הקרבנות” (Tractate Menahot, Chapter 8, Mishnah 2), we do not bring either from an arid irrigated field nor from a field containing at least three tees within a distance of a Seah, and above we stated (quoting from Tractate Menahot 71a), that from a place that you don’t bring [the Omer] you reap. Another explanation: “Because of the seedlings/מפני הנטיעות,” because of mixed seeds, because sometimes a person sows grain and there are no seedlings there, and after a time seedlings rise up there on their own between them, and one must reap he seeds because of mixed seeds/Kilyaim.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:9:3)


**וּמִפְּנֵי בֵית הָאֵבֶל.** שֶׁאֵין לָהֶם מָקוֹם פָּנוּי לֵישֵׁב לְבָרֵךְ בִּרְכַּת רְחָבָה שֶׁאוֹמְרִים בְּבֵית הָאֵבֶל:


מפני בית האבל – that they lack the free space rto sit and to recite the blessing of comfort that we say in the House of Mourning.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:9:4)


**וּמִפְּנֵי בֵּית הַמִּדְרָשׁ.** שֶׁאֵין מָקוֹם לַתַּלְמִידִים לֵישֵׁב. וְטַעֲמָא דְּכָל הָנֵי, מִשּׁוּם דִּקְצִיר מִצְוָה נִינְהוּ, וְהַכָּתוּב אוֹמֵר רֵאשִׁית קְצִירְכֶם, שֶׁיִּהְיֶה הָעֹמֶר רֵאשִׁית לִקְצִירְכֶם שֶׁל רְשׁוּת וְלֹא רֵאשִׁית לְקָצִיר שֶׁל מִצְוָה:


ומפני בית המדרש – for there is no place for the students to sit. And the reason of all of these, is because reaping is a Commandment, and the Scriptural verse states (Leviticus 23:10): “the first sheaf of your harvest,” that the Omer will be the fist of your optional harvest and not the first of the command of reaping.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:9:5)


**כְּרִיכוֹת.** אֲלֻמּוֹת קְשׁוּרוֹת:


כריכות – bound large sheaves.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:9:6)


**צְבָתִים.** אֲגֻדּוֹת בְּלֹא קִשּׁוּר. פֵּרוּשׁ אַחֵר, כְּרִיכוֹת אֲלֻמּוֹת גְּדוֹלוֹת, צְבָתִים אֲגֻדּוֹת קְטַנּוֹת:


צבתים – bundles without tying. Another interpretation: bundles of bound large sheaves, heaps are small bundles


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:9:7
