[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:9:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:9:7)


**מִן הַקָּמָה.** שֶׁיְּהֵא קוֹצֵר לִשְׁמוֹ:


מן הקמח – that he would reap for its sake.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:9:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:9:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:9:8)


**לֹא מָצָא.** שֶׁכְּבָר נִקְצַר הַכֹּל:


לא מצא – that everything was already reaped.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:9:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:9:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:9:9)


**מִן הַלַּח.** דִּכְתִיב כַּרְמֶל, רַךְ וּמָל:


מן הלח – as it is written (Leviticus 23:14): “You shall eat no [bread or parched grain] or fresh ears,” that it is soft and brittle (see Tractate Menahot 66b).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 10:9:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:9:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/10:9:10)


**לִקְצֹר בַּלַּיְלָה.** כִּדְיָלְפִינַן בְּרֵישׁ פִּרְקִין, מִדִּכְתִיב (דברים טז) מֵהָחֵל חֶרְמֵשׁ בַּקָּמָה תָּחֵל לִסְפֹּר, מִשָּׁעָה שֶׁאַתָּה מוֹנֶה אַתָּה קוֹצֵר, וְהַסְּפִירָה צְרִיכָה לִהְיוֹת בַּלַּיְלָה דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא כג) שֶׁבַע שַׁבָּתוֹת תְּמִימֹת:


לקצור בלילה – that we derived at the beginning of the chapter (see Tractate Menahot, Chapter 10, Mishnah 3), as it is written (Deuteronomy 16:9): “when the sickle is first put to the standing grain,” from the time that you count you reap, for the counting must be at night, as it is written (Leviticus 23:15): “you shall count off seven weeks. They must be complete.” (see also Tractate Megillah, Chapter 2, Mishnah 6).



## Chapter 11


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:1:1)


**שְׁתֵּי הַלֶּחֶם.** לֶחֶם הַפָּנִים נִלּוֹשׁ אֶחָד אֶחָד. דִּכְתִיב בֵּיהּ (ויקרא כד) שְׁנֵי עֶשְׂרֹנִים יִהְיֶה הַחַלָּה הָאֶחָת, מְלַמֵּד שֶׁנִּלּוֹשׁוֹת אַחַת אַחַת. וּמִנַּיִן שֶׁאֲפִיָּתָן שְׁתַּיִם שְׁתַּיִם, תַּלְמוּד לוֹמַר (שם) וְשַׂמְתָּ אֹתָם, שִׂימָה רִאשׁוֹנָה שֶׁאַתָּה עוֹשֶׂה מֵהֶם דְּהַיְנוּ בַּתַּנּוּר תְּהֵא בִּלְשׁוֹן אוֹתָם, דְּמַשְׁמַע שֶׁהָיָה נוֹתֵן שְׁנַיִם בִּשְׁנֵי דְּפוּסִים יַחַד בַּתַּנּוּר. יָכוֹל אַף שְׁתֵּי הַלֶּחֶם כֵּן, תַּלְמוּד לוֹמַר וְשַׂמְתָּ אֹתָם, שֶׁהָיָה לוֹ לוֹמַר וְשַׂמְתָּם, מַה תַּלְמוּד לוֹמַר וְשַׂמְתָּ אֹתָם, אוֹתָם אַתָּה נוֹתֵן שְׁנַיִם שְׁנַיִם בַּתַּנּוּר וְאִי אַתָּה נוֹתֵן שְׁתֵּי הַלֶּחֶם שְׁנַיִם שְׁנַיִם אֶלָּא אֶחָד אֶחָד:


שתי הלחם. לחם הפנים נילוש אחת אחת – as it is written concerning it (Leviticus 24:5): “[You shall take choice flour and bake of it twelve loaves,] two-tenths of a measure for each loaf,” which teaches that they are kneaded one by one. And from where [do we learn] that their baking is two at a time? The inference teaches us (Leviticus 24:6): “Place them/ושמת אותם [on the pure table before the LORD in two rows, six to a row].” The first placement that you do from them, that is in the oven, will be in the [plural] language of “them/אותם”, that implies that he would place two in two molds/frames together in the oven. It is possible that even the two loaves are like this, as the inference teaches us (ibid.,): “Place them,” that you should have said, “place them” [regarding the shewbread/לחם הפנים ], what does the inference each us – those – you place two by two in the oven, but you do not place the two loaves – two by two, but rather one at a time.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:1:2)


**וּבִטְפוּס.** כְּמוֹ דְּפוּס, פורמ"א בְּלַעַ"ז. כְּמִין תֵּבָה שֶׁנִּטַּל כִּסּוּיָהּ וּשְׁתֵּי דָּפְנוֹתֶיהָ זוֹ כְּנֶגֶד זוֹ, כָּךְ הַלֶּחֶם הָיוּ לוֹ שְׁתֵּי דְּפָנוֹת וְשׁוּלַיִם רְחָבִים, וּמְתַקֵּן הַבָּצֵק בְּתוֹךְ הַדְּפוּס שֶׁיְּהֵא עָשׂוּי כְּעֵין הַדְּפוּס:


ובטפוס – like דפוס/mold or frame – PURMA in the foreign tongue, like a sort of box that you take its cover and its two walls one opposite the other, so also the bread, you would have two walls and wide rims at the bottom of the vessel and you prepare the dough within the mold that it is made like a kind of mold.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:1:3)


**וּכְשֶׁהוּא.** רוֹדֶה אוֹתָן מִן הַתַּנּוּר:


וכשהוא רודה –[removes/detaches bread from where it sticks] from the oven.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:1:4)


**נוֹתְנָן בַּדְּפוּס כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יִתְקַלְקְלוּ.** וְיִשָּׁבְרוּ. נִמְצָא שְׁלֹשָׁה דְפוּסִים הֵן, אֶחָד כְּשֶׁהִיא בָּצֵק, וְאֶחָד הָיָה לָהּ בַּתַּנּוּר כְּשֶׁהִיא נֶאֱפֵית, וְאֶחָד כְּשֶׁהוּא רוֹדָהּ מִן הַתַּנּוּר נוֹתְנָהּ בַּדְּפוּס כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא תִּתְקַלְקֵל:


נותנן בדפוס. כדי שלא יתקלקלו – and break. It is found that there are three molds: one when it is dough and one when it was in the oven when it is baking, and one when he detaches bread from where it sticks from the oven and places it in a mold in order that it not be spoiled (see Tractate Menahot 94a).



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:2:1)


**לִישָׁתָן וַעֲרִיכָתָן בַּחוּץ וַאֲפִיָּתָן בִּפְנִים.** לֹא אִתְבְּרִירָא טַעֲמָא דְּהָא מִלְּתָא בַּגְּמָרָא אַמַּאי לִישָׁתָן וַעֲרִיכָתָן בַּחוּץ וַאֲפִיָּתָן בִּפְנִים:


לישתן ועריכתן בחוץ ואפייתן בפנים – but the reason for this matter is not explained in the Gemara, why their kneading and their rolling is outside [the Temple courtyard] but their baking is inside [the Temple courtyard].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:2:2)


**וְאֵינָן דּוֹחוֹת אֶת הַשַּׁבָּת.** אֲפִיָּתָן:


ואינן דוחות את השבת – their baking.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:2:3)


**וּכְשֵׁרוֹת בְּבֵית פָּאגִי.** רַמְבַּ"ם אוֹמֵר שֶׁהוּא מָקוֹם סָמוּךְ לְהַר הַבַּיִת מִחוּץ, שֶׁשָּׁם הָיוּ אוֹפִים הַמְּנָחוֹת. מִלְּשׁוֹן פַּת בַּג הַמֶּלֶךְ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה אֶלָּא כְּדִבְרֵי תַּנָּא קַמָּא:


וכשרות בבית פאגי – Maimonides states that it is a place close to the Temple Mount from outside (see Tractate Menahot, Chapter 7, Mishnah 3 – as a special place for the baking of meal-offerings) that they would bake the meal-offerings thee. From the language of (Daniel 1:12-13): “[Please test your servants for ten days, giving us legumes to eat and water to drink. Then compare our appearance with that of the youths] who eat of the king’s food, [and do with your servants as you see fit].” But the Halakha is onot other than according to the words of the first Tanna/teacher.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:3:1)


**חֲבִתֵּי כֹהֵן גָּדוֹל לִישָׁתָן וַעֲרִיכָתָן בִּפְנִים.** לְדִבְרֵי הַכֹּל. שֶׁאוֹתוֹ חֲצִי עִשָּׂרוֹן שֶׁחוֹלֵק בּוֹ עֶשְׂרוֹנוֹ שֶׁל כֹּהֵן גָּדוֹל נִמְשַׁח וְנִתְקַדֵּשׁ וּמְקַדֵּשׁ אֶת הַמִּנְחָה:


חביתי כהן גדול לישתן ועריכתן בפנים – according to the words of everyone. For that one-half Issaron that they divide its Issaron of the High Priest who is anointed and sanctified and sanctifies the meal offering (see also Tractate Menahot, Chapter 4, Mishnah 5 and Chapter 6, Mishnah 5).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:3:2)


**וְדוֹחוֹת אֶת הַשַּׁבָּת.** לְפִי שֶׁאִי אֶפְשָׁר לַעֲשׂוֹת לִישָׁה וַעֲרִיכָה וַאֲפִיָּה מֵאֶתְמוֹל, דְּכֵיוָן דְּמִקַּדְּשָׁא בִּכְלִי מִפַּסְּלָא בְּלִינָה:


ודוחות את השבת – because it is impossible to perform its kneading and its rolling and its baking from the day before, for since it is sanctified in a vessel, it becomes defiled staying overnight [This principle is also taught in Tractate Shabbat, Chapter 19, Mishnah 1 with regard to ritual circumcision and in Tractate Pesahim, Chapter 6, Mishnah 2 with regard to the Passover offering].



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:4:1)


**יֵשׁ בָּהֶן מַעֲשֵׂה כְלִי בִּפְנִים.** בִּמְלַאכְתָּן שֶׁנַּעֲשֶׂה בָּהֶן בִּפְנִים טְעוּנִים כְּלִי. לַאֲפוֹקֵי עַל גַּבֵּי טַבְלָא. וּבִמְלַאכְתָּן שֶׁנַּעֲשֵׂית בַּחוּץ, כְּגוֹן לִישָׁתוֹ וַעֲרִיכָתוֹ שֶׁל לֶחֶם הַפָּנִים, אֵינוֹ טָעוּן כְּלִי:


יש בהן מעשה כלי בפנים – in their work which is performed with them inside [the Temple courtyard] requires a [consecrated] utensil.. To exclude on top of a board. But their work (i.e., preparation) is done outside [the Temple courtyard], as for example the kneading and rolling of the shewbread, which does not require a [consecrated] utensil.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:4:2)


**וְקַרְנוֹתֵיהֶן.** שֶׁהוּא מַדְבִּיק בָּצֵק לְכָל דֹּפֶן כְּמִין קַרְנַיִם, וְאֹרֶךְ הַקֶּרֶן יוֹצֵא אַרְבַּע אֶצְבָּעוֹת:


וקרנותיה – on which he fastens/pastes dough for each board, like horns. And the length of each horn/projection extends four fingers.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:4:3)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר שֶׁלֹּא תִטְעֶה.** בֵּין שְׁתֵּי הַלֶּחֶם לְלֶחֶם הַפָּנִים בֵּין בְּשִׁעוּר אֹרֶךְ וְרֹחַב בֵּין בְּשִׁעוּר הַקְּרָנוֹת. בִּשְׁתֵּי הַלֶּחֶם אָרְכּוֹ שִׁבְעָה וְרָחְבּוֹ אַרְבָּעָה וְקַרְנוֹתָיו אַרְבַּע אֶצְבָּעוֹת. וְסִימָנָךְ זד"ד. וְלֶחֶם הַפָּנִים אָרְכּוֹ עֲשָׂרָה וְרָחְבּוֹ חֲמִשָּׁה וְקַרְנוֹתָיו שֶׁבַע אֶצְבָּעוֹת. וְסִימָנָךְ יה"ז. וְדַרְכּוֹ שֶׁל רַבִּי יְהוּדָה לָתֵת סִימָנִים, כְּמוֹ דצ"ך עד"ש באח"ב:


רבי יהודה אומר שלא תטעה – whether the two loaves to the shewbread, whether in the measurement of the length and the width, or whether in the measurement of the horns/projections. The two loaves, its length is seven [handbreadths] and its width is four [handbreadths] and its horns/projections are four finger lengths. And your sign/pneumonic is זד"ד/seven, four, four. The shewbread, its length is ten [handbreadths] and its width is five [handbreadths] and its horns/projections is seven fingers length. And your symbol/pneumonic is יה"ז/ten, five and seven. And the methodology of Rabbi Yehuda is to give signs/pneumonic like דצ"ך עד"ש באח"ב (i.e., the abbreviations for the Ten Plagues found in the Passover Haggadah).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:4:4)


**שֶׁיְּהֵא לוֹ פָנִים.** דְּפָנוֹת. [שֶׁיְּהֵא לוֹ פִּנִּים זָוִיּוֹת] וְהֵן הֵן הַקַּרְנַיִם:


שיהא לו פנים – walls [that it would have corners/angles] and these are the horns.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:5:1)


**אָרְכּוֹ עֲשָׂרָה וְרָחְבּוֹ חֲמִשָּׁה.** כְּדִכְתִיב (שמות כח) אַמָּתַיִם אָרְכּוֹ וְאַמָּה רָחְבּוֹ. רַבִּי יְהוּדָה לְטַעֲמֵיהּ דְּאָמַר שֶׁל כֵּלִים בַּת חֲמִשָּׁה טְפָחִים הִיא:


ארכו עשרה ורחבו חמשה – as it is written (Exodus 25:23): “[You shall make a table of acacia wood.] two cubits long, one cubit wide.” Rabbi Yehuda according to his reasoning that he stated of utensils that are five handbreadths [in width] (see also Tractate Kelim, Chapter 17, Mishnah 10).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:5:2)


**נוֹתֵן אָרְכּוֹ.** שֶׁל לֶחֶם הַפָּנִים כְּנֶגֶד רָחְבּוֹ שֶׁל שֻׁלְחָן:


נותן ארכו (one sets it lengthwise against the breadth of the table) – of the showbread corresponding to the width of the table.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:5:3)


**וְכוֹפֵל.** הַלֶּחֶם, טְפָחַיִם וּמֶחֱצָה לְמַעְלָה בַּגֹּבַהּ מִכָּאן וּמִכָּאן. וְהֵן הֵן הַקְּרָנוֹת. וְנִמְצְאוּ זְקוּפוֹת בְּשָׁוֶה לִשְׂפַת הַשֻּׁלְחָן:


וכופל – the bread, two-and-one-half handbreadths above at its height from here and there, and they are its horns/corners. But they were found equally bent at the rim of the table.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:5:4)


**וְנִמְצָא אָרְכּוֹ מְמַלֵּא רָחְבּוֹ שֶׁל שֻׁלְחָן.** וְרָחְבּוֹ מַחֲזִיק חֲצִי אֹרֶךְ שֶׁל שֻׁלְחָן, וּכְשֶׁהָיָה מְסַדֵּר אַחֵר אֶצְלוֹ נִמְצָא שֻׁלְחָן כֻּלּוֹ מָלֵא:


ונמצא ארכו ממלא רחבו של השלחן – and its width holds/contains half of the length of the table, and when he would set up another near it, it is found that the entire table is full.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:5:5)


**אָרְכּוֹ שְׁנֵים עָשָׂר.** רַבִּי מֵאִיר לְטַעֲמֵיהּ דְּאָמַר כָּל אַמָּה בַּת שִׁשָּׁה טְפָחִים, חוּץ מִמִּזְבַּח הַזָּהָב וְקֶרֶן מִזְבֵּחַ הַחִיצוֹן וְהַסּוֹבֵב וְהַיְסוֹד. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי מֵאִיר:


ארכו שנים עשר – Rabbi Meir, according to his reasoning who stated that each cubit is six handbreadths, except for the Golden Altar and the horn of the outer altar and the gallery around the altar for the priest to walk on and the base of the altar. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Meir.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:5:6)


**לֶחֶם הַפָּנִים אָרְכּוֹ עֲשָׂרָה.** בְּהָא מוֹדֶה רַבִּי מֵאִיר:


לחם הפנים ארכו עשרה – in this Rabbi Meir agrees (see Tractate Menahot, Chapter 11, Mishnah 4).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:5:7)


**וּטְפָחַיִם רֶוַח בָּאֶמְצָע.** בֵּין שְׁנֵי הַסְּדָרִים. דְּרֹחַב הַלֶּחֶם לֹא הֲוֵי אֶלָּא חֲמִשָּׁה, שְׁנֵי הַסְּדָרִים מַחֲזִיקִין עֲשָׂרָה בַּשֻּׁלְחָן לְאָרְכּוֹ, פַּשׁוּ לְהוּ טְפָחַיִם רֶוַח בֵּין סֵדֶר לְסֵדֶר, שֶׁתְּהֵא רוּחַ מְנַשֶּׁבֶת בָּהֶם שֶׁלֹּא יִתְעַפְּשׁוּ:


וטפחיים ריוח באמצע – between the two rows [of bread in the center]. For the width of the bread is not other than five [handbreadths], the two rows [of bread] sustain the ten handbreadths of the table to its length, subtract the two handbreadths of space between each row [of bread], so that the wind blows on them so that they don’t grow moldy.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:5:8)


**אַבָּא שָׁאוּל אוֹמֵר שָׁם.** בְּאוֹתוֹ רֶוַח שֶׁבֵּין סֵדֶר לְסֵדֶר נוֹתְנִים הַבְּזִיכִים:


אבא שאול אומר שם – in that space between each row/pile they place the dishes/vessels.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:5:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:5:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:5:9)


**וַהֲלֹא כְבָר נֶאֱמַר וְנָתַתָּ עַל הַמַּעֲרֶכֶת.** וְעַל, מַמָּשׁ מַשְׁמַע:


והלא כבר נאמר ונתת על המערכת – and on, actually by implication.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:5:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:5:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:5:10)


**וְעָלָיו מַטֵּה מְנַשֶּׁה.** וְהַיְנוּ סָמוּךְ, הָכָא נַמִּי עַל סָמוּךְ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּאַבָּא שָׁאוּל:


ועליו מטה מנשה – and that is near to it. Here also on nearby. And the Halakha is according to Abba Shaul.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:6:1)


**מֻפְצָלִין.** לָתֵת רָאשֵׁי קָנִים בְּאוֹתָן פִּצּוּלִים. וּכְמִנְיַן הַקָּנִים כָּךְ מִנְיַן הַפִּצּוּלִים:


מפוצלין – to give the heads of the branches the staves between the cakes of the shewbread heads shaped into forked branches [at the top], that as the number of the staves, so too the number of the forked branches.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:6:2)


**אַרְבָּעָה עָשָׂר לְסֵדֶר זֶה.** שֵׁשׁ חַלּוֹת שֶׁהָיוּ בְּסֵדֶר הַמַּעֲרֶכֶת. לְאַרְבָּעָה מֵהֶן לְכָל אַחַת שְׁלֹשָׁה קָנִים, הֲרֵי שְׁנֵים עָשָׂר קָנִים, וְהָעֶלְיוֹנָה אֵינָהּ צְרִיכָה אֶלָּא שְׁנַיִם לְפִי שֶׁאֵין עָלֶיהָ מַשְּׂאוֹי, הֲרֵי אַרְבָּעָה עָשָׂר. הַחַלָּה הַתַּחְתּוֹנָה אֵינָהּ צְרִיכָה כָּל עִקָּר, לְפִי שֶׁמֻּנַּחַת עַל טָהֳרוֹ שֶׁל שֻׁלְחָן:


ארבעה עשר לסדר זה – six loaves that were in the order of the arrangement on the pile of wood on the altar in the Temple. For four of them, each of them had three branches, there were twelve staves, but for the uppermost it only needed two because there is no burden upon it, hence [there are] fourteen. The lowest loaf did not need anything, because it was placed immediately on the golden table (see Tractate Menahot 97a).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:6:3)


**לֹא סִדּוּר קָנִים.** בַּמַּעֲרָכָה הַחֲדָשָׁה:


לא סדור קנים – in a new pile of wood on the altar.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:6:4)


**וְלֹא נְטִילָתָן.** מֵעַל הַיְשָׁנָה, דּוֹחֶה שַׁבָּת:


לא נטילתן – from the old [pile] supersedes the Sabbath.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:6:5)


**נִכְנָס מֵעֶרֶב שַׁבָּת וְשׁוֹמְטָן.** מִבֵּין כָּל לֶחֶם וְלֶחֶם, וּמַנִּיחָם לְאָרְכּוֹ שֶׁל שֻׁלְחָן, וּלְמָחָר מְסַדֵּר הַלֶּחֶם, וּלְמוֹצָאֵי שַׁבָּת נוֹתֵן הַקָּנִים בֵּין כָּל לֶחֶם כְּמִשְׁפָּטָן:


נכנס מערב שבת ושומטן – from between each and every loaf, and he places them on the length of the table. And on the next day, he arranges the bread, and on Saturday night (i.e., after the Sabbath), he places the reeds between each bread according to the statute.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:6:6)


**אָרְכָּן לְאָרְכּוֹ שֶׁל בָּיִת.** מִמִּזְרָח לְמַעֲרָב הָיוּ מֻנָּחִין בְּאָרְכָּן. חוּץ מִן הָאָרוֹן שֶׁאָרְכּוֹ לְרָחְבּוֹ שֶׁל בַּיִת, שֶׁהַבַּדִּים שֶׁבָּאָרוֹן הָיוּ בּוֹלְטִים וְדוֹחֲקִין בַּפָּרֹכֶת וְדּוֹמִין כִּשְׁנֵי דַּדֵּי אִשָּׁה, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (במלכים א ח) וַיַּאֲרִכוּ הַבַּדִּים וַיֵּרָאוּ, וְהַבַּדִּים לְרָחְבּוֹ שֶׁל אָרוֹן הָיוּ מֻנָּחִים, דְּאִלּוּ לְאָרְכּוֹ שֶׁל אָרוֹן הָיוּ בַּדִּים לֹא הָיָה בֵּין בַּד לְבַד אֶלָּא שִׁעוּר רֹחַב הָאָרוֹן דְּהַיְנוּ אַמְּתָא וּפַלְגָּא, וּתְרֵי גַּבְרֵי הַנּוֹשְׂאִים בִּשְׁנֵי בַּדִּים אֶת הָאָרוֹן שְׁנַיִם מִכָּאן וּשְׁנַיִם מִכָּאן לֹא הֲווּ מָצֵי לִכָּנֵס בֵּין בַּד לְבַד, אֶלָּא וַדַּאי לְרָחְבּוֹ נְתוּנִים, וְהֵם הָיוּ מִמִּזְרָח לְמַעֲרָב שֶׁהֲרֵי הָיוּ דּוֹחֲקִים בַּפָּרֹכֶת, נִמְצָא אָרְכּוֹ שֶׁל אָרוֹן לְרָחְבּוֹ שֶׁל בַּיִת:


ארכן לארכו של הבית – from east to west they would be placed by their length, except for the Ark whose length is according to the width of the Temple, for the staves/poles that were in the Ark would protrude and press/squeeze the curtain and they are similar a woman’s two breasts, as it states (I Kings 8:8): “The poles projected so that the ends of the poles were visible in the sanctuary in front of the Shrine,” and the poles were placed according to the width of the Ark, for had they been according to the length of the Ark, there would not have been each pole other than the measurement of the width of the Ark which is a one-and-one-half cubits, and two men carry the Ark with two poles, two from one side and two from the other, and they would not be able to enter between each pole, it certainly was placed in their width, and they wee from east to west that they would press the curtain, so it is found that the Ark was according to the width of the Temple.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:7:1)


**עַל פֶּתַח הַבַּיִת.** אֵצֶל פֶּתַח הַהֵיכָל בִּכְנִיסָתוֹ. וְלֹא הָיוּ מַנִּיחִים שָׁם אֶלָּא לְהַרְאוֹת שֶׁמַּעֲלִין בַּקֹּדֶשׁ, דְּעַכְשָׁיו מַנִּיחִין עַל שֶׁל שַׁיִשׁ וּמִיָּד נוֹשְׂאָן לַהֵיכָל וּמְסַדְּרָן עַל שֶׁל זָהָב שֶׁל מֹשֶׁה:


על פתח הבית – next to the door of the Sanctuary at their entrance, but they would not place [the tables] there other than to demonstrate that we ascend in holiness, that now that we place on one of marble and immediately carry them to the Sanctuary and set them up (i.e., the shewbread) on one (i.e., a table) of gold of Moses.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:7:2)


**וְעַל שֶׁל זָהָב בִּיצִיאָתוֹ.** עַד שֶׁיֻּקְטְרוּ בְּזִיכִים, כִּדְקָתָנֵי לְקַמָּן בְּמַתְנִיתִין:


ועל של זהב ביציאתו – until they will be offered as incense in dishes/censors as it is taught further on in our Mishnah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:7:3)


**הַמַּכְנִיסִים עוֹמְדִים בַּצָּפוֹן.** דְּהָכִי עָדִיף טְפֵי דְּאוֹתָן שֶׁמְּסַדְּרִים הָעֲבוֹדָה יִהְיוּ בַּצָּפוֹן:


המכניסים עומדים בצפון – that this is more preferable that those who arrange the Divine service will be iin the north.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:7:4)


**אֵלּוּ מוֹשְׁכִין.** וְעַד שֶׁלֹּא יַגְבִּיהוּ מִן הַשֻּׁלְחָן אֵלּוּ מַנִּיחִין:


אלו מושכין (draw out) – and until they don’t lift them (i.e., the old ones) from the table, these others place [the new ones] down.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:7:5)


**אֲפִלּוּ אֵלּוּ נוֹטְלִין.** וְאֵלּוּ עַצְמָן מַנִּיחִין אַחַר שֶׁנָּטְלוּ:


אפילו אלו נוטלין – and these themselves lay them down after they took them [off].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:7:6)


**אַף זוֹ הָיְתָה תָּמִיד.** דְּסָבַר רַבִּי יוֹסֵי דְּאֵין תָּמִיד אֶלָּא שֶׁלֹּא יָלִין שֻׁלְחָן לַיְלָה אֶחָד בְּלֹא לֶחֶם:


אף זו היתה תמיד – for Rabbi Yossi holds that it is not “perpetual” other than that the table should not be kept overnight without bread (i.e., the shewbread).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:7:7)


**וְהַחַלּוֹת מִתְחַלְּקוֹת לַכֹּהֲנִים.** מִשְׁמָר הַיּוֹצֵא חוֹלֵק עִם מִשְׁמָר הַנִּכְנָס:


והחלות מתחלקות לכהנים – the departing division of duty of the priests divides them up with the entering division of duty of the priests.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:7:8)


**חָל יוֹם הַכִּפּוּרִים לִהְיוֹת בְּשַׁבָּת.** שֶׁדִּין לֶחֶם הַפָּנִים לְאָכְלוֹ בְּאוֹתוֹ שַׁבָּת שֶׁנּוֹטְלִים אוֹתוֹ מֵעַל הַשֻּׁלְחָן, וְעַכְשָׁיו אֵין יְכוֹלִים לְאָכְלוֹ מִפְּנֵי הַתַּעֲנִית:


חל יום הכיפורים להיות בשבת – for the law of the shewbread is to consume it on that Sabbath when they take it from upon the table, but now, they are unable to consume it because of the fast [of Yom Kippur].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:7:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:7:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:7:9)


**הַחַלּוֹת מִתְחַלְּקוֹת לָעָרֶב.** וְאֵין מַמְתִּינִים לְחַלְּקוֹ לְמָחָר מִפְּנֵי שֶׁנִּפְסַל בְּלִינָה לְאַחַר זְמַנּוֹ שֶׁנִּסְתַּלֵּק:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:7:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:7:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:7:10)


**הַשָּׂעִיר.** שֶׁל מוּסָף שֶׁהוּא חַטָּאת וְנֶאֱכָל לַכֹּהֲנִים:


השעיר – of the Musaf/Additional [Service] sacrifice which is a sin-offering and it is consumed by the priests.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:7:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:7:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:7:11)


**נֶאֱכָל לָעָרֶב.** בְּלֵילֵי שַׁבָּת. שֶׁזְּמַנּוֹ לְיוֹם וְלַיְלָה עַד חָצוֹת. וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵין יְכוֹלִים לְבַשְּׁלוֹ בְּשַׁבָּת וְלֹא בְּיוֹם הַכִּפּוּרִים:


לנאכל לערב – on Friday night, for its time is during the day and that night until midnight. And even though they are not able to cook it on the Sabbath nor on Yom Kippur.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:7:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:7:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:7:12)


**הַבַּבְלִיִּים.** כֹּהֲנִים שֶׁעָלוּ מִבָּבֶל. וּבַגְּמָרָא מְפָרֵשׁ דְּלֹא בַּבְלִיִּים הָיוּ אֶלָּא אַלֶכְּסַנְדְּרִיִּים הָיוּ. וְעַל שֵׁם שֶׁשּׂוֹנְאִים תַּלְמִידֵי חֲכָמִים שֶׁבְּאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל אֶת הַבַּבְלִיִּים, קָרְאוּ לָאַלֶכְּסַנְדְּרִיִּים שֶׁעוֹשִׂים מַעֲשֵׂה רַעַבְתָנוּת בַּבְלִיִּים:


הבבליים – those Kohanim who came up from Babylonia. But the Gemara (Tractate Menahot 100a) explains that they were not Babylonians but rather that they were Alexandrians, and on account that the Sages in the Land of Israel hate the Babylonians, they called the Alexandrians who performed this act of voracity (i.e., in eating the sin-offering of Yom Kippur raw) with the Babylonians.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:7:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:7:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:7:13)


**הָיוּ אוֹכְלִין אוֹתוֹ חַי.** כְּשֶׁחָל יוֹם הַכִּפּוּרִים לִהְיוֹת בְּעֶרֶב שַׁבָּת:


היו אוכלין אותו חי – when Yom Kippur fell on Friday.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:7:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:7:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:7:14)


**מִפְּנֵי שֶׁדַּעְתָּן יָפָה.** וְאֵינָן קָצִים לֶאֱכֹל הַבָּשָׂר כְּשֶׁהוּא חַי:


מפני דעתן יפה – they did not detest eating the meat when it was raw.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:8:1)


**וְאֶת הַבְּזִיכִים לְאַחַר שַׁבָּת.** וְדִינָן הָיָה לְסַדְּרָן בְּשַׁבָּת בִּשְׁעַת עֲרִיכַת הַלֶּחֶם:


ואת הבזיכים לאחר השבת – and their law was to set them (i.e., the dishes of frankincense) on the Sabbath at the time of the arranging of the bread.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:8:2)


**וְהִקְטִיר אֶת הַבְּזִיכִים בְּשַׁבָּת פְּסוּלָה.** דִּמְחֻסַּר זְמַן הוּא, שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה עַל הַשֻּׁלְחָן אֶלָּא שִׁשָּׁה יָמִים. וְהָכָא אִי אֶפְשָׁר לְתַקֵּן וְלוֹמַר יַנִּיחֶנּוּ עַד שַׁבָּת הַבָּאָה, דְּכֵיוָן דְּלֶחֶם נִסְדָּר כְּדִינוֹ בְּשַׁבָּת, קִדְּשׁוֹ שֻׁלְחָן וְשׁוּב אֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לִשְׁהוֹתוֹ אֶלָּא עַד שַׁבָּת רִאשׁוֹנָה, דְּמִפְּסִיל בְּלִינָה:


והקטיר את הבזיכים בשבת פסולה – for it was lacking time (i.e., its time to be offered had not yet arrived, for it was not on the table other than for six days, but here, it is impossible to fix it and to say, let him leave it until the next Sabbath, for since the bread was set out/arranged according to its law on the Sabbath, the table was sanctified and furthermore, she is not able to delay it other until the first Sabbath where it is invalidated by staying [there] overnight.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:8:3)


**וְאֵין חַיָּבִין עֲלֵיהֶם מִשּׁוּם פִּגּוּל.** אִם הִקְטִירָן עַל מְנָת לֶאֱכֹל הַלֶּחֶם לְמָחָר אֵינוֹ פִּגּוּל, שֶׁלֹּא קָרַב הַמַּתִּיר כְּמִצְוָתוֹ:


ואין חייבין עליהם משום פגול – if he offered them up as incense on condition to eat the bread on the morrow it is not an offering disqualified by improper intention, for he did not offer that which is permitted according to its commandment.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:8:4)


**וְלֹא מִשּׁוּם נוֹתָר.** שֶׁאֵין נוֹתָר חָל עַל הַלֶּחֶם, שֶׁהֲרֵי אֵין רָאוּי לַאֲכִילָה:


ולא משום נותר – for no remnant occurs on the [shew]bread, for it is not worthy of consumption.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:8:5)


**וְטָמֵא.** הָאוֹכְלוֹ בְּטֻמְאַת הַגּוּף אֵינוֹ בְּכָרֵת, כִּדְאָמְרִינַן בְּהַקֹּמֶץ [דַּף כה] הַנִּתָּר לַטְּהוֹרִים חַיָּבִים עָלָיו מִשּׁוּם טֻמְאָה, וְזֶה לֹא נִתַּר לַטְּהוֹרִים מֵעוֹלָם, דְּבְזִיכִים הֲווּ מַתִּירִים דִּידֵיהּ וְלֹא קָרְבוּ כְּהִלְכָתָן:


וטמא – he who eats it in a state of bodily ritual impurity is not liable for extirpation, for we state in the chapter of “Taking a Handful of the Meal Offering/ הקומץ את המנחה” (Tractate Menahot 25b) that the remnant for those who are ritually pure, they are liable for it because of ritual defilement, but this was not left over ever for those who are ritually pure, for with the dishes [of frankincense, they would permit his, but they were not offered according to their law.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:8:6)


**יַנִּיחֶנּוּ לַשַּׁבָּת הַבָּאָה.** וְיַעֲמֹד שְׁבוּעַיִם עַל הַשֻּׁלְחָן. דְּכֵיוָן דְּלֹא נִסְדַּר בְּשַׁבָּת אֵין שֻׁלְחָן מְקַדֵּשׁ עַד שֶׁתַּגִּיעַ שַׁבָּת, וּמֵאוֹתוֹ שַׁבָּת יָכוֹל לְהַשְׁהוֹתָן שִׁבְעַת יָמִים:


יניחנו לשבת הבאה – and it would stand for two weeks on the table, for since it was not set down/arranged on Shabbat, the table is not sanctified until Shabbat arrives, and from that Shabbat he can have it them stay there for seven days.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:8:7)


**שֶׁאֲפִלּוּ הִיא עַל הַשֻּׁלְחָן יָמִים רַבִּים.** קֹדֶם הַשַּׁבָּת:


שאפילו היה על שלחן ימים רבים – prior to the Sabbath.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:8:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:8:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:8:8)


**אֵין בְּכָךְ כְּלוּם.** שֶׁאֵין הַשֻּׁלְחָן מְקַדְּשׁוֹ עַד הַשַּׁבָּת, הִלְכָּךְ לֹא מִיפְסָל בְּלִינָה לְאַחַר הַשַּׁבָּת:


אין בכך – [there is] nothing [regarding it] for the table does not sanctify it until the Sabbath, therefore it is not invalidated through staying over after the Sabbath.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:9:1)


**אֵין פָּחוֹת מִשְּׁנַיִם.** מִשְּׁנֵי יָמִים מִשֶּׁנֶּאֱפוּ:


אין פחות משנים – from two days from when they were baked.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:9:2)


**נֶאֱכָל לִשְׁלֹשָׁה.** שֶׁאֲפָאוּם בְּעֶרֶב שַׁבָּת. לְפִי שֶׁאֵין אֲפִיָּתָן דּוֹחָה לֹא שַׁבָּת וְלֹא יוֹם טוֹב:


נאכל לשלשה – that he baked them on Friday/the Eve of the Sabbath, because their baking does not supersede neither the Sabbath nor a Festival.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:9:3)


**לְתִשְׁעָה.** בְּשַׁבָּת שְׁנִיָּה, שֶׁהִיא תְּשִׁיעִי לַאֲפִיָּתוֹ:


לתשעה – on the second Sabbath, which is nine [days] from its being baked.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:9:4)


**לַעֲשָׂרָה.** שֶׁנֶּאֱפוּ בַּחֲמִישִׁי בְּשַׁבָּת:


לעשרה – that they wee baked on a Thursday.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:9:5)


**שְׁנֵי יָמִים טוֹבִים שֶׁל רֹאשׁ הַשָּׁנָה.** קֹדֶם לַשַּׁבָּת. נֶאֱפָה בִּרְבִיעִי וְנֶאֱכָל בְּשַׁבָּת שְׁנִיָּה, הֲרֵי לְאַחַד עָשָׂר. וְאִם תֹּאמַר, הֲרֵי שְׁנֵים עָשָׂר נִינְהוּ, דְּהָא אוֹתָהּ שַׁבָּת הָוֵי יוֹם הַכִּפּוּרִים וְאֵין הַלֶּחֶם נֶאֱכָל עַד לְמוֹצָאֵי שַׁבָּת. הָא לֹא קַשְׁיָא, דִּלְעִנְיַן אֲכִילַת קָדָשִׁים הַלַּיְלָה הוֹלֵךְ אַחַר הַיּוֹם שֶׁעָבַר, הִלְכָּךְ מִקְרֵי אַחַד עָשָׂר:


שני ימים טובים של ראש השנה – prior to Shabbat. It was baked on Wednesday and consumed on the second Sabbath, that is eleven [days], but if you say that they are twelve days, for that Sabbath is Yom Kippur, and the bread is not eaten until Saturday night. This is not difficult = for regarding the matter of consuming the Holy Things, the night follows after the day that passed, therefore, it is called eleven [days].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:9:6)


**וְאֵינוֹ דּוֹחֶה לֹא אֶת הַשַּׁבָּת וְלֹא אֶת יוֹם טוֹב.** לְפִי שֶׁאֵין אוֹפִים וּמְבַשְּׁלִים בְּיוֹם טוֹב אֶלָּא מַה שֶּׁאוֹכֵל בְּאוֹתוֹ הַיּוֹם בִּלְבַד:


ואינו דוחה לא את השבת ולא את יום טוב – because they don’t bake or cook on the Festival other than what one eats on that day alone.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 11:9:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:9:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/11:9:7)


**רַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל אוֹמֵר וְכוּ'.** וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל:


רבן שמעון בן גמליאל אומר וכו' – but the Halakha is not according to Rabban Shimon ben Gamaliel.



## Chapter 12


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 12:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/12:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/12:1:1)


**הַמְּנָחוֹת וְהַנְּסָכִים.** עַד שֶׁלֹּא קָדְשׁוּ בַּכְּלִי. אֵינָן קְדוֹשִׁים קְדֻשַּׁת הַגּוּף, אֶלָּא קְדֻשַּׁת דָּמִים, שֶׁצָּרִיךְ לִפְדּוֹתָן וְהַדָּמִים קְדוֹשִׁים. וְדַוְקָא כְּשֶׁנִּטְמְאוּ יֵשׁ לָהֶן פִּדְיוֹן אִם לֹא קָדְשׁוּ בַּכְּלִי, אֲבָל לֹא נִטְמְאוּ, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא קָדְשׁוּ בַּכְּלִי אֵין פּוֹדִים אוֹתָן:


המנחות והנכסים. עד שלא קדשו בכלי – they are not sanctified as an object which is consecrated as such (i.e., irredeemable), but rather that which is consecrated for its value (i.e., redeemable), that one must redeem them and the monies are holy. And especially, when they were impure, they have redemption, before one has consecrated them in a [consecrated] utensil, but if they were defiled even though they had not been consecrated in a [consecrated] utensil, we don’t redeem them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 12:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/12:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/12:1:2)


**הָעוֹפוֹת וְהָעֵצִים וְהַלְּבוֹנָה וּכְלֵי שָׁרֵת.** שֶׁנִּטְמְאוּ: **אֵין לָהֶם פִּדְיוֹן.** שֶׁלֹּא נֶאֱמַר פִּדְיוֹן בִּקְדֻשַּׁת הַגּוּף אֶלָּא בִּבְהֵמָה בַּעֲלַת מוּם, דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא כז) וְאִם כָּל בְּהֵמָה טְמֵאָה, אֲשֶׁר לֹא יַקְרִיבוּ מִמֶּנָּה קָרְבָּן לַה', וְהֶעֱרִיךְ הַכֹּהֵן אֹתָהּ וְכוּ'. וּבְבַעֲלֵי מוּמִים שֶׁנִּפְדּוּ הַכָּתוּב מְדַבֵּר, דְּאִי בִּבְהֵמָה טְמֵאָה מַמָּשׁ, כְּשֶׁהוּא אוֹמֵר (שם) וְאִם בַּבְּהֵמָה הַטְּמֵאָה וּפָדָה בְּעֶרְכְּךָ, הֲרֵי בְּהֵמָה טְמֵאָה אֲמוּרָה. וְאַשְׁמוֹעִינַן בְּמַתְנִיתִין דְּאַף עַל גַּב דְּאַשְׁכְּחַן בְּקָדוֹשׁ קְדֻשַּׁת הַגּוּף וְנָפַל בֵּיהּ מוּמָא דְּמִפְרִיק, הָעוֹפוֹת וְהָעֵצִים וְהַלְּבוֹנָה וּכְלֵי שָׁרֵת דְּקַדְּשֵׁי קְדֻשַּׁת הַגּוּף וְנִטְמְאוּ לֹא מִפְרְקֵי:


העופות והעצים והלבונה וכלי שרת שנטמאו אין להם פדיון – for redemption that is not stated with an object which is consecrated as such (i.e., irredeemable) other than with a animal having a blemish, as it is written (Leviticus 27:11-12): “If [the vow concerns] any impure animal that may not be brought as an offering to the LORD…and the priest shall assess it.” And the Scriptural verse is speaking about animals with blemishes that had been redeemed, for if it was an actual impure animal, when it (i.e., the Biblical verse) states (Leviticus 27:27): “But if it is of impure animals, it may be ransomed as its assessment, [with one-fifth added; if it is not redeemed, it shall be sold at its assessment],” behold, it is stated regarding an impure animal, and our Mishnah comes to inform us that even though we find with something holy an object which is consecrated as such (i.e., irredeemable) and a blemish befell it we redeem it, whereas the birds and the wood and the frankincense and the utensils of service that are sanctified as objects consecrated as such (i.e., irredeemable) and are made impure, we don’t redeem them.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 12:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/12:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/12:2:1)


**מַה שֶּׁהֵבִיא הֵבִיא.** דְּאָמְרִינַן לֹא לְשֵׁם נִדְרוֹ הֱבִיאָהּ, אֶלָּא נְדָבָה אַחֶרֶת הִיא זוֹ:


מה שהביא הביא (see also Tractate Menahot, Chapter 5, Mishnah 8) – for we state that it was not for the purpose of his vow that the brought, but rather It is a different free-will offering/donation.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 12:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/12:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/12:2:2)


**זוֹ לְהָבִיא בְמַחֲבַת.** שֶׁהָיְתָה עִשָּׂרוֹן סֹלֶת מֻנַּחַת לְפָנָיו, וְאָמַר, זוֹ לְהָבִיא בַּמַּחֲבַת וְכוּ':


זו להביא במחבת – for there was an Issaron/one-tenth of an Ephah of choice four placed before him, and he said, [Lo, I pledge myself to bring this choice flour as a meal-offering prepared] in a pan/frying pan, etc.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 12:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/12:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/12:2:3)


**הֲרֵי זוֹ פְסוּלָה.** דִּקְבָעָהּ לַכְּלִי שֶׁהִזְכִּיר בָּהּ וְאֵין יָכוֹל לְשַׁנּוֹתָהּ לִכְלִי אַחֵר:


הרי זו פסולה – for he appointed it for the utensil that he mentioned and he is not able to change it to t


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 12:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/12:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/12:2:4)


**אֵלּוּ לְהָבִיא בִּכְלִי אֶחָד.** אֵלּוּ שֶׁהָיוּ מֻנָּחִים לְפָנָיו:


אלו להביא בכלי אחד – these that were placed/lying before him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 12:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/12:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/12:2:5)


**הֲרֵי אֵלּוּ פְסוּלִים.** דְּהֵיכָא דְּנָדַר בִּכְלִי אֶחָד וְהֵבִיא בִּשְׁנֵי כֵּלִים מַפְרִישׁ מִמֶּנָּה שְׁנֵי קֳמָצִים, וְהוּא לֹא נָדַר אֶלָּא קֹמֶץ אֶחָד. וְעוֹד, שֶׁמִּנְחָה חֲסֵרָה הִיא בְּכָל כְּלִי וּכְלִי. וְהֵיכָא דְּנָדַר בִּשְׁנֵי כֵּלִים וְהֵבִיא בִּכְלִי אֶחָד הַוְיָא מִנְחָה יְתֵרָה וּמִעֵט בַּקֳּמָצִין, שֶׁהוּא בֵּרֵר לָהּ שְׁנֵי קֳמָצִין וְלֹא קָמַץ אֶלָּא אֶחָד:


הרי אלו פסולים – for where he made a vow for one utensil and brought in two utensils, he sets aside/dedicates from it two handfuls, but he did not vow other than one handful. And further, that the meal -offering is lacking in each and every utensil. But where he vowed for two utensils, but brought [only] for one utensil, it would be an extra meal-offering but where he diminished/reduced the handfuls, for he sifted/selected for it two handfuls, but only took one handful.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 12:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/12:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/12:2:6)


**אָמְרוּ לוֹ בִּכְלִי אֶחָד נָדָרְתָּ.** וְלֹא חָשׁ לְדִבְרֵיהֶם וְהִקְרִיבָהּ בִּשְׁנֵי כֵּלִים, פְּסוּלִים, וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּלֹא אָמַר אֵלּוּ לְהָבִיא בִּכְלִי אֶחָד. מִשּׁוּם דְּהַשְׁתָּא לֵיכָּא לְמֵימַר לְשׁוּם נְדָבָה אַחֶרֶת אַיְתֵי לַהּ, דְּכֵיוָן דְּאָמְרֵי לֵיהּ בִּכְלִי אֶחָד נָדַרְתָּ הֲוָה לֵיהּ לְמֵימַר לְהוּ אֲנָא מִשּׁוּם נֶדֶר אַחֲרִינָא מַיְתִינָא לַהּ:


אמרו לו בכלי אחד נדרת – but he was not troubled by their words and he offered it in two utensils, it is invalid, but even though that he did not state that these would be brought in one utensil. Because that now, one cannot say that I have brought another free-will offering, for since they said to him that he made a votive offering in one utensil, he could have said to them: “I because of another vow bring this.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 12:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/12:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/12:2:7)


**כִּשְׁתֵּי מְנָחוֹת שֶׁנִּתְעָרָבוּ.** דְּאָמְרִינַן בְּפֶרֶק הַקּוֹמֵץ, אִם יָכוֹל לִקְמֹץ מִזּוֹ בִּפְנֵי עַצְמָהּ וּמִזּוֹ בִּפְנֵי עַצְמָהּ כְּשֵׁרוֹת, וְאִם לָאו פְּסוּלוֹת. וְהָא דְּקָתָנֵי לְעֵיל אֵלּוּ לְהָבִיא בִּשְׁנֵי כֵּלִים וְהֵבִיא בִּכְלִי אֶחָד פְּסוּלוֹת, מַיְרֵי כְּגוֹן דְּלֹא יָכוֹל לִקְמֹץ מִכָּל אַחַת בִּפְנֵי עַצְמָהּ:


כשתי מנחות שנתערבו – for we said in the chapter of the “The One Who Takes a Handful”/הקומץ (Chapter 3, Mishnah 3 of Tractate Menahot)that if he is able to take a handful from this one on its own and from that one on its own, they are kosher/fit, but if not they are invalid. But that which is taught above [in our Mishnah]: “these which are to be brought in two utensils but he brought them in one utensil, they are invalid,” speaks, as for example, that he was not able to take a handful from each one of them on its own.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 12:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/12:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/12:3:1)


**הֲרֵי עָלַי מִנְחָה מִן הַשְּׂעֹרִים יָבִיא מִן הַחִטִּים.** שֶׁאֵין מִנְחַת נְדָבָה בָּאָה אֶלָּא מִן הַחִטִּים. וּכְגוֹן שֶׁאָמַר אִלּוּ הָיִיתִי יוֹדֵעַ שֶׁאֵין מְבִיאִים מִנְחָה מִן הַשְּׂעֹרִים לֹא הָיִיתִי נוֹדֵר אֶלָּא מִן הַחִטִּים. אֲבָל אִם אָמַר לֹא הָיִיתִי נוֹדֵר כְּלוּם, אֵינוֹ חַיָּב כְּלוּם:


הרי עלי מנחה מן השעורים יביא מן החטים – for the free-will offerings of meal offerings do not come other than from wheat, and as for example that he said, “Had I known that we don’t bring a meal-offering from barley, I would not have made a votive offering other than from wheat.” But if he said, “I would not vow anything,” he is not liable for anything.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 12:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/12:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/12:3:2)


**יָבִיא סֹלֶת.** דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא ב) סֹלֶת יִהְיֶה קָרְבָּנוֹ:


יביא סלת – as it is written (Leviticus 2:1): “[When a person presents an offering of grain to the LORD,] his offering shall be of choice flour.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 12:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/12:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/12:3:3)


**רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן פּוֹטֵר.** רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן סָבַר בִּגְמַר דְּבָרָיו אָדָם נִתְפָּס, וְהוֹאִיל וּגְמַר דְּבָרָיו שֶׁלֹּא כְּדִין הַמִּנְחָה, אֵינוֹ חַיָּב כְּלוּם. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


ר' שמעון פוטר – Rabbi Shimon holds that a person is made responsible for the his concluding words, for since he concluded his words not in accordance with the law of the meal-offering, he is not liable for anything. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon (see Talmud Menahot 103b).



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 12:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/12:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/12:4:1)


**שֶׁכֵּן צִבּוּר מְבִיאִים וְכוּ'.** שְׁלֹשָׁה עֶשְׂרוֹנִים לַפָּר לִשְׁלֹשָׁה עָשָׂר פָּרִים הֵם תִּשְׁעָה וּשְׁלֹשִׁים עֶשְׂרוֹנִים, וְעִשָּׂרוֹן לַכֶּבֶשׂ לְאַרְבָּעָה עָשָׂר כְּבָשִׂים הֵם אַרְבָּעָה עָשָׂר עֶשְׂרוֹנִים, וּשְׁנֵי עֶשְׂרוֹנִים לָאַיִל לִשְׁנֵי הָאֵילִים הֵם אַרְבָּעָה עֶשְׂרוֹנִים, הֲרֵי חֲמִשִּׁים וְשִׁבְעָה, וְלִשְׁנֵי תְמִידִים שְׁנַיִם, וְלִשְׁנֵי כִבְשֵׂי מוּסַף שַׁבָּת שְׁנַיִם, הֲרֵי שִׁשִּׁים וְאֶחָד. וְאָמַר לָהֶם רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן, וְכִי כָּל אוֹתָן שֶׁל אוֹתוֹ יוֹם בִּכְלִי אֶחָד הָיוּ, וַהֲלֹא אֵלּוּ לַפָּרִים וְאֵלּוּ לָאֵילִים וְלַכְּבָשִׂים, וְאִי מְעָרֵב לְהוּ מִפְּסִיל, שֶׁמִּנְחַת פָּרִים בְּלִילָתָן עָבָה, שְׁנֵי לֻגִּים לְעִשָּׂרוֹן, חֲצִי הַהִין לִשְׁלֹשָׁה עֶשְׂרוֹנִים, וּמִנְחַת כְּבָשִׂים בְּלִילָתָן רַכָּה, רְבִיעִית הַהִין לְעִשָּׂרוֹן דְּהַיְנוּ שְׁלֹשָׁה לֻגִּים, וְהֵן בּוֹלְעוֹת זוֹ מִזּוֹ, וְנִמְצֵאת זוֹ חֲסֵרָה וְזוֹ יְתֵרָה. אֶלָּא מִפְּנֵי מָה אֵין מֵבִיא יָחִיד שִׁשִּׁים וְאֶחָד עֶשְׂרוֹנִים בִּכְלִי אֶחָד, דְּעַד שִׁשִּׁים יָכוֹל לְהִבָּלֵל בְּלֹג אֶחָד. וְהַאי תַּנָּא סָבַר לַהּ כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר בֶּן יַעֲקֹב דְּאָמַר בְּפֶרֶק שְׁתֵּי מִדּוֹת אֲפִלּוּ מִנְחָה שֶׁל שִׁשִּׁים עֶשְׂרוֹנִים אֵין לָהּ אֶלָּא לֹג. וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא נִבְלְלָה כֻּלָּהּ, כֵּיוָן שֶׁרְאוּיָה לְהִבָּלֵל אֵין הַבְּלִילָה מְעַכֶּבֶת, דְּהָכִי קַיְמָא לָן דְּכָל הָרָאוּי לְבִילָה אֵין בִּילָה מְעַכֶּבֶת בּוֹ, וְכָל שֶׁאֵינוֹ רָאוּי לְבִילָה בִּילָה מְעַכֶּבֶת בּוֹ:


שמן צבור מביאים וכו' – three Esronim (i.e., three-tenths of an Omer) for a bull, for thirteen bulls they are thirty-nine Esronim, and an Issaron (i.e., one-tenth of an Omer) for a lamb; for fourteen lambs they are fourteen Esronim, and two Esronim; for two rams they are four Esronim, that is fifty seven, and for the two daily offerings/Tamidim, two, and for the two lambs for the Musaf/additional offering for Shabbat is two, this is sixty one. But Rabbi Shimon said to them: But were all of them for that self-same day in one utensil, but weren’t these for bulls and these for rams and lambs, for if he mixed/combined them, he invalidated them, for the meal-offering of bulls – their mixture was thick, two LOG (i.e., a LOG equals the volume of six eggs bulk) for an Issaron, one-half of a Hin for thee Esronim, but for the meal offering of lambs, their mixture was thin, one-quarter of a Hin for an Issaron, which is three LOG, and they absorb one from another , and it is found that this one is less and that one has more. But for what reason does an individual not bring sixty-one Esronim in one utensil, for up to sixty, one can thoroughly mix in one LOG. But this Tanna/teacher holds like Rabbi Eliezer ben Yaakov who stated in the chapter [nine, Mishnah three of Tractate Menahot] “Two Measures”/שתי מדות – even a meal offering of sixty Esronim, it does not have any ought one Log. But even though it was all mixed, since it was appropriate to be mixed, the mixing does not make it indispensable., for such we hold that everything wherever from the proportions and properties of the mixture a perfect fusion is possible, the real act of mixing thoroughly is not indispensable (see Talmud Hullin 83b), but that which is not appropriate for a thorough mixture, the thorough mixture makes it indispensable.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 12:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/12:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/12:4:2)


**וְשִׁשִּׁים וְאֶחָד אֵין נִבְלָלִים.** בִּתְמִיָּה:


וששים ואחד אין נבללים – in astonishment!


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 12:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/12:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/12:4:3)


**קֻרְטוֹב.** רְבִיעִית שֶׁל רְבִיעִית הַלֹּג:


קרטוב (1/64th of a LOG or 3/32 of an egg-bulk) – a fourth of a fourth of a LOG.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 12:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/12:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/12:4:4)


**אֵין מִתְנַדְּבִים לֹג.** יַיִן לַנְּסָכִים. שֶׁלֹּא מָצִינוּ מִנְחַת נְסָכִים שֶׁל לֹג אֶחָד וְלֹא שֶׁל שְׁנַיִם וְלֹא שֶׁל חֲמִשָּׁה:


אין מתנדבים לוג – wine for libations. For we have not found a meal-offering of libations of one LOG and not of two nor of five [of wine].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 12:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/12:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/12:4:5)


**אֲבָל מִתְנַדְּבִים שְׁלֹשָׁה.** לֻגִּים, דַּחֲזוּ לְכֶבֶשׂ: **וְאַרְבָּעָה.** דַּחֲזוּ לְאַיִל: **וְשִׁשָּׁה.** לֻגִּים, שֶׁהוּא חֲצִי הַהִין, דַּחֲזוּ לְפַר: **וּמִשִּׁשָּׁה וּלְמַעְלָה.** מִתְנַדְּבִים, דְּשִׁבְעָה חֲזוּ שְׁלֹשָׁה מִנַּיְהוּ לְכֶבֶשׂ וְאַרְבָּעָה לְאַיִל, וּשְׁמֹנָה חֲזוּ לִשְׁנֵי אֵילִים, וְתִשְׁעָה חֲזוּ לְכֶבֶשׂ וּפַר, עֲשָׂרָה חֲזוּ לְאַיִל וּפַר, אַחַד עָשָׂר חֲזוּ לִשְׁנֵי אֵילִים וּלְכֶבֶשׂ. וְכֵן לְעוֹלָם:


אבל מתנדבים ג' לוגים – that are appropriate for a lamb. And four [LOG] that are appropriate for a ram. And six LOG which is one-half of Hin that are appropriate for a bull. But from six [LOG] and above, they volunteer as a free-will offering, for seven are appropriate for three of them for a lamb and four for a ram, and eight are appropriate for two rams, and nine are appropriate for a lamb and a bull, ten are appropriate for a ram and a bull, eleven are appropriate for two rams and a lamb. And similarly throughout.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 12:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/12:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/12:5:1)


**מִתְנַדְּבִים יַיִן.** בְּלֹא סֹלֶת וְשֶׁמֶן, וּמְנַסְּכוֹ לַשִּׁיתִין בִּפְנֵי עַצְמוֹ:


מתנדבים יין – without choice flour and oil, and we offer it as a libation for sixty on its own.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 12:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/12:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/12:5:2)


**וְאֵין מִתְנַדְּבִים שֶׁמֶן.** בְּלֹא סֹלֶת וְיַיִן:


ואין מתנדבים שמן – without choice flour and wine.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 12:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/12:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/12:5:3)


**מִתְנַדְּבִין שָׁמֶן.** וְקֻמְצוֹ וּמַקְטִיר הַקֹּמֶץ וְשִׁירַיִם נֶאֱכָלִים:


מתנדבין שמן – and he takes a fistful and offers up the handful [of the meal-offering] which is offered up as incense but the remnants are consumed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 12:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/12:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/12:5:4)


**שֶׁכֵּן הוּא קָרֵב חוֹבָתוֹ בִפְנֵי עַצְמוֹ.** עִם חוֹבָתוֹ הוּא קָרֵב בִּפְנֵי עַצְמוֹ. אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהוּא בָּא חוֹבָה עִם הַמִּנְחָה, אֵינוֹ מְעַכֵּב הַמִּנְחָה:


שכן הוא קרב חובתו בפני עצמו – with his obligation it is offered on its own. Even though it comes as an obligation with the meal offering, it is not indispensable to the meal-offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 12:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/12:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/12:5:5)


**תֹּאמַר בַּשֶּׁמֶן כוּ'.** דְּכֵיוָן דְּלֹא אַשְׁכְּחַן בֵּיהּ דְּלֵיתֵי בִּכְלִי בִּפְנֵי עַצְמוֹ, הַשְׁתָּא נַמִּי לֹא לֵיתֵי:


תאמר בשמן כו' – for since we do not find it in it, for it is not in a utensil on its own, now also it is not brought.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 12:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/12:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/12:5:6)


**אֲפִלּוּ פְרִידָה אֶחָת.** תּוֹר אֶחָד, אוֹ בֶּן יוֹנָה אֶחָד, מִתְנַדְּבִים בֵּין שְׁנַיִם. וַהֲלָכָה שֶׁמִּתְנַדְּבִים יַיִן וְשֶׁמֶן בִּפְנֵי עַצְמָן. וּמִתְנַדְּבִים מִנְחַת נְסָכִים בֵּין נִסְכֵּי פַּר בֵּין נִסְכֵּי אַיִל וְכֶבֶשׂ. וְכָל הַקָּרְבָּנוֹת בָּאִים בִּנְדָבָה בְּשֻׁתָּפוּת, חוּץ מִן הַמִּנְחָה, לְפִי שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בָּהּ נֶפֶשׁ, דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא ב) וְנֶפֶשׁ כִּי תַקְרִיב קָרְבַּן מִנְחָה:


אפילו פרידה אחת – one turtle-dove or one pigeon, we make a free-will offering among the two [of them]. And the Halakah is that we make free-will offerings of wine and oil on their own, and we make a free-will offering of a meal-offering of libations, whether the libations of a bull or whether the libations of a ram or a lamb. And all of the sacrifices come as a jointly owned free-will offering, except for the meal-offering, because it is stated regarding it "נפש"/an individual soul, as it is written (Leviticus 2:1): “When a person presents an offering of grain [to the LORD, his offering shall be of choice flour].”



## Chapter 13


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 13:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:1:1)


**הֲרֵי עָלַי עִשָּׂרוֹן. פֵּרַשְׁתִּי.** כַּמָּה עֶשְׂרוֹנִים אָבִיא וְאֵינִי יוֹדֵעַ כַּמָּה אָמַרְתִּי:


הרי עלי עשרון. פירשתי – I will bring several Esronim but I don’t know how many I stated.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 13:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:1:2)


**יָבִיא שִׁשִּׁים עֶשְׂרוֹנִים.** דְּאִי בְּצִיר מֵהָכִי נָדַר, לֹא אִכְפַּת לֵיהּ, דְּמַתְנֵי וְאוֹמֵר מַה שֶּׁפֵּרַשְׁתִּי יְהֵא לְנִדְרִי וְהַשְּׁאָר יְהֵא לִנְדָבָה. וּבִטְפֵי מֵהָכִי לֵיכָּא לְסַפּוֹקֵי, דְּאֵין מִנְחָה אַחַת יְתֵרָה מִשִּׁשִּׁים עִשָּׂרוֹן:


יביא ששים עשרונים – for if he vowed less than this, it does not matter to him, for it teaches [in the Mishnah] and he states: “What that I expressly said will be for my vow, and the rest will be for a free-will offering.” But more than this one does not have to supply, for one meal-offering is not larger than sixty Issarons (see Tractate Menahot, Chapter 12, Mishnah 4).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 13:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:1:3)


**הֲרֵי עָלַי מִנְחָה.** הָאוֹמֵר הֲרֵי עָלַי מִנְחָה סְתָם:


הרי עלי מנחה – a person who states, “I pledge myself to bring a mere grain-offering.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 13:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:1:4)


**יָבִיא אֵיזוֹ שֶׁיִּרְצֶה.** מֵחָמֵשׁ מְנָחוֹת:


יביא איזה שירצה – from the five meal offerings (explained in the Torah: choice flour, in a deep and covered pan, in a pan – without a lid, baked loaves and baked wafers).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 13:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:1:5)


**מְיֻחֶדֶת.** שֶׁנִּקְרֵאת מִנְחָה סְתָם וְאֵין לָהּ שֵׁם לְוַוי. וְכָל שְׁאָר מְנָחוֹת יֵשׁ לָהֶם שֵׁם לְוַוי, מִנְחַת מַחֲבַת, מִנְחַת מַרְחֶשֶׁת, מִנְחַת מַאֲפֶה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


מיוחדת – which is called a mere meal/grain-offering, but it does not have a differentiating epithet, for all of the rest of the meal/grain-offerings have a differentiating epithet, the meal offering of the pan-without a lid, the meal-offering of the deep covered pan, the meal-offering that is baked. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 13:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:2:1)


**מִנְחָה מִין הַמִּנְחָה.** הָאוֹמֵר הֲרֵי עָלַי מִנְחָה, אוֹ מִין מִנְחָה:


מנחה מין המנחה – he who says, “I pledge myself to bring a meal-offering,” or a kind of meal-offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 13:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:2:2)


**יָבִיא אֶחָת.** מִן הַמְּנָחוֹת הָאֲמוּרוֹת בַּפָּרָשָׁה:


יביא אחת – from the meal-offerings that are mentioned in the portion (i.e., choice flour, in a deep and covered pan, in a pan -without a lid, baked loaves and/or baked wafers).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 13:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:2:3)


**מְנָחוֹת.** עָלַי, אוֹ שֶׁאוֹמֵר מִין מְנָחוֹת עָלַי, יָבִיא שְׁתֵּי מְנָחוֹת מִמִּין אֶחָד:


מנחות – are upon me [as a pledge to bring] or that he states: “from a kind/species of the meal offerings are upon me,” he would bring two meal-offerings from one kind.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 13:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:2:4)


**פֵּרַשְׁתִּי.** מִינֵי מְנָחוֹת שֶׁאָבִיא, וְאֵינִי יוֹדֵעַ כַּמָּה מִינִים הֵם שֶׁנָּדַרְתִּי לְהָבִיא:


פירשתי – kids of meal-offerings that I would bring, but I don’t know how many kinds they are that I vowed to bring.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 13:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:2:5)


**מֵבִיא חֲמִשְׁתָּן.** מִנְחַת סֹלֶת, מִנְחַת מַרְחֶשֶׁת, מִנְחַת מַחֲבַת, מִנְחַת מַאֲפֵה תַּנּוּר, וְהִיא בָּאָה שְׁנֵי מִינִים, חַלּוֹת וּרְקִיקִין:


מביא חמשתן – a meal-offering of choice flour, a meal-offering in a deep and covered pan, a meal- offering in a pan-without a lid, a meal-offering of that which is baked in an oven and it comes from two kinds – loaves and wafers.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 13:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:2:6)


**פֵּרַשְׁתִּי מִנְחָה שֶׁל עֶשְׂרוֹנִים.** בִּכְלִי אֶחָד, וְאֵינִי יוֹדֵעַ כַּמָּה עֶשְׂרוֹנִים קָבַעְתִּי בָּהּ:


... 6) Rebbi says: Since it says 'mincha [shel esronim],' it means he specified one [amount]. Perhaps he specified to bring a mincha of one issaron; if he brings 60 issaron in place of [lit. 'in the vessel of] one issaron, it could be an extraneous mincha. Rebbi is of the opinion that since he specified exaclt how big the mincha will be, [he must bring that exact one], therefore he must bring 60 menachot from 1 through 60, one of 1 issaron, one of two issarons, one of three, one of four, until 60. He will find himself bringing in total 1830 issaron of flour, and for certain one of them will fulfill his vow, and it is not a Nedava. The halacha is not according to Rebbi. [If you specified, and forget what you specified, you bring only a 60 issaron mincha.]


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 13:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:2:7)


**יָבִיא שִׁשִּׁים עִשָּׂרוֹן.** דִּטְפֵי מֵהָכִי לֵיכָּא לְסַפּוֹקֵי. וְאִי בָּצִיר מֵהָכִי נָדַר לֹא אִכְפַּת לָן, דְּמַתְנֶה וְאוֹמֵר כַּמָּה שֶׁפֵּרַשְׁתִּי יְהֵא לְנִדְרִי וְהַשְּׁאָר יְהֵא לִנְדָבָה:


יביא ששים עשרון – for more than this he is not able to supply. But if he vowed less than this, if does not matter to us, for he makes a condition and states how many he specified will be for my vow and the rest will be a free-will offering (see also, Mishnah 8 in this chapter).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 13:2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:2:8)


**רַבִּי אוֹמֵר.** כֵּיוָן דְּאָמַר מִנְחָה דְּמַשְׁמַע חֲדָא, אַלְמָא בִּכְלִי אֶחָד קְבָעָהּ, וְאִם יָבִיא שִׁשִּׁים בִּכְלִי אֶחָד, שֶׁמָּא בְּצִיר מֵהָכִי נָדַר וְהָוֵי מִנְחָה יְתֵרָה, דְּאִית לֵיהּ לְרַבִּי דִּקְבִיעוּתָא דְּמָנָא מִלְּתָא הִיא, הִלְכָּךְ יָבִיא שִׁשִּׁים מְנָחוֹת מֵאַחַת וְעַד שִׁשִּׁים, חֲדָא שֶׁל עִשָּׂרוֹן אֶחָד, וַחֲדָא שֶׁל שְׁנַיִם, וַחֲדָא שֶׁל שְׁלֹשָׁה, וַחֲדָא שֶׁל אַרְבָּעָה, עַד שִׁשִּׁים. שֶׁנִּמְצָא מֵבִיא בֵּין הַכֹּל אֶלֶף שְׁמֹנֶה מֵאוֹת וּשְׁלֹשִׁים עִשָּׂרוֹן, דְּוַדַּאי חֲדָא מִנַּיְהוּ נָדַר. וְאִינָךְ נְדָבָה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי:


רבי אומר – since he said “a meal-offering,” that implies one, so we see, he established it in one utensil, but if he would bring sixty in one utensil, perhaps he vowed less than this, it would be an additional meal-offering, for Rabbi holds that the appointment of a portion is substantial, therefore, he should bring sixty meal-offerings from one until sixty, one of one Isaron, and one of two {Esronim] and one of three [Esronim] and one of four [Esronim] until sixty. That it is found that he brings among them all one thousand eight hundred and thirty Esronim, for certainly vowed one of them, but it is not a free-will offering. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi [Judah the Prince].



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 13:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:3:1)


**לֹא יִפְחֹת מִשְּׁנֵי גְזִירִין.** שְׁתֵּי בְּקַעִיּוֹת גְּדוֹלוֹת. דְּמִעוּט עֵצִים שְׁנַיִם:


לא יפחות משני זגירין – two large logs, for the least of wood is two.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 13:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:3:2)


**וְהַמַּעֲלֶה אֶת הַקֹּמֶץ בַּחוּץ חַיָּב.** כָּרֵת, דְּהַעֲלָאָה הִיא. וּבְהַאי כְּלָלָא הָוֵי נַמִּי הַמַּעֲלֶה קֹמֶץ בִּפְנִים דְּהַקְטָרָה מְעַלְיְתָא הִיא, הִלְכָּךְ חֲשִׁיב חֲמִשָּׁה קֳמָצִים וְתוּ לֹא, וְלֹא חֲשִׁיב לְהַאי דְּהַמַּעֲלֶה קֹמֶץ בִּפְנִים לְקֹמֶץ שִׁשִּׁי:


והמעלה את הקומץ בחוץ חייב – [is liable for] extirpation, for it is an offering on the altar. But with this general rule it is also offering up a handful inside for the burning on the altar is valid; therefore, five handfuls are considered and not any more, for the person who offers up a handful inside is not considered as a sixth handful.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 13:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:3:3)


**וּשְׁנֵי בְזִיכִים.** שֶׁל לֶחֶם הַפָּנִים:


ושני בזיכים – of shewbread.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 13:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:4:1)


**לֹא יִפְחֹת מִדִּינַר זָהָב.** וְהוּא שֶׁיֹּאמַר מַטְבֵּעַ שֶׁל זָהָב. דְּאִי לֹא, דִּלְמָא נִסְכָּא דְּדַהֲבָא קָאָמַר, דְּהַיְנוּ חֲתִיכָה שֶׁל זָהָב:


לא יפחות מדינר זהב – and he that would state a gold coin. For if not, perhaps he states a piece of gold bar, that is a piece of gold.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 13:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:4:2)


**נְחֹשֶׁת לֹא יִפְחֹת מִמָּעָה כָסֶף.** שֶׁיָּבִיא נְחֹשֶׁת שֶׁשָּׁוָה מָעָה כֶּסֶף:


נחושת לא יפחות ממעה כסף – that he should bring copper that is worth a Maah of silver (i.e., a Maah is a small silver coin worth thirty-two peruta or one-sixth of a dinar = the Biblical gerah).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 13:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:4:3)


**פֵּרַשְׁתִּי.** כָּךְ וְכָךְ זָהָב:


פירשתי – such and such [an amount of] gold.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 13:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:4:4)


**וְאֵינִי יוֹדֵעַ כַּמָּה פֵּרַשְׁתִּי.** יָבִיא כָּל כָּךְ עַד שֶׁיֵּדַע בְּעַצְמוֹ שֶׁמֵּעוֹלָם לֹא נִתְכַּוֵּן לְכָל כָּךְ:


ואיני יודע כמה פירשתי – he should bring so much until that he would himself would know that he never intended for so much.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 13:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:5:1)


**הֲרֵי עָלַי יַיִן.** לַנְּסָכִים:


הרי עלי יין – for libations.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 13:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:5:2)


**לֹא יִפְחֹת מִשְּׁלֹשָׁה לֻגִּין.** שֶׁהֵן פְּחוּתִים שֶׁבַּנְּסָכִים. רְבִיעִית הַהִין לַכֶּבֶשׂ, תְּלָתָא לֻגִּין הֲווּ, שֶׁהִין שְׁנֵים עָשָׂר לֻגִּין:


לא יפחות משלשה לוגין – for they are the least that are of the libations. A quarter of a Hin for a lamb are three Logs, for a Hin is twelve LOG.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 13:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:5:3)


**שֶׁמֶן לֹא יִפְחֹת מִלֹּג.** שֶׁהַפָּחוּת שֶׁבַּמִּנְחָה עִשָּׂרוֹן סֹלֶת, וְהִיא טְעוּנָה לֹג שֶׁמֶן:


שמן לא יפחות מלוג – that the least that is for a meal/grain-offering is an Issaron of choice flour, and it requires a LOG of oil.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 13:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:5:4)


**רַבִּי אוֹמֵר שְׁלֹשָׁה לֻגִּין.** כַּפָּחוּת שֶׁבְּמִנְחַת נְסָכִים, עִשָּׂרוֹן לַכֶּבֶשׂ בָּלוּל בִּרְבִיעִית הַהִין שֶׁמֶן. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי:


רבי אומר שלשה לוגין – like the least that is in the meal/grain-offering is an Issaron for a lamb mixed with a quarter of Hin of oil. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi [Judah the Prince].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 13:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:5:5)


**כַּיּוֹם הַמְּרֻבֶּה.** כְּיוֹם טוֹב רִאשׁוֹן שֶׁל חַג הַסֻּכּוֹת כְּשֶׁחָל לִהְיוֹת בְּשַׁבָּת. שֶׁאוֹתוֹ הַיּוֹם מְרֻבֶּה בִּנְסָכִים לַקָּרְבָּנוֹת שֶׁל חוֹבַת הַיּוֹם מִכָּל שְׁאָר יְמוֹת הַשָּׁנָה. דַּהֲווּ שְׁלֹשָׁה עָשָׂר פָּרִים וְאַרְבָּעָה עָשָׂר כְּבָשִׂים וְאַרְבָּעָה מוּסָפִים, שְׁנֵי מוּסָף דְּשַׁבָּת וּשְׁנֵי מוּסָף דְּחַג, וְאֵילִים שְׁנַיִם וְשָׂעִיר אֶחָד. וְהַנְּסָכִים הַצְּרִיכִים לְכֻלָּם מֵאָה וְאַרְבָּעִים לֹג:


כיום מרובה – like the First Day of the Festival of the Sukkot holiday when it falls on the Sabbath. For on that day, there is an abundance of libations for the obligatory sacrifices of the day from the rest of the days of the year. For there were thirteen bulls and fourteen lambs and four additional sacrifices – two for the Additional Sacrifices of the Sabbath and two for the Additional sacrifices of the Festival [of Sukkot], and two rams and one goat (see Numbers 29:12-16). And the libations required for all of them one-hundred and forty LOG.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 13:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:6:1)


**הֲרֵי עָלַי עוֹלָה יָבִיא כֶבֶשׂ.** רַבִּי אֶלְעָזָר בֶּן עֲזַרְיָה אוֹמֵר אוֹ תּוֹר אוֹ בֶּן יוֹנָה. בְּאַתְרֵיהּ דְּתַנָּא קַמָּא לֹא הָיוּ קוֹרִים עוֹלָה סְתָם אֶלָּא לְעוֹלַת בְּהֵמָה, וּפְחוּתָה שֶׁבְּעוֹלַת בְּהֵמָה הוּא כֶּבֶשׂ, הִלְכָּךְ יָבִיא כֶּבֶשׂ. וּבְאַתְרֵיהּ דְּרַבִּי אֶלְעָזָר בֶּן עֲזַרְיָה הָיוּ קוֹרִים עוֹלָה סְתָם גַּם לְעוֹלַת הָעוֹף, הִלְכָּךְ יָבִיא תּוֹר אוֹ בֶּן יוֹנָה. וּמַר כִּי אַתְרֵיהּ וּמַר כִּי אַתְרֵיהּ:


הרי עלי עולה יביא כבש. ר' אלעזר בן עזריה אומר או תור או בן יונה – in the place of the first Tanna/teacher (i.e., anonymous teacher), he would not call a mere burnt offering anything other than a burnt offering of cattle, and the least that is of the burnt offering of cattle is a lamb, therefore he would bring a lamb. But in the place of Rabbi Eleazar ben Azariah they would call a mere burnt offering also for the burnt offering of birds, therefore, he would bring a turtle dove or a pigeon. And the [one] Master according to his place and the [other] Master according to his place.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 13:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:6:2)


**יָבִיא פַר וָעֵגֶל.** זְכָרִים וְלֹא נְקֵבוֹת. דְּעוֹלָה לֵיכָּא לְסַפּוֹקֵי אֶלָּא בִּזְכָרִים. וּמַתְנִיתִין רַבִּי הִיא דְּאָמַר לְקַמָּן הַמִּתְנַדֵּב קָטָן וְהֵבִיא גָּדוֹל לֹא יָצָא. אֲבָל רַבָּנָן פְּלִיגֵי עֲלֵיהּ וְאָמְרֵי הַמִּתְנַדֵּב קָטָן וְהֵבִיא גָּדוֹל יָצָא, דְּיֵשׁ בִּכְלָל מְרֻבֶּה מֻעָט. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


יביא פר ועגל – male [bulls and calves] but not females, for a burnt offering is not furnished other than with males. But our Mishnah is [according to] Rabbi [Judah the Prince] who states further on (see the conclusion of Tractate Menahot, Chapter 12, Mishnah 8) that a person who vows a free-will offering of a small animal and he brings a large one has not fulfilled his religious obligation. But the Rabbis dispute him and state that one who vows a free-will offering and he brought a large one has fulfilled his religious obligation, for there is within the general category of that which is numerous that which is small in size. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 13:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:6:3)


**פֵּרַשְׁתִּי.** אֶחָד מִקָּרְבְּנוֹת הַבְּהֵמָה וְאֵינִי יוֹדֵעַ מֵאֵיזֶה מֵהֶן פֵּרַשְׁתִּי. יָבִיא מִכָּל מִינֵי בְּהֵמָה הַזְּכָרִים גְּדוֹלִים וּקְטַנִּים, דְּהַיְנוּ פַּר וָעֵגֶל אַיִל וְשָׂעִיר וּגְדִי וְטָלֶה:


פירשתי – one of the sacrifices of cattle.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 13:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:6:4)


**פֵּרַשְׁתִּי.** אֶת הַמִּין:


ואיני יודע מאיזה מהן פירשתי – he should bring from all the kinds of male cattle, large and small, which are a bull, a calf, a ram, a goat, a kid and a lamb.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 13:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:6:5)


**וְאֵינִי יוֹדֵעַ מַה פֵּרַשְׁתִּי.** אִם מִין בְּהֵמָה אוֹ מִין עוֹף:


פירשתי – [I explicitly mentioned] the species. But I don’t know what I mentioned specifically. Whether it is a species of cattle or a species of bird.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 13:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:7:1)


**תּוֹדָה וּשְׁלָמִים.** תּוֹדָה אוֹ שְׁלָמִים:


תודה ושלמים – either a thanksgiving offering or a peace-offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 13:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:7:2)


**יָבִיא כֶבֶשׂ.** פָּחוּת שֶׁבַּתּוֹדָה וּשְׁלָמִים:


יביא כבש – which is the least that is of the thanksgiving and peace-offerings.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 13:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:7:3)


**יָבִיא פָר וּפָרָה.** דְּתוֹדָה וּשְׁלָמִים אִיכָּא לְסַפּוֹקֵי בִּזְכָרִים וּנְקֵבוֹת:


יביא פר ופרה – for the thanksgiving offering and/or peace-offering can be furnished by [either] males or females.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 13:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:7:4)


**וְרָחֵל.** נְקֵבָה בַּת שְׁתֵּי שָׁנִים:


ורחל – a female lamb/sheep which is two years old.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 13:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:7:5)


**גְּדִי.** בֶּן שָׁנָה מִן הָעִזִּים:


גדי – [a kid] which is one year old from the goats.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 13:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:7:6)


**שָׂעִיר.** עֵז בֶּן שְׁתֵּי שָׁנִים:


שעיר – a goat which is two years old.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 13:7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:7:7)


**טָלֶה.** כֶּבֶשׂ בֶּן שְׁנָתוֹ:


טלה – a lamb which is one year old.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 13:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:8:1)


**יָבִיא הוּא וּנְסָכָיו בְּמָנֶה.** כָּךְ נִתְפָּרֵשׁ דִּינוֹ בַּתּוֹרָה שֶׁבְּעַל פֶּה, שֶׁיְּהֵא עֵרֶךְ הַשּׁוֹר עִם נְסָכָיו מָנֶה:


יביא הוא ונכסיו במנה – such is explained its law in the Oral Tradition that the value of a bullock with its libations is a Maneh (i.e., 100 denar).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 13:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:8:2)


**עֵגֶל יָבִיא הוּא וּנְסָכָיו בְּחָמֵשׁ.** סְלָעִים:


עגל יביא הוא ונסכיו בחמש – [five] Selas (i.e., one Sela equals four Denars).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 13:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:8:3)


**אָמַר הֲרֵי עָלַי שׁוֹר בְּמָנֶה.** יְבִיאֶנּוּ בְּמָנֶה חוּץ מִנְּסָכָיו, שֶׁהֲרֵי כָּךְ קְבָעוֹ:


אמר הרי עלי שור במנה – he should bring it for a Maneh (i.e., a Maneh equals twenty-five Selas), except for its libations, for such is what they (i.e., the Rabbis) established.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 13:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:8:4)


**שׁוֹר בְּמָנֶה וְהֵבִיא שְׁנַיִם בְּמָנֶה לֹא יָצָא.** שֶׁהֲרֵי קָבַע שׁוֹר בְּמָנֶה:


שור במנה הביא שנים במנה לא יצא – for it was established that a bull is with a Maneh.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Menachot 13:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Menachot/r/13:8:5)


**קָטָן וְהֵבִיא גָּדוֹל יָצָא, רַבִּי אוֹמֵר לֹא יָצָא.** מִשּׁוּם הָכִי תָּנָא פְּלֻגְתָּא דְּרַבִּי וְרַבָּנָן הָכָא בְּסֵיפָא, לְפָרוֹשֵׁי דְּרֵישָׁא לָאו דִּבְרֵי הַכֹּל הִיא אֶלָּא רַבִּי הִיא וְלֹא רַבָּנָן. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי:
